        st.caption("Percentages and ratios cannot be summed. The chart uses an unweighted average; for financial margins, compare underlying revenue and profit instead.")
        return "Average"
    return st.selectbox("Aggregation", ["Sum", "Average"], key=key)


def _notice(meta: dict) -> None:
    excluded = meta.get("excluded_measure_rows", 0)
    if excluded:
        st.warning(f"{excluded:,} rows with missing or non-finite chart values were excluded. They were not converted to zero.")
    date_excluded = meta.get("excluded_invalid_dates", 0)
    if date_excluded:
        st.warning(f"{date_excluded:,} rows with missing or invalid dates were excluded from this chart.")


def render_chart_studio(filtered: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    with st.expander("07  Â·  Interactive chart studio", expanded=False):
        st.caption(
            "Change chart type, measures and dimensions hereâ€”without switching to the legacy Dashboard. "
            "The chart uses exactly the Executive Dashboard filters and mapped financial calculations."
        )
        try:
            measures, default_index = _metrics(filtered, calculations)
        except ValueError as exc:
            st.warning(f"Cannot open chart studio: {exc}")
            return
        if not measures:
            st.info("No numeric fields are available to chart.")
            return
        kind = st.selectbox(
            "Chart type", ["Bar", "Line", "Area", "Donut", "Scatter", "Histogram"],
            key="studio_chart_type",
        )
        categories = [col for col in filtered.columns if
                      pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(filtered[col])
                      or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(filtered[col])
                      or isinstance(filtered[col].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)]
        date_fields = [col for col in filtered.columns if
                       "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
                       or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(filtered[col])]
        left, right = st.columns(2)
        with left:
            metric = st.selectbox("Measure", measures, index=default_index, key="studio_metric")
        if kind in ("Bar", "Donut"):
            if not categories:
                st.info("Add a categorical field to use bar or donut charts.")
                return
            with right:
                category = st.selectbox(
                    "Category / X-axis", categories,
                    index=_choose_index(categories, ("Product", "Region", "City")),
                    key="studio_category",
                )
            c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
            with c1:
                agg = "Sum" if kind == "Donut" else _aggregation(metric, "studio_bar_aggregation")
            with c2:
                top_n = st.select_slider("Categories to display", [5, 10, 12, 20, 30, 50], value=12,
                                         key="studio_top_n")
            with c3:
                direction = "Highest" if kind == "Donut" else st.selectbox(
                    "Rank order", ["Highest", "Lowest"], key="studio_rank_order",
                )
            try:
                chart, meta = category_chart_data(
                    filtered, calculations, category, metric, agg, top_n, direction,
                    with_remainder=(kind == "Donut"),
                )
            except ValueError as exc:
                st.warning(str(exc))
                return
            _notice(meta)
            if chart.empty:
                st.info("No valid chart values are available.")
                return
            if kind == "Donut":
                if float(chart["Value"].sum()) <= 0:
                    st.info("A donut chart requires a positive total. Use a bar chart instead.")
                    return
                fig = px.pie(chart, names="Category", values="Value", hole=0.48,
                             color_discrete_sequence=FINANCE_ACCENT,
                             title=f"{metric} by {category}")
            else:
                fig = px.bar(chart, x="Category", y="Value", title=f"{agg} Â· {metric}",
                             color_discrete_sequence=FINANCE_ACCENT)
            st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig), width="stretch")
            st.caption(
                f"{meta['included_rows']:,} valid records, {meta['all_groups']:,} categories. "
                + ("Remaining categories are grouped into Other to preserve the sum." if kind == "Donut" and meta['truncated_groups'] else
                   f"Chart displays {meta['shown_groups']:,} categories; {meta['truncated_groups']:,} not shown. "
                   "Exports contain the displayed result only.")
            )
            _download(chart, "python_bi_custom_category_chart.csv", "studio_cat_export")

        elif kind in ("Line", "Area"):
            if not date_fields:
                st.info("Select a dataset containing a date/timestamp column for time-series charts.")
                return
            with right:
                date_col = st.selectbox("Date / X-axis", date_fields,
                                        index=_choose_index(date_fields, ("Date", "Order Date")),
                                        key="studio_date_column")
            a, b = st.columns(2)
            with a:
                cadence = st.selectbox("Period", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"],
                                        key="studio_time_period")
            with b:
                agg = _aggregation(metric, "studio_time_aggregation")
            try:
                chart, meta = time_chart_data(filtered, calculations, date_col, metric, cadence, agg)
            except ValueError as exc:
                st.warning(str(exc))
                return
            _notice(meta)
            if chart.empty:
                st.info("No valid dated observations were found.")
                return
            fig = (px.line(chart, x="Period", y="Value", markers=True,
                           title=f"{metric} by {cadence.lower()} period", color_discrete_sequence=FINANCE_ACCENT)
                   if kind == "Line" else
                   px.area(chart, x="Period", y="Value", title=f"{metric} by {cadence.lower()} period",
                           color_discrete_sequence=FINANCE_ACCENT))
            st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig), width="stretch")
            st.caption(f"Showing {meta['periods']:,} periods from {meta['included_rows']:,} valid rows. Empty calendar periods are not invented as zero.")
            _download(chart, "python_bi_custom_time_chart.csv", "studio_time_export")

        elif kind == "Scatter":
            with right:
                y_metric = st.selectbox("Y-axis measure", measures,
                                        index=min(default_index + 1, len(measures) - 1),
                                        key="studio_scatter_y")
            size = st.select_slider("Maximum plotted points", [100, 500, 1_000, 2_500, 5_000],
                                    value=1_000, key="studio_scatter_limit")
            try:
                chart, meta = scatter_chart_data(filtered, calculations, metric, y_metric, size)
            except ValueError as exc:
                st.warning(str(exc))
                return
            if chart.empty:
                st.info("No valid X/Y pairs were found.")
                return
            fig = px.scatter(chart, x="X", y="Y", title=f"{metric} versus {y_metric}",
                             color_discrete_sequence=FINANCE_ACCENT)
            st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig), width="stretch")
            st.caption(
                f"{meta['charted_rows']:,} points displayed from {meta['valid_rows']:,} complete numeric pairs. "
                + ("This is a reproducible sample, not every source record." if meta['is_sample'] else "Every valid pair is shown.")
            )
            if meta['excluded_rows']:
                st.warning(f"{meta['excluded_rows']:,} rows excluded due to missing/non-finite X or Y values.")
            _download(chart, "python_bi_custom_scatter_chart.csv", "studio_scatter_export")

        else:  # Histogram
            with right:
                bins = st.slider("Number of histogram bins", min_value=5, max_value=80,
                                 value=20, step=5, key="studio_histogram_bins")
            try:
                chart, meta = histogram_chart_data(filtered, calculations, metric, bins)
            except ValueError as exc:
                st.warning(str(exc))
                return
            _notice(meta)
            if chart.empty:
                st.info("No valid numeric values were found.")
                return
            fig = px.bar(chart, x="Bin midpoint", y="Records", title=f"Distribution of {metric}",
                         color_discrete_sequence=FINANCE_ACCENT)
            st.plotly_chart(_finish(fig), width="stretch")
            st.caption(f"Histogram bin counts cover all {meta['included_rows']:,} valid records; individual records are not rendered.")
            _download(chart, "python_bi_custom_histogram.csv", "studio_histogram_export")


def _download(frame: pd.DataFrame, file_name: str, key: str) -> None:
    st.download_button("Download this chart's data", data=excel_safe_csv(frame),
                       file_name=file_name, mime="text/csv", key=key)


def render_performance_lab(source: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    with st.expander("08  Â·  Performance lab (bounded sample)", expanded=False):
        st.caption(
            "Run an optional local grouping diagnostic on a reproducible sample (maximum 50,000 rows). "
            "This is NOT a benchmark of 500,000-record uploads, full-source charts, or the complete website."
        )
        categories = [col for col in source.columns if
                      pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(source[col])
                      or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(source[col])
                      or isinstance(source[col].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)]
        numeric = source.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
        if not categories or not numeric:
            st.info("A categorical field and a numeric field are required for this diagnostic.")
            return
        a, b, c = st.columns(3)
        with a:
            category = st.selectbox("Group by", categories,
                                     index=_choose_index(categories, ("Product", "Region", "City")),
                                     key="studio_bench_category")
        with b:
            metric = st.selectbox("Numeric value", numeric,
                                  index=_choose_index(numeric, ("Sales", "Revenue")),
                                  key="studio_bench_metric")
        with c:
            sample_size = st.select_slider("Max sampled records", [100, 1_000, 5_000, 10_000, 25_000, 50_000],
                                           value=10_000, key="studio_bench_sample")
        if st.button("Run sample grouping diagnostic", key="studio_run_benchmark"):
            try:
                result, leaders = benchmark_grouping(source, category, metric, sample_size)
            except ValueError as exc:
                st.warning(str(exc))
                return
            b1, b2, b3 = st.columns(3)
            b1.metric("Sample rows", f"{result['sample_rows']:,}")
            b2.metric("Elapsed (ms)", f"{result['elapsed_ms']:,.2f}")
            b3.metric("Groups", f"{result['group_count']:,}")
            if result["excluded_nonnumeric"]:
                st.warning(f"{result['excluded_nonnumeric']:,} invalid numeric rows excluded from this diagnostic.")
            st.caption(
                f"Sample memory: {result['sample_memory_mib']:.3f} MiB. "
                "Time includes the sampled two-column preparation and grouping. "
                "Your hardware, data types and number of categories affect this measurement."
            )
            st.dataframe(leaders, width="stretch", hide_index=True)


========== FILE: commerce_engine.py ==========

"""Python BI Studio â€” auditable commercial metrics and safe financial-field mapping."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

NONE = "â€” Not provided â€”"


def _numeric(data: pd.DataFrame, column: str | None) -> pd.Series:
    """Never silently turn malformed or missing financial numbers into zero."""
    if not column or column == NONE:
        return pd.Series(0.0, index=data.index, dtype="float64")
    values = pd.to_numeric(data[column], errors="coerce")
    invalid = values.isna() | ~values.map(lambda x: bool(pd.notna(x)) and abs(x) != float('inf'))
    if invalid.any():
        raise ValueError(
            f"Financial field '{column}' has {int(invalid.sum()):,} missing or invalid "
            "values. Review these records in Prepare & Validate before calculating."
        )
    return values.astype("float64")


def calculate_commercial_metrics(
    data: pd.DataFrame,
    revenue: str,
    cost: str | None = None,
    discount: str | None = None,
    returns: str | None = None,
    operating_expenses: str | None = None,
    taxes: str | None = None,
    invested_capital: str | None = None,
    complete_costs: bool = False,
    capital_is_row_additive: bool = False,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    """Returns row-level audit and totals. All monetary fields must be same currency.

    Gross revenue must be before separately mapped discounts/returns. Mapped cost
    fields cannot overlap. ROI only uses capital mapped per row if the user explicitly
    confirms those capital amounts can be summed across transactions.
    """
    if revenue not in data.columns:
        raise ValueError("Choose a valid revenue column.")
    if not data.columns.is_unique:
        raise ValueError("Duplicate source column names prevent reliable financial mapping.")
    if (not cost or cost == NONE) and any(field and field != NONE for field in (operating_expenses, taxes)):
        raise ValueError("Map a cost-of-sales column before mapping operating expenses or taxes. "
                         "Those expenses must not be silently excluded from profit calculations.")
    columns = [v for v in [revenue, cost, discount, returns, operating_expenses, taxes, invested_capital]
               if v and v != NONE]
    if len(columns) != len(set(columns)):
        raise ValueError("One column is mapped to multiple financial roles. Choose distinct fields.")
    output = pd.DataFrame(index=data.index)
    output["Gross Revenue"] = _numeric(data, revenue)
    output["Discounts"] = _numeric(data, discount)
    output["Returns"] = _numeric(data, returns)
    output["Net Revenue"] = output["Gross Revenue"] - output["Discounts"] - output["Returns"]
    has_cost = bool(cost and cost != NONE)
    if has_cost:
        output["Mapped Costs"] = (
            _numeric(data, cost) + _numeric(data, operating_expenses) + _numeric(data, taxes)
        )
        output["Profit After Mapped Costs"] = output["Net Revenue"] - output["Mapped Costs"]
        output["Loss After Mapped Costs"] = (-output["Profit After Mapped Costs"]).clip(lower=0)
    totals = {
        "records": len(data),
        "gross_revenue": float(output["Gross Revenue"].sum()),
        "total_discounts": float(output["Discounts"].sum()),
        "total_returns": float(output["Returns"].sum()),
        "net_revenue": float(output["Net Revenue"].sum()),
    }
    if has_cost:
        profit = float(output["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum())
        totals.update({
            "mapped_costs": float(output["Mapped Costs"].sum()),
            "profit_after_mapped_costs": profit,
            "loss_after_mapped_costs": float(output["Loss After Mapped Costs"].sum()),
            "net_business_loss": max(-profit, 0.0),
            "profit_margin_pct": (
                profit / totals["net_revenue"] * 100 if totals["net_revenue"] else None
            ),
        })
        if complete_costs:
            totals["net_profit"] = profit
            if invested_capital and invested_capital != NONE and capital_is_row_additive:
                invested = float(_numeric(data, invested_capital).sum())
                totals["invested_capital"] = invested
                totals["roi_pct"] = profit / invested * 100 if invested > 0 else None
    return output, totals


def _field_selector(label: str, fields: list[str], key: str, keywords: list[str], required=False) -> str:
    options = fields if required else [NONE] + fields
    default = 0
    for i, field in enumerate(options):
        if field == NONE:
            continue
        normalized = field.lower().replace("_", " ")
        if any(normalized == word for word in keywords):
            default = i
            break
    return st.selectbox(label, options, index=default, key=key)


def render_commercial_intelligence(data: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    st.subheader("Commercial Intelligence")
    st.caption("Map source columns, examine calculation rules, then export an auditable result.")
    if data.empty:
        st.warning("The active dataset is empty.")
        return
    fields = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
    if not fields:
        st.warning("No numeric source columns detected. Convert numeric-looking text in Prepare & Validate first.")
        return

    with st.expander("Financial source mapping", expanded=True):
        left, right = st.columns(2)
        with left:
            revenue = _field_selector("Revenue (required)", fields, "ci_revenue", ["sales", "revenue", "gross revenue"], True)
            cost = _field_selector("Cost of sales", fields, "ci_cost", ["total cost", "cost", "cogs"])
            discount = _field_selector("Discounts (optional)", fields, "ci_discount", ["discounts", "discount amount"])
            returns = _field_selector("Returns / refunds (optional)", fields, "ci_returns", ["returns", "refunds"])
        with right:
            operating = _field_selector("Operating expenses (optional)", fields, "ci_opex", ["operating expenses", "opex"])
            taxes = _field_selector("Taxes (optional)", fields, "ci_taxes", ["taxes", "tax expense"])
            capital = _field_selector("Invested capital (optional)", fields, "ci_capital", ["invested capital", "capital invested"])
        complete = st.checkbox(
            "I confirm mapped costs include all relevant expenses and taxes required to report net profit",
            key="ci_complete_costs"
        )
        capital_additive = st.checkbox(
            "I confirm invested capital is an independent, additive amount in each row (not a repeated account balance)",
            key="ci_capital_additive"
        ) if capital != NONE else False
        st.info("Use revenue BEFORE mapped discounts/returns. Do not map overlapping columns, or add deductions already included in Sales.")

    try:
        calculated, m = calculate_commercial_metrics(
            data, revenue, cost, discount, returns, operating, taxes, capital,
            complete, capital_additive
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
        st.error(str(exc))
        return

    left, right = st.columns(2)
    left.metric("Gross Revenue", f"{m['gross_revenue']:,.2f}")
    right.metric("Net Revenue", f"{m['net_revenue']:,.2f}")
    if "mapped_costs" not in m:
        st.warning("Choose a cost-of-sales column to calculate profit, loss and margin.")
    else:
        c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
        c1.metric("Mapped Costs", f"{m['mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        c2.metric("Profit After Mapped Costs", f"{m['profit_after_mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        c3.metric("Net Business Loss", f"{m['net_business_loss']:,.2f}")
        st.caption(f"Sum of losses on individual rows: {m['loss_after_mapped_costs']:,.2f}. This differs from net business loss.")
        margin = m["profit_margin_pct"]
        st.metric("Profit Margin After Mapped Costs", f"{margin:.2f}%" if margin is not None else "Not defined")
    if "net_profit" in m:
        col_a, col_b = st.columns(2)
        col_a.metric("Net Profit (confirmed)", f"{m['net_profit']:,.2f}")
        roi = m.get("roi_pct")
        col_b.metric("ROI (confirmed investment basis)", f"{roi:.2f}%" if roi is not None else "Not available")
    else:
        st.caption("Net Profit and ROI remain unavailable until the required assumptions are confirmed.")
    with st.expander("Calculation notes and interpretation"):
        st.markdown("""
- Net revenue = gross revenue âˆ’ separately mapped discounts âˆ’ returns.
- Profit after mapped costs = net revenue âˆ’ cost of sales âˆ’ mapped operating expenses âˆ’ mapped taxes.
- Net business loss = max(negative aggregate profit, 0). Row-level loss totals are reported separately.
- Net profit is shown only when users confirm all relevant costs and taxes are included.
- ROI uses confirmed net profit / sum of independently additive invested-capital amounts. It is **not** available if capital is a repeated balance.
- All monetary amounts must use the same currency. This tool does not infer missing amounts or convert currencies.
- Null, malformed or infinite values in mapped financial fields block the calculation until fixed.
        """)
    with st.expander("Audit individual calculations and export"):
        st.dataframe(calculated.head(200), width="stretch")
        st.download_button(
            "Download calculated metrics", calculated.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8"),
            file_name="python_bi_commercial_metrics.csv", mime="text/csv", key="ci_export"
        )

========== FILE: data_readiness.py ==========

"""Dataset scale and readiness checks with explicitly labelled sampling.

A diagnostic tool, not an assertion of 500,000-row throughput or an exhaustive
validation unless a user chooses a complete missing-value scan.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd


def dataset_profile(
    data: pd.DataFrame,
    *,
    sample_limit: int = 10_000,
    exact_missing_scan: bool = False,
) -> tuple[dict, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Return (summary, per-field profile, sampled preview), without mutation.

    Unique and duplicate values are always SAMPLE counts. Sampling is seeded.
    Full missing-value counts are calculated only when exact_missing_scan=True.
    """
    if sample_limit < 1 or sample_limit > 50_000:
        raise ValueError("Sample limit must be between 1 and 50,000 rows.")
    if not data.columns.is_unique:
        raise ValueError("The dataset contains duplicate column names; rename them before profiling.")
    total_rows, total_columns = data.shape
    count = min(sample_limit, total_rows)
    sample = data.sample(n=count, random_state=26).reset_index(drop=True) if count else data.head(0).copy()
    source_shallow_mb = float(data.memory_usage(index=True, deep=False).sum() / (1024 * 1024))
    sample_deep_mb = float(sample.memory_usage(index=True, deep=True).sum() / (1024 * 1024))
    approx_full_deep_mb = (
        float(sample_deep_mb * (total_rows / count)) if count else 0.0
    )
    sample_missing_cells = int(sample.isna().sum().sum())
    sample_duplicates = int(sample.duplicated().sum()) if total_columns else 0
    full_missing = data.isna().sum() if exact_missing_scan else None
    fields = []
    for column in data.columns:
        series = sample[column]
        fields.append({
            "Column": str(column),
            "Source dtype": str(data[column].dtype),
            "Missing in sample": int(series.isna().sum()),
            "Sample missing %": round(float(series.isna().mean() * 100), 2) if count else None,
            "Unique in sample": int(series.nunique(dropna=True)),
            **({"Exact missing in source": int(full_missing[column])} if full_missing is not None else {}),
        })
    per_column = pd.DataFrame(fields, columns=[
        "Column", "Source dtype", "Missing in sample", "Sample missing %", "Unique in sample",
        *(["Exact missing in source"] if exact_missing_scan else []),
    ])
    summary = {
        "source_rows": total_rows,
        "source_columns": total_columns,
        "sample_rows": count,
        "sample_missing_cells": sample_missing_cells,
        "sample_duplicate_rows": sample_duplicates,
        "source_shallow_memory_mb": source_shallow_mb,
        "estimated_deep_memory_mb": approx_full_deep_mb,
        "exact_missing_scan": exact_missing_scan,
        "exact_missing_cells": int(full_missing.sum()) if full_missing is not None else None,
    }
    return summary, per_column, sample


========== FILE: dependency_snapshot.py ==========

"""Opt-in, exact installed-version dependency snapshot for VS Code.

Never overwrites requirements.txt automatically. Pinning is local-machine evidence,
not a guarantee of Windows-to-cloud compatibility.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import importlib.metadata
from pathlib import Path
from deployment_audit import DEPENDENCIES


def dependency_lines() -> list[str]:
    result = []
    for name in DEPENDENCIES:
        result.append(f"{name}=={importlib.metadata.version(name)}")
    return result


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--output", default="requirements.release-candidate.txt")
    p.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true")
    args = p.parse_args(argv)
    target = Path(args.output)
    if target.exists() and not args.overwrite:
        print("No changes: candidate file already exists. Use --overwrite explicitly to replace it.")
        return 2
    try:
        lines = dependency_lines()
    except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError as e:
        print("A direct dependency is missing:", e)
        return 1
    target.write_text("# Local Python BI Studio dependency snapshot. Review before cloud use.\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print("Saved candidate requirements:", target.name)
    return 0

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


========== FILE: deployment_audit.py ==========

"""Conservative read-only deployment preflight for a *single-user local* BI app.

An automated audit must never claim the current shared filesystem Dataset
Library is safe for anonymous public or multi-tenant operation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass
import importlib.metadata
import tomllib

DEPENDENCIES = ("streamlit", "pandas", "numpy", "plotly", "openpyxl")
SENSITIVE_EXCLUSIONS = ("stored_datasets/", "Data/", ".venv/", "__pycache__/", ".streamlit/secrets.toml", ".env", "assurance_results/", "release_results/", "benchmark_artifacts/")

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Check:
    id: str
    status: str
    area: str
    finding: str

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        return {"id": self.id, "status": self.status, "area": self.area, "finding": self.finding}


def check_deployment(root: Path) -> list[dict]:
    """Never includes file contents, credentials or absolute source paths in results."""
    root = Path(root)
    findings = [
        Check("AUTH", "BLOCKED", "Access control", "No verified authentication and user-authorisation boundary for business files."),
        Check("USER-ISOLATION", "BLOCKED", "Data isolation", "stored_datasets and active_dataset.json are shared filesystem state; per-user storage is not implemented."),
        Check("PERSISTENCE", "BLOCKED", "Durability", "Local server files are not a managed, durable multi-user dataset store."),
        Check("EXTERNAL-SECURITY", "MANUAL", "Security", "Threat modelling, penetration testing, upload quotas and operating controls require an independent review."),
        Check("BROWSER-TEST", "MANUAL", "Acceptance", "Browser-level multi-schema, 500k workflow and regression tests require documented human acceptance."),
        Check("DEPLOYMENT", "MANUAL", "Release", "Cloud environment, routing, access and recovery have not been commissioned or verified."),
    ]
    for name in DEPENDENCIES:
        try:
            version = importlib.metadata.version(name)
            findings.append(Check(f"DEP:{name}", "PASS", "Dependencies", f"{name} installed ({version})."))
        except importlib.metadata.PackageNotFoundError:
            findings.append(Check(f"DEP:{name}", "REVIEW", "Dependencies", f"{name} not installed in the Python environment running this audit."))

    config = root / ".streamlit" / "config.toml"
    try:
        theme = tomllib.loads(config.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("theme", {})
        if theme.get("base") == "light":
            findings.append(Check("LIGHT-THEME", "PASS", "Appearance", "Project config retains the requested light base theme."))
        else:
            findings.append(Check("LIGHT-THEME", "REVIEW", "Appearance", "Check the light-theme base setting."))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        findings.append(Check("LIGHT-THEME", "REVIEW", "Appearance", "Configuration file is missing or unreadable."))

    ignore_path = root / ".gitignore"
    if not ignore_path.exists():
        findings.append(Check("PRIVATE-GIT", "REVIEW", "Source control", "No .gitignore found. Keep private datasets and secrets out of Git."))
    else:
        contents = ignore_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines()
        rules = {line.strip() for line in contents if line.strip() and not line.lstrip().startswith("#")}
        missing = [rule for rule in SENSITIVE_EXCLUSIONS if rule not in rules]
        if not missing:
            findings.append(Check("PRIVATE-GIT", "PASS", "Source control", "Recommended privacy patterns appear in .gitignore (tracking status not established)."))
        else:
            findings.append(Check("PRIVATE-GIT", "REVIEW", "Source control", "Missing recommended ignore patterns: " + ", ".join(missing)))
    findings.append(Check("REPOSITORY-REVIEW", "MANUAL", "Source control", "Check git tracked files, history, workbook contents and any remote repository before publishing."))
    return [f.as_dict() for f in findings]


def release_gate(checks: list[dict]) -> str:
    return "BLOCKED_FOR_PUBLIC_DEPLOYMENT" if any(x["status"] == "BLOCKED" for x in checks) else "MANUAL_APPROVAL_REQUIRED"


def public_blockers(checks: list[dict]) -> int:
    return sum(x["status"] == "BLOCKED" for x in checks)


========== FILE: design_system.py ==========

"""Reusable, escaped navigation mastheads for Python BI Studio.

Works with the existing Streamlit sidebar and does not mutate session state.
The human-facing branding is original; it is not an imitation of another firm.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from html import escape
from typing import Any

PAGES = {
    "Home": ("PLATFORM OVERVIEW", "Python BI Studio", "Analytics, assurance and evidence-led decisions in one workspace."),
    "Data Workspace": ("DATA OPERATIONS", "Data Workspace", "Explore, prepare and analyse the same active source with a controlled workflow."),
    "Dashboard": ("EXECUTIVE ANALYTICS", "Executive Dashboard", "Decision-ready charts, audited financial measures and reporting."),
    "Investment Intelligence": ("INVESTMENT RESEARCH", "Investment Intelligence", "Business cash-flow scenarios and historical market risk â€” separate evidence streams."),
}


def masthead_html(page: str, dataset_name: str, row_count: int | None) -> str:
    """Render a small, safe masthead. No raw records or client code enter the DOM."""
    kicker, title, description = PAGES.get(page, ("PLATFORM", "Python BI Studio", "Workspace"))
    safe_name = escape(str(dataset_name)[:120], quote=True)
    scope = "NO ACTIVE DATASET" if row_count is None else f"ACTIVE DATASET Â· {row_count:,} RECORDS"
    return (
        '<div class="bi-page-masthead" role="region" aria-label="Workspace context">'
        f'<div class="bi-page-kicker">{escape(kicker)}</div>'
        '<div class="bi-page-masthead-inner">'
        '<div class="bi-page-ident">'
        f'<h1 class="bi-page-title">{escape(title)}</h1>'
        f'<p class="bi-page-description">{escape(description)}</p>'
        '</div>'
        '<div class="bi-dataset-pill" aria-label="Active dataset">'
        '<span class="bi-live-dot" aria-hidden="true"></span>'
        f'<div><span class="bi-dataset-state">{scope}</span>'
        f'<span class="bi-dataset-name">{safe_name}</span></div>'
        '</div></div></div>'
    )


def render_page_masthead(page: str, dataset_name: str, active_data: Any) -> None:
    import streamlit as st
    rows = len(active_data) if hasattr(active_data, "columns") and hasattr(active_data, "__len__") else None
    st.markdown(masthead_html(page, dataset_name, rows), unsafe_allow_html=True)


========== FILE: executive_addons.py ==========

"""Concise, optional Executive Dashboard comparison and readiness panels."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv
from data_readiness import dataset_profile
from period_comparison import available_periods, compare_periods


def _field_options(data: pd.DataFrame) -> list[str]:
    return [col for col in data.columns if
            "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
            or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])]


def render_comparison_panel(filtered: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    with st.expander("05  Â·  Compare financial periods", expanded=False):
        st.caption(
            "Compare two calendar months, quarters or years within the same Dashboard filters. "
            "Dates excluded from the comparison do not affect the Dashboard's main KPIs."
        )
        dates = _field_options(filtered)
        if not dates:
            st.info("This dataset has no identified date or timestamp field.")
            return
        col1, col2 = st.columns(2)
        with col1:
            date_col = st.selectbox("Date field", dates,
                                    index=dates.index("Date") if "Date" in dates else 0,
                                    key="cmp_date")
        with col2:
            cadence = st.selectbox("Calendar periods", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"],
                                    key="cmp_cadence")
        try:
            periods, invalid_count = available_periods(filtered, date_col, cadence)
            if invalid_count:
                st.warning(f"{invalid_count:,} records have invalid/missing dates; only the comparison excludes them.")
            if len(periods) < 2:
                st.info("Two different observed periods are required. The current dataset or filters include fewer than two.")
                return
            start, end = st.columns(2)
            with start:
                earlier = st.selectbox("Earlier period", periods, index=len(periods) - 2,
                                       key=f"cmp_earlier_{cadence}")
            with end:
                later = st.selectbox("Later period", periods, index=len(periods) - 1,
                                     key=f"cmp_later_{cadence}")
            options = ["No category breakdown"] + [col for col in filtered.columns
                if pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(filtered[col])
                or pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(filtered[col])
                or isinstance(filtered[col].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)]
            cat = st.selectbox("Optional category breakdown", options,
                               index=options.index("Product") if "Product" in options else 0,
                               key="cmp_category")
            summary, breakdown, audit = compare_periods(
                filtered, calculations, date_col, cadence, earlier, later,
                None if cat == "No category breakdown" else cat,
            )
            if not audit["periods_are_adjacent"]:
                st.warning("Periods are not adjacent: the displayed change is between the two selected periods, not a consecutive-period growth rate.")
            st.caption(
                f"Compared {audit['earlier_rows']:,} earlier and {audit['later_rows']:,} later records; "
                f"{audit['excluded_other_period_rows']:,} valid-dated records are from other periods."
            )
            st.dataframe(summary, width="stretch", hide_index=True)
            st.download_button("Export period comparison", excel_safe_csv(summary),
                               file_name="python_bi_period_comparison.csv", mime="text/csv",
                               key="cmp_summary_export")
            if not breakdown.empty:
                st.markdown("#### Category movements")
                st.dataframe(breakdown.head(100), width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                top = breakdown.head(12)
                if not top.empty:
                    chart = top.melt(id_vars="Category", value_vars=["Earlier Revenue", "Later Revenue"],
                                     var_name="Period", value_name="Revenue")
                    st.plotly_chart(px.bar(chart, x="Category", y="Revenue", color="Period",
                                           barmode="group", title="Net revenue by category and period"),
                                    width="stretch")
                st.download_button("Export full category comparison", excel_safe_csv(breakdown),
                                   file_name="python_bi_period_category_comparison.csv", mime="text/csv",
                                   key="cmp_category_export")
                st.caption("Full category report is exported even when only the first 100 groups appear in the table.")
        except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as error:
            st.warning(f"Unable to compare the selected periods: {error}")


def render_readiness_panel(source: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    with st.expander("06  Â·  Dataset scale and readiness", expanded=False):
        st.caption(
            "This diagnostic describes the entire active source dataset, not just Dashboard-filtered records. "
            "Profiles are sampled for responsiveness; an optional exact missing-value scan is available."
        )
        sample_limit = st.select_slider("Sample rows for diagnostics", options=[100, 500, 1_000, 5_000, 10_000, 25_000],
                                        value=5_000, key="readiness_sample_limit")
        exact_scan = st.checkbox("Calculate exact missing counts for every source column",
                                 value=(len(source) <= 5_000), key="readiness_exact_missing")
        try:
            info, table, preview = dataset_profile(source, sample_limit=sample_limit,
                                                   exact_missing_scan=exact_scan)
        except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
            st.warning(f"Unable to profile this dataset: {error}")
            return
        tiles = st.columns(4)
        tiles[0].metric("Source records", f"{info['source_rows']:,}")
        tiles[1].metric("Source columns", f"{info['source_columns']:,}")
        tiles[2].metric("Sampled records", f"{info['sample_rows']:,}")
        tiles[3].metric("Missing cells" if exact_scan else "Missing in sample",
                        f"{info['exact_missing_cells'] if exact_scan else info['sample_missing_cells']:,}")
        st.caption(
            f"Sample duplicate rows: {info['sample_duplicate_rows']:,} (not a full-source count). "
            f"Shallow DataFrame storage: {info['source_shallow_memory_mb']:.2f} MiB. "
            f"Approximate total memory from sample: {info['estimated_deep_memory_mb']:.2f} MiB "
            "(rough estimate, not a benchmark)."
        )
        st.dataframe(table, width="stretch", hide_index=True)
        st.download_button("Export data-readiness profile", excel_safe_csv(table),
                           file_name="python_bi_data_readiness.csv", mime="text/csv",
                           key="readiness_export")
        st.dataframe(preview.head(50), width="stretch")
        st.caption(
            "The preview shows up to 50 reproducibly sampled rows. The application still loads the complete dataset "
            "into memory; these controls do not guarantee performance on 500,000+ rows."
        )


========== FILE: executive_dashboard.py ==========

"""Executive overview: all metrics, trends and exports use the SAME filtered rows."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from business_metrics import apply_dashboard_filters, revenue_by_period
from commerce_engine import NONE, calculate_commercial_metrics
from audit_exports import build_financial_export, excel_safe_csv
from performance_insights import category_profitability, loss_making_rows
from executive_addons import render_comparison_panel, render_readiness_panel
from chart_builder import render_chart_studio, render_performance_lab
from executive_report import render_executive_report
from portfolio_panel import render_portfolio_panel


def _default(options: list[str], preferred: list[str], fallback: int = 0) -> int:
    lower = {name.casefold(): i for i, name in enumerate(options)}
    for name in preferred:
        if name.casefold() in lower:
            return lower[name.casefold()]
    return fallback


def render_executive_dashboard(data: pd.DataFrame, dataset_name: str) -> None:
    st.header("Executive Performance Dashboard")
    st.caption(f"Dataset: {dataset_name}  â€¢  All figures on this page reflect the selected filters.")
    st.caption("Want to change the graph type? Expand Section 07 Â· Interactive chart studio below; choose bar, line, area, donut, scatter or histogram.")
    if data.empty:
        st.warning("The active dataset has no records.")
        return

    numeric = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
    if not numeric:
        st.warning("No numeric financial fields are available. Prepare the source data first.")
        return

    text_fields = data.select_dtypes(include=["object", "string", "category"]).columns.tolist()
    category_options = ["No category filter"] + text_fields
    date_options = ["No date filter"] + [
        col for col in data.columns
        if "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
        or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])
    ]

    st.markdown("### Business performance")
    with st.expander("01  Â·  Refine dataset", expanded=True):
        first, second = st.columns(2)
        with first:
            category = st.selectbox(
                "Category", category_options,
                index=_default(category_options, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                key="ex_category",
            )
            selected = None
            category_values = []
            if category != "No category filter":
                category_values = sorted(data[category].dropna().astype(str).unique().tolist())
                if len(category_values) > 250:
                    st.info("This field has over 250 values; use another category for an interactive selector.")
                    category = "No category filter"
                else:
                    selected = st.multiselect(
                        "Values to include", category_values, default=category_values,
                        key=f"ex_values_{category}",
                    )
        with second:
            date_col = st.selectbox(
                "Date field", date_options,
                index=_default(date_options, ["Date", "Order Date", "Transaction Date"]),
                key="ex_date_field",
            )
            date_bounds = None
            if date_col != "No date filter":
                apply_date_range = st.checkbox(
                    "Restrict records to a date range", value=False, key="ex_use_date_filter"
                )
                parsed = pd.to_datetime(data[date_col], errors="coerce", format="mixed").dropna()
                if not parsed.empty and apply_date_range:
                    date_bounds = st.date_input(
                        "Date range", value=(parsed.min().date(), parsed.max().date()),
                        min_value=parsed.min().date(), max_value=parsed.max().date(),
                        key=f"ex_date_bounds_{date_col}",
                    )
                elif parsed.empty:
                    st.warning("No valid dates are available in the selected field.")
        numeric_filter = st.selectbox(
            "Additional numeric filter", ["No numeric filter"] + numeric,
            key="ex_number_field",
        )
        value_bounds = None
        if numeric_filter != "No numeric filter":
            series = pd.to_numeric(data[numeric_filter], errors="coerce").dropna()
            series = series[series.map(lambda v: bool(pd.notna(v)) and abs(v) != float("inf"))]
            if not series.empty:
                lower_default, upper_default = float(series.min()), float(series.max())
                n1, n2 = st.columns(2)
                with n1:
                    low = st.number_input("Minimum", value=lower_default, key=f"ex_min_{numeric_filter}")
                with n2:
                    high = st.number_input("Maximum", value=upper_default, key=f"ex_max_{numeric_filter}")
                value_bounds = (low, high)

    # A partially selected date range must not silently apply only one endpoint.
    start = end = None
    if date_col != "No date filter" and isinstance(date_bounds, (list, tuple)):
        if len(date_bounds) == 2:
            start, end = date_bounds
        else:
            st.info("Choose both endpoints to apply the date filter.")
    if value_bounds is not None and value_bounds[0] > value_bounds[1]:
        st.error("Minimum must be less than or equal to maximum.")
        return

    try:
        filtered = apply_dashboard_filters(
            data,
            # All selected means unrestricted, including source rows with missing categories.
            category_column=category if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            categories=selected if selected is not None and len(selected) < len(category_values) else None,
            date_column=date_col if start is not None else None,
            start_date=start, end_date=end,
            numeric_column=numeric_filter if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_min=value_bounds[0] if value_bounds is not None else None,
            numeric_max=value_bounds[1] if value_bounds is not None else None,
        ).reset_index(drop=True)
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
        st.error(f"The filter is invalid: {error}")
        return

    st.caption(f"{len(filtered):,} of {len(data):,} records matched the filters.")
    if filtered.empty:
        st.warning("No records match. Adjust the filters to continue.")
        return

    with st.expander("02  Â·  Confirm financial field mapping", expanded=True):
        a, b = st.columns(2)
        with a:
            revenue_col = st.selectbox(
                "Gross revenue field", numeric,
                index=_default(numeric, ["Sales", "Gross Revenue", "Revenue"]),
                key="ex_revenue",
            )
            cost_col = st.selectbox(
                "Cost of sales field (optional)", [NONE] + numeric,
                index=_default([NONE] + numeric, ["Total Cost", "COGS", "Cost"]),
                key="ex_cost",
            )
        with b:
            discount_col = st.selectbox("Discounts (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_discount")
            returns_col = st.selectbox("Returns (optional)", [NONE] + numeric, key="ex_returns")
        st.caption(
            "Gross revenue must be before separately mapped discounts/returns. "
            "Do not map a field twice or treat Profit as a cost. All amounts must use one currency."
        )

    try:
        calculations, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(
            filtered, revenue_col, cost_col, discount_col, returns_col
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as error:
        st.error(f"Financial calculation blocked: {error}")
        return

    headline = st.columns(4)
    headline[0].metric("Matching Records", f"{len(filtered):,}")
    headline[1].metric("Gross Revenue", f"{totals['gross_revenue']:,.2f}")
    headline[2].metric("Net Revenue", f"{totals['net_revenue']:,.2f}")
    if "profit_after_mapped_costs" in totals:
        headline[3].metric("Profit after mapped costs", f"{totals['profit_after_mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub = st.columns(3)
        sub[0].metric("Mapped costs", f"{totals['mapped_costs']:,.2f}")
        sub[1].metric("Net business loss", f"{totals['net_business_loss']:,.2f}")
        margin = totals["profit_margin_pct"]
        sub[2].metric("Profit margin", f"{margin:.2f}%" if margin is not None else "Not defined")
        st.caption("Profit shown here is after mapped costs; it is not labelled net profit unless all relevant costs are established.")
    else:
        headline[3].metric("Profit", "Not available")
        st.info("Map a cost-of-sales field to enable profit, loss and margin metrics.")

    chart_col, chart_col2 = st.columns(2)
    with chart_col:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue by category")
        if category != "No category filter" or text_fields:
            dimension = category if category != "No category filter" else text_fields[0]
            chart_frame = pd.DataFrame({
                "Category": filtered[dimension].astype("string").fillna("(Missing)").to_numpy(),
                "Net Revenue": calculations["Net Revenue"].to_numpy(),
            })
            category_summary = (
                chart_frame.groupby("Category", as_index=False, dropna=False)["Net Revenue"]
                .sum().sort_values("Net Revenue", ascending=False).head(12)
            )
            st.plotly_chart(
                px.bar(category_summary, x="Category", y="Net Revenue", title="Top 12 categories"),
                width="stretch",
            )
        else:
            st.info("Add a text/category field to compare revenue across groups.")
    with chart_col2:
        st.markdown("#### Revenue trend")
        if date_col == "No date filter":
            st.info("Select a date field above to display period trends.")
        else:
            cadence = st.selectbox("Period", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"], key="ex_cadence")
            try:
                time_series, excluded_dates = revenue_by_period(
                    filtered[date_col], calculations["Net Revenue"], cadence
                )
                if excluded_dates:
                    st.warning(f"{excluded_dates:,} rows with invalid/missing dates were excluded from this trend only.")
                if not time_series.empty:
                    st.plotly_chart(
                        px.line(time_series, x="Period", y="Revenue", markers=True,
                                title="Net revenue by period"), width="stretch",
                    )
                    if time_series["Growth %"].notna().any():
                        st.caption("Growth is calculated only for adjacent periods with positive prior-period revenue.")
                else:
                    st.info("No valid dated records remain after filtering.")
            except ValueError as error:
                st.warning(f"Cannot calculate the trend: {error}")

    with st.expander("03  Â·  Audit records and download", expanded=False):
        st.dataframe(filtered.head(200), width="stretch")
        output = build_financial_export(filtered, calculations)
        st.download_button(
            "Download filtered records and financial calculations",
            data=excel_safe_csv(output),
            file_name="python_bi_executive_filtered_data.csv", mime="text/csv", key="ex_export",
        )
        st.caption(
            "Export includes all matching records, not just the 200 previewed rows. "
            "Calculated fields are named BI_Gross_Revenue, BI_Net_Revenue, etc. "
            "Spreadsheet-dangerous text is protected in the exported copy."
        )

    if "Mapped Costs" in calculations.columns:
        with st.expander("04  Â·  Category profitability and transaction exceptions", expanded=False):
            st.caption(
                "These results use exactly the filtered records and financial field mapping above. "
                "Profit is after mapped costs; it is not necessarily accounting net profit."
            )
            available_categories = [
                c for c in filtered.columns
                if pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(filtered[c])
                or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(filtered[c])
                or isinstance(filtered[c].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)
            ]
            if available_categories:
                category_field = st.selectbox(
                    "Group profitability by", available_categories,
                    index=_default(available_categories, ["Product", "Region", "City"]),
                    key="ex_profit_category",
                )
                groups = category_profitability(filtered, calculations, category_field)
                st.dataframe(groups.head(100), width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                st.download_button(
                    "Export category profitability",
                    data=excel_safe_csv(groups),
                    file_name="python_bi_category_profitability.csv",
                    mime="text/csv", key="ex_profitability_export",
                )
                st.caption(
                    "Category totals reconcile to the filtered net revenue, mapped costs "
                    "and aggregate profit above. A zero net-revenue category has undefined margin."
                )
            else:
                st.info("No categorical source field is available for grouped analysis.")
            exceptions = loss_making_rows(filtered, calculations)
            st.metric("Loss-making transactions", f"{len(exceptions):,}")
            if not exceptions.empty:
                st.dataframe(exceptions.head(100), width="stretch")
                st.download_button(
                    "Export loss-making transactions",
                    data=excel_safe_csv(exceptions),
                    file_name="python_bi_loss_making_transactions.csv",
                    mime="text/csv", key="ex_loss_export",
                )
            else:
                st.success("No transactions have a negative profit after mapped costs.")

    # Phase 3 Batches 7 + 8: optional consolidated analytical diagnostics.
    render_comparison_panel(filtered, calculations)
    render_readiness_panel(data)

    # Phase 3 Batches 9 + 10: chart selection without leaving Executive Overview.
    render_chart_studio(filtered, calculations)
    # Intentionally source-wide and opt-in, unlike Dashboard-filtered charts.
    render_performance_lab(data)

    # Phase 3 Batches 11 + 12 â€” executive report uses the same filtered results.
    filter_descriptions = [f"{len(filtered):,} of {len(data):,} source records included"]
    if category != "No category filter":
        filter_descriptions.append(f"Category: {category}")
        if selected is not None:
            filter_descriptions.append(f"Selected values: {', '.join(map(str, selected[:20]))}" +
                                       (" (additional values omitted from label)" if len(selected) > 20 else ""))
    if start is not None:
        filter_descriptions.append(f"Dates {start} through {end} in {date_col}")
    if value_bounds is not None:
        filter_descriptions.append(f"{numeric_filter} from {value_bounds[0]:,.2f} to {value_bounds[1]:,.2f}")
    mapping = {"Gross revenue": revenue_col, "Cost of sales": cost_col,
               "Discounts": discount_col, "Returns": returns_col}
    render_executive_report(
        filtered, calculations, totals,
        dataset_name=dataset_name,
        mapping=mapping,
        filter_summary="; ".join(filter_descriptions),
    )

    # Phase 3 Batch 14 â€” optional concentration insight from filtered financial rows.
    render_portfolio_panel(filtered, calculations, totals)


========== FILE: executive_report.py ==========

"""Streamlit Section 09 â€” on-demand, audited executive report downloads."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv
from report_engine import build_executive_report
from report_bundle import build_report_archive


def render_executive_report(
    filtered: pd.DataFrame,
    calculations: pd.DataFrame,
    totals: dict,
    *,
    dataset_name: str,
    mapping: dict[str, str],
    filter_summary: str,
) -> None:
    with st.expander("09  Â·  Executive report centre", expanded=False):
        st.caption(
            "Create a standalone, print-ready executive HTML report from the same filtered records "
            "and commercial mapping used above. CSV reports include all category/period rows. "
            "No transactions or files leave your computer."
        )
        categories = [c for c in filtered.columns if
                      pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(filtered[c])
                      or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(filtered[c])
                      or isinstance(filtered[c].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)]
        dates = [c for c in filtered.columns if
                 "date" in str(c).casefold() or "time" in str(c).casefold()
                 or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(filtered[c])]
        a, b = st.columns(2)
        with a:
            group = st.selectbox(
                "Report category", ["No category breakdown"] + categories,
                index=(["No category breakdown"] + categories).index("Product")
                if "Product" in categories else 0, key="exec_report_group",
            )
            frequency = st.selectbox(
                "Report calendar periods", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"],
                key="exec_report_frequency",
            )
        with b:
            date = st.selectbox(
                "Report date field", ["No time breakdown"] + dates,
                index=(["No time breakdown"] + dates).index("Date")
                if "Date" in dates else 0, key="exec_report_date",
            )
            unit = st.text_input(
                "Currency / reporting unit", value="Not specified",
                max_chars=40, help="A label only; figures are not converted between currencies.",
                key="exec_report_unit",
            )
        st.caption(
            "The printed report shows aggregate results only, not customer-level transaction records. "
            "The transaction-level CSV audit remains in Section 03. "
            "Use Chrome/Edge Print â†’ Save as PDF after opening the downloaded HTML file."
        )
        if not st.button("Generate executive report", key="exec_report_generate", type="primary"):
            return
        try:
            bundle = build_executive_report(
                filtered, calculations, totals,
                dataset_name=dataset_name,
                category=None if group == "No category breakdown" else group,
                date_column=None if date == "No time breakdown" else date,
                period=frequency,
                field_mapping=mapping,
                filter_notes=filter_summary,
                currency_label=unit.strip() or "Not specified",
            )
        except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
            st.error(f"Report generation stopped: {exc}")
            return
        st.success(f"Report ready for {bundle['matching_rows']:,} filtered transactions.")
        # One downloadable package with self-verifiable aggregate reports.
        try:
            archive = build_report_archive(
                bundle, dataset_name=dataset_name,
                filter_context=filter_summary,
                period=frequency,
                category=None if group == "No category breakdown" else group,
                currency=unit.strip() or "Not specified",
            )
        except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
            st.warning(f"Complete report pack unavailable: {exc}")
        else:
            st.download_button(
                "Download complete executive report pack (ZIP)",
                data=archive, file_name="python_bi_executive_report_pack.zip",
                mime="application/zip", key="exec_report_pack_zip", on_click="ignore",
            )
            st.caption(
                "Pack includes the executive HTML, Excel-friendly aggregate CSVs, "
                "README and SHA-256 integrity manifest. No raw transaction rows are included; "
                "selected category labels may contain sensitive identifiers."
            )
        st.download_button(
            "Download executive report (HTML)", data=bundle["html"],
            file_name="python_bi_executive_report.html", mime="text/html",
            key="exec_report_html_download", on_click="ignore",
        )
        left, middle, right = st.columns(3)
        with left:
            st.download_button(
                "Download KPI summary (CSV)", excel_safe_csv(bundle["summary"]),
                file_name="python_bi_report_kpis.csv", mime="text/csv",
                key="exec_report_kpi_csv", on_click="ignore",
            )
        with middle:
            if not bundle["categories"].empty:
                st.download_button(
                    "Download category report (CSV)", excel_safe_csv(bundle["categories"]),
                    file_name="python_bi_report_categories.csv", mime="text/csv",
                    key="exec_report_category_csv", on_click="ignore",
                )
        with right:
            if not bundle["periods"].empty:
                st.download_button(
                    "Download period report (CSV)", excel_safe_csv(bundle["periods"]),
                    file_name="python_bi_report_periods.csv", mime="text/csv",
                    key="exec_report_period_csv", on_click="ignore",
                )
        st.dataframe(bundle["summary"], width="stretch", hide_index=True)
        st.caption("Report totals are checked against the same underlying row-level commercial calculations.")


========== FILE: explorer_workspace.py ==========

"""Professional, full-source Explorer: controls grouped and previews bounded.

Importing this module does not read, persist or mutate any source dataset.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv
from full_dataset_engine import (
    DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE, MAX_EXPORT_ROWS, MAX_PREVIEW_ROWS,
    explore_dataset, safe_preview,
)


def render_explorer_workspace(source: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    """Replace the old first-N-rows-only search without changing the Library."""
    if source.columns.empty:
        st.warning("This dataset has no columns to explore.")
        return
    if not source.columns.is_unique or any(not isinstance(col, str) for col in source.columns):
        st.error("Column names must be unique text values before exploration.")
        return

    # Scope widget state to the column schema to prevent invalid stale selections
    # when users activate a different Dataset Library file.
    schema = repr((tuple(source.columns), tuple(str(t) for t in source.dtypes)))
    schema_id = hashlib.sha256(schema.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:12]
    def widget_key(suffix: str) -> str:
        return f"explorer_v2_{schema_id}_{suffix}"

    st.caption(
        "Searches, category filters and numeric filters examine ALL active records. "
        "Preview and CSV sizes are limited separately to protect responsiveness."
    )
    overview1, overview2, overview3 = st.columns(3)
    overview1.metric("Source records", f"{len(source):,}")
    overview2.metric("Source columns", f"{len(source.columns):,}")
    overview3.metric("Preview cap", f"{MAX_PREVIEW_ROWS:,}")

    with st.expander("01 Â· Fields and search", expanded=True):
        selected = st.multiselect(
            "Columns to explore", options=source.columns.tolist(),
            default=source.columns.tolist(), key=widget_key("columns")
        )
        query = st.text_input(
            "Search all records (case-insensitive, literal)",
            placeholder="Example: Laptop, Delhi, T001 or 2026-01",
            key=widget_key("query"),
        )
        preview_limit = st.selectbox(
            "Maximum preview rows", [10, 20, 50, 100, 500, 1000],
            index=3, key=widget_key("preview_rows"),
        )

    if not selected:
        st.info("Choose at least one column to explore.")
        return

    category_column = None
    selected_values = None
    numeric_column = None
    numeric_min = None
    numeric_max = None
    sort_column = None
    ascending = True

    with st.expander("02 Â· Optional filters and sorting", expanded=False):
        category_choice = st.selectbox(
            "Category column", ["No category filter", *selected],
            key=widget_key("category_column"),
        )
        if category_choice != "No category filter":
            category_column = category_choice
            # Show up to 100 most common options. High-cardinality values can
            # still be found via the full-dataset text search.
            values = source[category_column].value_counts(dropna=True).head(100)
            category_options = [str(v) for v in values.index]
            selected_values = st.multiselect(
                "Category values (leave empty for all)", category_options,
                default=[], key=widget_key("category_values"),
            )
            if source[category_column].nunique(dropna=True) > len(category_options):
                st.caption(
                    "The selector shows only the 100 most common categories. "
                    "Use text search to find other category values across all rows."
                )
            if not selected_values:
                # An empty widget means the filter is not active.
                category_column = None
                selected_values = None

        numeric_choices = [col for col in selected if pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(source[col])]
        numeric_choice = st.selectbox(
            "Numeric column", ["No numeric filter", *numeric_choices],
            key=widget_key("numeric_column"),
        )
        if numeric_choice != "No numeric filter":
            candidates = pd.to_numeric(source[numeric_choice], errors="coerce")
            candidates = candidates.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).dropna()
            if not candidates.empty:
                numeric_column = numeric_choice
                numeric_min = st.number_input(
                    "Minimum (inclusive)", value=float(candidates.min()),
                    key=widget_key("min"),
                )
                numeric_max = st.number_input(
                    "Maximum (inclusive)", value=float(candidates.max()),
                    key=widget_key("max"),
                )
            else:
                st.info("The chosen numeric field has no finite values.")

        sort_choice = st.selectbox(
            "Sort matching records", ["No sorting", *selected],
            key=widget_key("sort_column"),
        )
        if sort_choice != "No sorting":
            sort_column = sort_choice
            ascending = st.radio(
                "Sort direction", ["Ascending", "Descending"],
                horizontal=True, key=widget_key("sort_direction"),
            ) == "Ascending"
            if len(source) >= 100_000:
                st.caption("Sorting large matching sets may take additional time and memory.")

    if numeric_column and numeric_min > numeric_max:
        st.warning("The minimum numeric value must not exceed the maximum.")
        return

    try:
        preview, export_rows, info = explore_dataset(
            source, selected, search_text=query,
            category_column=category_column, category_values=selected_values,
            numeric_column=numeric_column, minimum=numeric_min, maximum=numeric_max,
            sort_column=sort_column, ascending=ascending,
            preview_limit=preview_limit, export_limit=MAX_EXPORT_ROWS,
            chunk_size=DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE,
        )
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as error:
        st.error(f"Unable to apply the selected Explorer controls: {error}")
        return

    st.markdown("**Matching records**")
    stat1, stat2, stat3 = st.columns(3)
    stat1.metric("Full-dataset matches", f"{info.matched_rows:,}")
    stat2.metric("Records shown", f"{info.shown_rows:,}")
    stat3.metric("Filter processing", f"{info.total_seconds:.3f} s")
    st.dataframe(safe_preview(preview), width="stretch", hide_index=True)
    st.caption(
        f"Results are computed across {info.source_rows:,} source records, "
        f"with a separate preview limit of {preview_limit:,}. "
        "The complete active dataset is not changed."
    )

    with st.expander("03 Â· Download and field quality", expanded=False):
        if info.export_truncated:
            st.warning(
                f"{info.matched_rows:,} records match, but the download is capped "
                f"at {info.exported_rows:,} rows. It is a PARTIAL export. "
                "Refine your filters to export all matching rows."
            )
        else:
            st.caption(f"This export contains all {info.exported_rows:,} matching rows.")
        if info.exported_rows:
            st.download_button(
                "Download filtered CSV",
                excel_safe_csv(export_rows),
                file_name="python_bi_full_dataset_explorer.csv",
                mime="text/csv", key=widget_key("csv"),
            )
        else:
            st.info("No matching records are available to export.")

        st.caption("Source field summary. Exact missing counts are optional on large files.")
        check_exact = st.checkbox(
            "Calculate exact missing and duplicate counts",
            value=len(source) <= 25_000,
            key=widget_key("exact_quality"),
        )
        field_info = pd.DataFrame({
            "Column": [str(col) for col in source.columns],
            "Data type": [str(dtype) for dtype in source.dtypes],
        })
        if check_exact:
            field_info["Missing cells"] = source.isna().sum().to_numpy(dtype=int)
            st.metric("Exact duplicate records", f"{source.duplicated().sum():,}")
        st.dataframe(field_info, width="stretch", hide_index=True)


========== FILE: final_financial_checks.py ==========

"""Independent arithmetic and edge-case acceptance for Phases 4 and 5.

Synthetic fixtures only; never reads private datasets or calls external services.
Results contain only check identifiers and pass/fail messages, not records.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from math import isclose
from collections.abc import Callable
import pandas as pd
from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics, NONE
from business_metrics import apply_dashboard_filters, revenue_by_period, break_even_scenario
from performance_insights import category_profitability
from audit_exports import build_financial_export, excel_safe_csv
from report_engine import build_executive_report
from sector_acceptance import check_one_sector
from schema_aware_dashboard import assess_legacy


def close(a, b, label):
    assert a is not None and isclose(float(a),float(b),abs_tol=0.01,rel_tol=1e-10), (label,a,b)


def expect_error(fn: Callable, message: str):
    try: fn()
    except (ValueError, TypeError): return
    raise AssertionError(message)


def case() -> pd.DataFrame:
    return pd.DataFrame({
        'Category':['A',None,'A','B'],
        'Date':['2025-12-12','invalid','2026-01-05','2026-02-10'],
        'Gross':[100.,0.,-20.,80.],
        'Cost':[40.,0.,0.,90.],
        'Discount':[5.,0.,0.,0.],
        'Returns':[0.,0.,0.,10.],
        'Operations':[3.,0.,0.,7.],
        'Taxes':[2.,0.,0.,3.],
    })


def financial_edge_suite() -> list[dict]:
    tests=[]
    def record(key, title, fn):
        try:
            detail=fn()
            tests.append({'id':key,'title':title,'status':'PASS','detail':str(detail or 'Verified')})
        except Exception as err:
            tests.append({'id':key,'title':title,'status':'FAIL',
                          'detail':f'{type(err).__name__}: {err}'})
    frame=case(); original=frame.copy(deep=True)
    audit, totals=calculate_commercial_metrics(frame,'Gross','Cost','Discount','Returns','Operations','Taxes',complete_costs=True)

    def independent_reconciliation():
        # Independent row arithmetic, without using the totals being tested.
        gross=sum([100,0,-20,80]); discount=5; returns=10
        expenses=sum([40,0,0,90])+sum([3,0,0,7])+sum([2,0,0,3])
        for key,val in [('gross_revenue',gross),('total_discounts',discount),
                        ('total_returns',returns),('net_revenue',gross-discount-returns),
                        ('mapped_costs',expenses),('profit_after_mapped_costs',gross-discount-returns-expenses),
                        ('net_profit',gross-discount-returns-expenses)]:close(totals[key],val,key)
        close(float(audit['Net Revenue'].sum()),gross-discount-returns,'audited Net Revenue')
        return 'Gross 160; discounts 5; returns 10; net 145; mapped costs 145; net 0'
    record('F5-01','Independent revenue/cost/discount/returns/tax reconciliation',independent_reconciliation)

    def row_losses():
        individual=[100-5-40-3-2,0,-20,80-10-90-7-3]
        close(totals['loss_after_mapped_costs'],sum(-x for x in individual if x<0),'row losses')
        close(totals['net_business_loss'],0,'net business loss')
        return 'Row-level losses deliberately distinguished from net business loss'
    record('F5-02','Loss vs cumulative row losses',row_losses)

    record('F5-03','Zero net revenue yields undefined profit margin',
           lambda: (lambda t: t['profit_margin_pct'] is None or (_ for _ in ()).throw(AssertionError('margin not undefined')))(
               calculate_commercial_metrics(pd.DataFrame({'Gross':[0.], 'Cost':[5.]}),'Gross','Cost')[1]))
    record('F5-04','Missing cost cannot silently discard operating expenses',
           lambda: expect_error(lambda: calculate_commercial_metrics(frame,'Gross',operating_expenses='Operations'),
                                'Operating expenses without mapped cost did not raise'))
    record('F5-05','Taxes without cost are not silently ignored',
           lambda: expect_error(lambda: calculate_commercial_metrics(frame,'Gross',taxes='Taxes'),
                                'Tax without mapped cost did not raise'))
    record('F5-06','Duplicate numeric column role prohibited',
           lambda: expect_error(lambda: calculate_commercial_metrics(frame,'Gross','Cost',discount='Cost'),
                                'Overlapping field mapping was accepted'))
    record('F5-07','Invalid financial null blocks figures',
           lambda: expect_error(lambda: calculate_commercial_metrics(frame.assign(Cost=[float('nan'),0,0,90]),'Gross','Cost'),
                                'Missing cost accepted'))
    record('F5-08','Infinite monetary value rejected',
           lambda: expect_error(lambda: calculate_commercial_metrics(frame.assign(Gross=[float('inf'),0,-20,80]),'Gross','Cost'),
                                'Infinite revenue accepted'))
    record('F5-09','Duplicate column labels rejected',
           lambda: expect_error(lambda: calculate_commercial_metrics(pd.DataFrame([[1,2]],columns=['Gross','Gross']),'Gross'),
                                'Duplicate labels accepted'))
    def no_net_claim():
        _,m=calculate_commercial_metrics(frame,'Gross','Cost')
        assert 'net_profit' not in m and 'roi_pct' not in m
        return 'Profit after mapped costs is not labelled accounting net profit'
    record('F5-10','Net profit requires explicit complete-cost confirmation',no_net_claim)
    def capital_rules():
        data=pd.DataFrame({'Gross':[100.,100.],'Cost':[30.,40.],'Capital':[50.,50.]})
        _,m=calculate_commercial_metrics(data,'Gross','Cost',invested_capital='Capital',complete_costs=True)
        assert 'roi_pct' not in m
        _,yes=calculate_commercial_metrics(data,'Gross','Cost',invested_capital='Capital',complete_costs=True,capital_is_row_additive=True)
        close(yes['roi_pct'],130,'ROI')
        return 'ROI 130% only with confirmed additive capital'
    record('F5-11','ROI opt-in additive-capital treatment',capital_rules)
    def filters():
        empty=apply_dashboard_filters(frame,category_column='Category',categories=[])
        assert empty.empty
        subset=apply_dashboard_filters(frame,numeric_column='Gross',numeric_min=-20,numeric_max=0)
        assert len(subset)==2
        return 'Explicit empty selection returns zero; negative and zero values preserved'
    record('F4-01','Dashboard empty and negative numeric filters',filters)
    def dates():
        periods,omitted=revenue_by_period(frame['Date'],audit['Net Revenue'])
        assert omitted==1 and len(periods)==3
        close(periods['Revenue'].sum(),totals['net_revenue'],'dated totals')  # missing date row has net 0
        assert periods.iloc[0]['Period']=='2025-12'
        return 'Multi-year grouping correct; 1 invalid date excluded from trend only'
    record('F4-02','Date parsing and multi-year period integrity',dates)
    def category():
        grouped=category_profitability(frame,audit,'Category')
        close(grouped['Net Revenue'].sum(),145,'category revenues')
        close(grouped['Profit After Mapped Costs'].sum(),0,'category profit')
        assert grouped['Records'].sum()==4
        return 'Missing category retained and group totals reconcile'
    record('F4-03','Null category and aggregation reconciliation',category)
    def report():
        output=build_executive_report(frame,audit,totals,dataset_name='SYNTHETIC ONLY',
            category='Category',date_column='Date',period='Monthly',
            field_mapping={'gross revenue':'Gross'},filter_notes='Synthetic', currency_label='Unspecified')
        close(output['summary'].loc[output['summary']['Metric']=='Net Revenue','Value'].iloc[0],145,'report net')
        close(output['categories']['Net Revenue'].sum(),145,'report groups')
        assert output['missing_date_rows']==1
        assert b'SYNTHETIC ONLY' in output['html']
        return 'KPI, category, trend and HTML report agree on the synthetic fixture'
    record('F7-01','Report-to-source totals and missing-date disclosure',report)
    def incorrect_report_blocked():
        wrong=dict(totals);wrong['net_revenue']=999
        expect_error(lambda:build_executive_report(frame,audit,wrong,dataset_name='Synthetic',
                  category='Category',date_column='Date',period='Monthly',
                  field_mapping={},filter_notes='Synthetic'), 'Mismatched report totals accepted')
        return 'Incorrect report totals stop report generation'
    record('F7-02','Tampered report totals fail closed',incorrect_report_blocked)
    def safe_csv():
        exported=excel_safe_csv(pd.DataFrame({'Text':['=HYPERLINK("https://test")','  @SUM(1,2)','plain'],
                                               'Amount':[-5.0,0.0,5.0]}))
        assert b"'=HYPERLINK" in exported and b"'  @SUM" in exported and b'-5.0' in exported
        assert len(build_financial_export(frame,audit))==len(frame)
        return 'Formula-like text escaped and negative numeric amounts preserved'
    record('F7-03','Spreadsheet export formula safety and record alignment',safe_csv)
    record('F4-04','Original source DataFrame unchanged',
           lambda:pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(frame,original))
