    for sector in ('Retail','Services','Manufacturing'):
        def sector_test(name=sector):
            report=check_one_sector(name,120)
            assert report['status']=='PASS'
            return f'{name}: independent sector finance/dashboard/report checks passed'
        record(f'F45-SECTOR-{sector.upper()}',f'{sector} schema acceptance',sector_test)
    def legacy_compatibility():
        base=pd.DataFrame({'Sales':[100.,200.],'Total Cost':[60.,100.],'Profit':[40.,100.],
             'Profit Margin (%)':[40.,50.],'Quantity':[2,4],
             'Product':['A','B'],'Region':['N','S'],'City':['X','Y'],
             'Date':pd.to_datetime(['2026-01-01','2026-02-01']),
             'ProductID':['P1','P2'],'CustomerID':['C1','C2']})
        assert assess_legacy(base).compatible
        assert not assess_legacy(base.assign(Profit=[99.,100.])).compatible
        assert not assess_legacy(base.assign(**{'Profit Margin (%)':[99.,50.]})).compatible
        assert not assess_legacy(base.drop(columns=['ProductID'])).compatible
        assert not assess_legacy(base.assign(Sales=[float('nan'),200.])).compatible
        assert not assess_legacy(base, max_rows=1).compatible
        return 'Correct legacy passes; inconsistent, missing, nonfinite or oversize legacy is redirected'
    record('F4-05','Legacy compatibility and reconciliation gate',legacy_compatibility)
    record('F5-12','Break-even and noncontributing-price handling',
           lambda: (close(break_even_scenario(500,20,10)['break_even_revenue'],1000,'break-even'),
                    break_even_scenario(500,10,20)['break_even_units'] is None or (_ for _ in ()).throw(AssertionError('finite break-even wrongly returned'))))
    return tests


if __name__=='__main__':
    results=financial_edge_suite()
    for r in results:print(r['status']+': '+r['id']+' â€” '+r['title'])
    print('TOTAL:',sum(x['status']=='PASS' for x in results),'passed,',sum(x['status']=='FAIL' for x in results),'failed')
    raise SystemExit(0 if all(x['status']=='PASS' for x in results) else 1)


========== FILE: final_reliability_checks.py ==========

"""Non-destructive local recovery, dataset path and export-safety tests.

All files created for this suite live in temporary directories; no user's
stored_datasets directory, saved active dataset, or real workbook is modified.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
from pathlib import Path
from hashlib import sha256
from io import BytesIO
import json
import pandas as pd
from local_file_guard import safe_stored_path, validate_upload
from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv
from report_engine import build_executive_report
from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics


class FakeUpload:
    def __init__(self, name: str, raw: bytes):
        self.name=name;self.raw=raw;self.size=len(raw)
    def getvalue(self):return self.raw


def run_recovery_checks() -> list[dict]:
    results=[]
    def run(key, title, fn):
        try:
            detail=fn()
            results.append({'id':key,'title':title,'status':'PASS','detail':str(detail or 'Verified')})
        except Exception as err:
            results.append({'id':key,'title':title,'status':'FAIL','detail':f'{type(err).__name__}: {err}'})
    def reject(fn):
        try:fn()
        except (OSError,ValueError,FileNotFoundError):return
        raise AssertionError('Unsafe path/input was accepted')
    def private_library():
        with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            library=Path(tmp)/'stored_datasets';library.mkdir()
            data=b'Revenue,Cost\n100,40\n'
            named='fixture_001.csv'; saved=safe_stored_path(library,named)
            saved.write_bytes(data)
            metadata={'stored_name':named,'hash':sha256(data).hexdigest()}
            # Simulate the same SHA-256 check used for app.py restore.
            assert sha256(safe_stored_path(library,metadata['stored_name'],must_exist=True).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==metadata['hash']
            saved.write_bytes(data+b'200,120\n')
            assert sha256(saved.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=metadata['hash']
            reject(lambda:safe_stored_path(library,'../outside.csv',must_exist=True))
            reject(lambda:safe_stored_path(library,'C:\\outside.csv'))
            reject(lambda:safe_stored_path(library,'notfound.csv',must_exist=True))
        return 'Temporary library tampering detected; traversal and missing saved files blocked'
    run('G4-01','Temporary library recovery/integrity verification',private_library)
    def valid_csv():
        info=validate_upload(FakeUpload('SYNTHETIC.csv',b'Gross,Cost\n10,5\n'))
        assert info['extension']=='.csv' and info['sha256']==sha256(b'Gross,Cost\n10,5\n').hexdigest()
        return 'Local CSV passed validation without uploading or changing any saved dataset'
    run('G4-02','Local fake-CSV upload validation',valid_csv)
    def invalid_uploads():
        reject(lambda:validate_upload(FakeUpload('../outside.csv',b'a,b\n1,2')))
        reject(lambda:validate_upload(FakeUpload('payload.exe',b'a,b\n1,2')))
        reject(lambda:validate_upload(FakeUpload('trick.csv',b'PK\x03\x04fake archive')))
        reject(lambda:validate_upload(FakeUpload('blank.csv',b'')))
        return 'Traversal, unsupported extension, archive-masquerading and empty data blocked'
    run('G4-03','Unsafe dataset input rejection',invalid_uploads)
    def formula_export():
        src=pd.DataFrame({'Text':['+EVIL()','  =1+1','-CMD()', '@SUM(1,2)'],
                          'Revenue':[1.,-2.,0.,3.]})
        original=src.copy(deep=True)
        csv=excel_safe_csv(src)
        assert csv.startswith(b'\xef\xbb\xbf')
        for marker in [b"'+EVIL",b"'  =1+1",b"'-CMD",b"'@SUM"]: assert marker in csv, marker
        assert b'-2.0' in csv
        pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(src,original)
        return 'Formula-like text guarded; negative numeric amount stays numeric; source untouched'
    run('G4-04','Spreadsheet export hardening',formula_export)
    def report_escape():
        source=pd.DataFrame({'Category':['<script>alert(1)</script>'], 'Gross':[40.], 'Cost':[10.]})
        audit,totals=calculate_commercial_metrics(source,'Gross','Cost')
        result=build_executive_report(source,audit,totals,dataset_name='<private>&test',
                                      category='Category',date_column=None,period='Monthly',
                                      field_mapping={'source':'<script>alert(1)</script>'},
                                      filter_notes='<x>',currency_label='Unspecified')
        html=result['html'].decode()
        assert '<script>alert(1)</script>' not in html and '&lt;script&gt;' in html
        assert '<private>' not in html and '&lt;private&gt;' in html
        return 'User-provided category, field map and dataset labels are HTML-escaped'
    run('G4-05','HTML report escaping',report_escape)
    def invalid_report():
        source=pd.DataFrame({'Gross':[100.], 'Cost':[10.]})
        audit,totals=calculate_commercial_metrics(source,'Gross','Cost')
        mismatch=dict(totals);mismatch['gross_revenue']=200.
        reject(lambda:build_executive_report(source,audit,mismatch,dataset_name='SYNTHETIC',
                              category=None,date_column=None,period='Monthly',field_mapping={},filter_notes='test'))
        return 'Report generator refuses mismatched audit totals'
    run('G4-06','Out-of-balance report rejection',invalid_report)
    return results


if __name__=='__main__':
    out=run_recovery_checks()
    for item in out: print(item['status']+': '+item['id']+' â€” '+item['title'])
    print('TOTAL:',sum(x['status']=='PASS' for x in out),'passed,',sum(x['status']=='FAIL' for x in out),'failed')
    raise SystemExit(0 if all(x['status']=='PASS' for x in out) else 1)


========== FILE: forecast_engine.py ==========

"""Python BI Studio | explicit scenario stress tests and historical research.

No live-market connection, proprietary ML claim, investment recommendation, or
promise of future returns. Market bootstrap is an IID historical-return
resampling experiment; its distribution is NOT a calibrated confidence interval.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from math import isfinite

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from investment_engine import FREQUENCY_PERIODS, evaluate_project


def _finite(value: float, field: str) -> float:
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValueError(f"{field} must be a finite number.")
    try:
        number = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{field} must be numeric.") from exc
    if not isfinite(number):
        raise ValueError(f"{field} must be finite.")
    return number


def evaluate_cashflow_stress(
    initial_investment: float,
    cashflows: Sequence[float],
    discount_rate_pct: float,
    downside_pct: float = 20,
    upside_pct: float = 20,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Deterministic cash-flow shocks, not probabilistic forecasts.

    Downside = base - abs(base) * shock, so negative outflows get worse too.
    Initial project outlay is held fixed and no costs are invented.
    """
    downside = _finite(downside_pct, "Downside shock")
    upside = _finite(upside_pct, "Upside change")
    if not 0 <= downside <= 100 or not 0 <= upside <= 100:
        raise ValueError("Cash-flow shocks must be between 0 and 100 percent.")
    if isinstance(cashflows, (str, bytes)) or not isinstance(cashflows, Sequence):
        raise ValueError("Cash flows must be a sequence of annual amounts.")
    # evaluate_project performs comprehensive validation of the initial values.
    base_summary, _ = evaluate_project(initial_investment, cashflows, discount_rate_pct)
    base = [float(v) for v in cashflows]
    versions = (
        ("Downside", [v - abs(v) * downside / 100 for v in base]),
        ("Base", base),
        ("Upside", [v + abs(v) * upside / 100 for v in base]),
    )
    summaries = []
    audits = []
    for name, flows in versions:
        result, audit = evaluate_project(initial_investment, flows, discount_rate_pct)
        summaries.append({
            "Scenario": name,
            "NPV": result["npv"],
            "IRR % (if unique)": result["irr_pct"],
            "Simple Payback Years": result["simple_payback_years"],
            "Discounted Payback Years": result["discounted_payback_years"],
            "Undiscounted ROI %": result["undiscounted_scenario_roi_pct"],
            "Total Net Cash Inflows": result["sum_of_net_cash_flows"],
        })
        audit.insert(0, "Scenario", name)
        audits.append(audit)
    assert len(base_summary) > 0
    return pd.DataFrame(summaries), pd.concat(audits, ignore_index=True)


def _observed_returns(history: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
    """Validate a chronological market-history output from evaluate_market_history."""
    if not isinstance(history, pd.DataFrame) or not {"Date", "Price"}.issubset(history.columns):
        raise ValueError("Provide validated historical Date/Price rows from Financial Markets.")
    if not 2 <= len(history) <= 100_000:
        raise ValueError("Historical price series must contain between 2 and 100,000 records.")
    dates = pd.to_datetime(history["Date"], utc=True, errors="coerce")
    prices = pd.to_numeric(history["Price"], errors="coerce").to_numpy(dtype=float)
    if dates.isna().any() or not np.isfinite(prices).all() or (prices <= 0).any():
        raise ValueError("Historical dates and strictly positive finite prices are required.")
    if dates.duplicated().any() or not dates.is_monotonic_increasing:
        raise ValueError("Historical timestamps must be unique and chronological.")
    # Ratio avoids pct_change's implicit forward-fill semantics.
    ratios = prices[1:] / prices[:-1]
    if not np.isfinite(ratios).all() or (ratios <= 0).any():
        raise ValueError("Historical price ratios cannot be non-finite or non-positive.")
    log_returns = np.log(ratios)
    if not np.isfinite(log_returns).all():
        raise ValueError("Observed log returns must be finite.")
    return log_returns, dates.iloc[1:].to_numpy()


def simulate_historical_bootstrap(
    history: pd.DataFrame,
    frequency: str,
    horizon_periods: int = 12,
    simulated_paths: int = 1500,
    starting_value: float = 100.0,
    seed: int = 42,
) -> tuple[dict, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Resample historical log returns IID; do not imply a reliable forecast.

    Returns per-period p05/p50/p95 of simulated *hypothetical* portfolio values.
    This is a distribution of sampled scenarios, not a predictive confidence band.
    """
    if frequency not in FREQUENCY_PERIODS:
        raise ValueError("Frequency must be Daily, Weekly or Monthly.")
    if type(horizon_periods) is not int or not 1 <= horizon_periods <= 60:
        raise ValueError("Horizon must be an integer between 1 and 60 observations.")
    if type(simulated_paths) is not int or not 500 <= simulated_paths <= 3000:
        raise ValueError("Simulations must be between 500 and 3,000 paths.")
    if type(seed) is not int or not 0 <= seed <= 2_147_483_647:
        raise ValueError("Seed must be a non-negative 32-bit integer.")
    initial = _finite(starting_value, "Hypothetical starting value")
    if initial <= 0 or initial > 1e12:
        raise ValueError("Hypothetical starting value must be above zero and no greater than one trillion.")
    log_returns, _ = _observed_returns(history)
    if len(log_returns) < 60:
        raise ValueError("At least 60 historical period returns are needed for this simulation.")
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    draws = rng.choice(log_returns, size=(simulated_paths, horizon_periods), replace=True)
    running_logs = np.cumsum(draws, axis=1)
    if np.any(running_logs > 600) or np.any(running_logs < -600):
        raise ValueError("Historical returns are too extreme for numerically stable simulation.")
    trajectory = initial * np.exp(running_logs)
    if not np.isfinite(trajectory).all():
        raise ValueError("Simulation produced non-finite outcomes; review historical data.")
    terminal = trajectory[:, -1]
    percentiles = np.quantile(trajectory, [0.05, 0.5, 0.95], axis=0)
    results = pd.DataFrame({
        "Future Observation": np.arange(0, horizon_periods + 1, dtype=int),
        "Simulation P05": np.concatenate(([initial], percentiles[0])),
        "Simulation Median": np.concatenate(([initial], percentiles[1])),
        "Simulation P95": np.concatenate(([initial], percentiles[2])),
    })
    terminal_p05, terminal_median, terminal_p95 = np.quantile(terminal, [0.05, 0.5, 0.95])
    return {
        "method": "IID bootstrap of uploaded historical log returns; descriptive what-if distribution only",
        "observed_return_count": len(log_returns),
        "observation_frequency": frequency,
        "forecast_horizon_observations": horizon_periods,
        "simulated_paths": simulated_paths,
        "seed": seed,
        "hypothetical_initial_value": initial,
        "terminal_value_p05": float(terminal_p05),
        "terminal_value_median": float(terminal_median),
        "terminal_value_p95": float(terminal_p95),
        "simulated_share_finishing_below_start_pct": float(np.mean(terminal < initial) * 100),
        "calibrated_prediction": False,
    }, results


def backtest_expanding_mean(
    history: pd.DataFrame,
    training_min: int = 40,
    max_evaluation_periods: int = 60,
) -> tuple[dict, pd.DataFrame]:
    """One-step-ahead, non-leaky return baseline vs flat-price baseline.

    The historical mean for period i uses returns [0:i], never the target i.
    Predictions are NOT trade signals or learned AI models.
    """
    if type(training_min) is not int or not 40 <= training_min <= 500:
        raise ValueError("Minimum training returns must be an integer between 40 and 500.")
    if type(max_evaluation_periods) is not int or not 20 <= max_evaluation_periods <= 200:
        raise ValueError("Evaluation periods must be an integer between 20 and 200.")
    log_returns, dates = _observed_returns(history)
    if len(log_returns) < training_min + 20:
        raise ValueError("At least 20 evaluation returns are required beyond the training window.")
    returns = np.expm1(log_returns)
    first = max(training_min, len(returns) - max_evaluation_periods)
    indices = np.arange(first, len(returns), dtype=int)
    cumulative = np.cumsum(returns, dtype=float)
    forecast = cumulative[indices - 1] / indices  # 100% past returns only
    actual = returns[indices]
    zero_baseline = np.zeros_like(actual)
    mae_model = float(np.mean(np.abs(forecast - actual)))
    mae_zero = float(np.mean(np.abs(zero_baseline - actual)))
    rmse_model = float(np.sqrt(np.mean((forecast - actual) ** 2)))
    rmse_zero = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(actual ** 2)))
    if not np.isfinite([mae_model, mae_zero, rmse_model, rmse_zero]).all():
        raise ValueError("Backtest errors must be finite.")
    rows = pd.DataFrame({
        "Observation Date": dates[indices],
        "Actual Period Return %": actual * 100.0,
        "Prior-Mean Forecast %": forecast * 100.0,
        "Flat-Price Baseline %": zero_baseline * 100.0,
        "Prior Observations Used": indices,
    })
    return {
        "test_observations": len(indices),
        "training_min": training_min,
        "mean_forecast_mae_pct_points": mae_model * 100,
        "flat_baseline_mae_pct_points": mae_zero * 100,
        "mean_forecast_rmse_pct_points": rmse_model * 100,
        "flat_baseline_rmse_pct_points": rmse_zero * 100,
        "mean_beats_flat_baseline_on_mae": bool(mae_model < mae_zero),
        "method": "Expanding-window one-step historical mean vs zero-return baseline; no look-ahead",
        "machine_learning_model": False,
    }, rows


========== FILE: forecast_panel.py ==========

"""Streamlit UI for transparent, on-demand investment research.

This module does not access networks, persist market uploads, or mutate the
business Dataset Library. Simulation results are hypothetical and uncalibrated.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

from forecast_engine import (
    backtest_expanding_mean,
    evaluate_cashflow_stress,
    simulate_historical_bootstrap,
)


def render_business_stress_test(
    initial: float, annual_flows: list[float], discount_rate_pct: float
) -> None:
    with st.expander("03 Â· Business scenario stress tests (not forecasts)", expanded=False):
        st.caption(
            "Vary the NET annual cash flows you entered above. Downside makes positive "
            "cash flows smaller and negative cash flows more negative; upside does the "
            "reverse. Initial investment and discount rate remain fixed. These are "
            "user-defined what-if scenarios, NOT predicted probabilities."
        )
        left, right = st.columns(2)
        downside = left.number_input(
            "Downside cash-flow stress (%)", min_value=0.0, max_value=100.0,
            value=20.0, step=5.0, key="ii_stress_downside"
        )
        upside = right.number_input(
            "Upside cash-flow improvement (%)", min_value=0.0, max_value=100.0,
            value=20.0, step=5.0, key="ii_stress_upside"
        )
        try:
            summary, audit = evaluate_cashflow_stress(
                initial, annual_flows, discount_rate_pct, downside, upside
            )
            st.dataframe(summary, width="stretch", hide_index=True)
            st.download_button(
                "Export three-scenario summary (CSV)",
                summary.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8-sig"),
                file_name="python_bi_business_stress_scenarios.csv",
                mime="text/csv", key="ii_stress_summary_export",
            )
            st.download_button(
                "Export annual scenario audit (CSV)",
                audit.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8-sig"),
                file_name="python_bi_business_stress_audit.csv",
                mime="text/csv", key="ii_stress_audit_export",
            )
            st.caption("No scenario is a guaranteed, expected, or AI-predicted return.")
        except (ValueError, TypeError, KeyError, OverflowError) as exc:
            st.warning(f"The scenario cannot be calculated: {exc}")


def render_market_research(history: pd.DataFrame, frequency: str, cadence_ok: bool) -> None:
    with st.expander("03 Â· Forecasting research â€” historical bootstrap and backtest", expanded=False):
        st.warning(
            "Experimental research only. Resampling past price returns is NOT a calibrated "
            "prediction, a market forecast, or an investment recommendation. The expanding-mean "
            "backtest is a simple statistical baseline, not a trained AI model."
        )
        if not cadence_ok:
            st.info(
                "The selected observation frequency does not match the dates. "
                "Correct the frequency before running annualization-dependent research."
            )
            return
        if len(history) - 1 < 60:
            st.info("At least 60 observed period returns are required for this research lab.")
            return
        left, middle, right = st.columns(3)
        horizon = int(left.number_input(
            "Future observation periods", min_value=1, max_value=60, value=12,
            step=1, key="ii_mc_horizon",
        ))
        paths = int(middle.selectbox(
            "Simulation paths", [500, 1000, 1500, 2000, 3000],
            index=2, key="ii_mc_paths",
        ))
        hypothetical_value = right.number_input(
            "Hypothetical starting value", min_value=0.01, max_value=1e12,
            value=100.0, step=10.0, key="ii_mc_initial",
        )
        seed = int(st.number_input(
            "Repeatable random seed", min_value=0, max_value=2147483647,
            value=42, step=1, key="ii_mc_seed",
        ))
        st.caption(
            f"Each period is one {frequency.lower()} observation, not necessarily one calendar "
            "day/week/month. Results assume IID reuse of uploaded historical returns, "
            "ignore dividend income, fees, taxes, currency changes and unobserved risks."
        )
        # Hash source AND controls: stale results never masquerade as new-input results.
        row_hash = pd.util.hash_pandas_object(
            history[["Date", "Price"]], index=False
        ).to_numpy(dtype="uint64").tobytes()
        options = (frequency, horizon, paths, float(hypothetical_value), seed)
        signature = hashlib.sha256(row_hash + repr(options).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
        if st.button("Run historical simulation and out-of-sample test", key="ii_mc_run"):
            try:
                simulation, paths_df = simulate_historical_bootstrap(
                    history, frequency, horizon, paths, hypothetical_value, seed
                )
                backtest, backtest_rows = backtest_expanding_mean(history)
                st.session_state["ii_mc_result"] = (
                    simulation, paths_df, backtest, backtest_rows
                )
                st.session_state["ii_mc_signature"] = signature
            except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError, KeyError) as exc:
                st.session_state.pop("ii_mc_result", None)
                st.session_state.pop("ii_mc_signature", None)
                st.error(f"Research cannot be calculated: {exc}")
        if st.session_state.get("ii_mc_signature") != signature:
            st.info("Run the research test to view results for the current source and settings.")
            return
        saved = st.session_state.get("ii_mc_result")
        if not saved:
            return
        simulation, paths_df, backtest, backtest_rows = saved
        st.markdown("#### Historical bootstrap: hypothetical terminal values")
        c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
        c1.metric("Simulation P05", f"{simulation['terminal_value_p05']:,.2f}")
        c2.metric("Simulation median", f"{simulation['terminal_value_median']:,.2f}")
        c3.metric("Simulation P95", f"{simulation['terminal_value_p95']:,.2f}")
        st.metric(
            "Simulated paths finishing below starting value",
            f"{simulation['simulated_share_finishing_below_start_pct']:.1f}%",
        )
        st.line_chart(paths_df.set_index("Future Observation"))
        st.caption(
            "P05â€“P95 describes only the middle 90% of these simulated paths; "
            "it is not a statistically validated 90% real-world prediction interval. "
            "Simulation shares are not actual future loss probabilities."
        )
        st.download_button(
            "Download simulation distribution (CSV)",
            paths_df.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8-sig"),
            file_name="python_bi_market_bootstrap.csv",
            mime="text/csv", key="ii_mc_paths_export",
        )
        st.markdown("#### Historical out-of-sample backtest")
        a, b = st.columns(2)
        a.metric("Past-only mean model MAE (percentage points)",
                 f"{backtest['mean_forecast_mae_pct_points']:.4f}")
        b.metric("Flat-price baseline MAE (percentage points)",
                 f"{backtest['flat_baseline_mae_pct_points']:.4f}")
        if not backtest["mean_beats_flat_baseline_on_mae"]:
            st.info("On this test window the simple model did not beat the flat-price baseline by MAE.")
        else:
            st.info("The simple model beat the flat-price baseline on this test window only; future results may differ.")
        st.caption(
            f"{backtest['test_observations']} evaluation observations, each forecast "
            "uses earlier returns only. No trading costs, slippage, dividends or model "
            "selection correction are included."
        )
        st.line_chart(
            backtest_rows.set_index("Observation Date")[[
                "Actual Period Return %", "Prior-Mean Forecast %", "Flat-Price Baseline %"
            ]]
        )
        with st.expander("Review out-of-sample observations"):
            st.dataframe(backtest_rows, width="stretch", hide_index=True)
        st.download_button(
            "Download backtest observations (CSV)",
            backtest_rows.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8-sig"),
            file_name="python_bi_market_backtest.csv",
            mime="text/csv", key="ii_mc_backtest_export",
        )
        st.download_button(
            "Download research assumptions and metrics (JSON)",
            json.dumps({"simulation": simulation, "backtest": backtest},
                       indent=2, allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8"),
            file_name="python_bi_forecasting_research.json",
            mime="application/json", key="ii_mc_json_export",
        )


========== FILE: full_dataset_engine.py ==========

"""Pure full-dataset Explorer operations for Python BI Studio.

Select minimal columns, search in fixed-size chunks and count *all* matching
records before applying a display limit. No changes are made to source data.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from time import perf_counter
from typing import Sequence

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE = 20_000
MAX_PREVIEW_ROWS = 1_000
MAX_EXPORT_ROWS = 50_000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ExplorerMeta:
    source_rows: int
    matched_rows: int
    shown_rows: int
    exported_rows: int
    export_truncated: bool
    scan_seconds: float
    preview_seconds: float
    total_seconds: float
    search_scope: str = "Entire active dataset"


def _valid_columns(source: pd.DataFrame, selected: Sequence[str]) -> list[str]:
    if not source.columns.is_unique:
        raise ValueError("Duplicate source column names must be resolved before exploration.")
    if not source.columns.map(lambda x: isinstance(x, str)).all():
        raise ValueError("Source column names must be text.")
    if not selected:
        raise ValueError("Select at least one column.")
    if len(set(selected)) != len(selected):
        raise ValueError("Selected columns must be unique.")
    if any(column not in source.columns for column in selected):
        raise ValueError("An unknown column was selected.")
    return list(selected)


def literal_search_mask(
    source: pd.DataFrame,
    columns: Sequence[str],
    term: str,
    *,
    chunk_size: int = DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Case-insensitive literal OR search across *all rows*, with bounded chunks."""
    checked = _valid_columns(source, columns)
    if not isinstance(term, str):
        raise ValueError("Search text must be a string.")
    if not 1 <= int(chunk_size) <= 100_000:
        raise ValueError("Chunk size must be between 1 and 100,000.")
    phrase = term.strip()
    count = len(source)
    if not phrase:
        return np.ones(count, dtype=bool)
    result = np.zeros(count, dtype=bool)
    for start in range(0, count, int(chunk_size)):
        end = min(start + int(chunk_size), count)
        chunk_match = np.zeros(end - start, dtype=bool)
        for column in checked:
            if chunk_match.all():
                break
            column_text = source[column].iloc[start:end].astype("string")
            found = column_text.str.contains(phrase, case=False, regex=False, na=False)
            chunk_match |= found.to_numpy(dtype=bool, na_value=False)
        result[start:end] = chunk_match
    return result


def build_filter_mask(
    source: pd.DataFrame,
    selected: Sequence[str],
    search_text: str = "",
    *,
    category_column: str | None = None,
    category_values: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    numeric_column: str | None = None,
    minimum: float | None = None,
    maximum: float | None = None,
    chunk_size: int = DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """Apply filters to *all records*, preserving duplicate row indices."""
    checked = _valid_columns(source, selected)
    mask = literal_search_mask(source, checked, search_text, chunk_size=chunk_size)
    if category_column is not None:
        if category_column not in checked:
            raise ValueError("Category field must be a selected column.")
        if category_values is None:
            raise ValueError("Category values must be provided.")
        if len(category_values) == 0:
            return np.zeros(len(source), dtype=bool)
        options = list(category_values)
        mask &= source[category_column].astype("string").isin(options).to_numpy(dtype=bool)
    if numeric_column is not None:
        if numeric_column not in checked:
            raise ValueError("Numeric field must be a selected column.")
        if minimum is None or maximum is None:
            raise ValueError("Both numeric range limits are required.")
        lower, upper = float(minimum), float(maximum)
        if not (np.isfinite(lower) and np.isfinite(upper) and lower <= upper):
            raise ValueError("Numeric bounds must be finite and ascending.")
        values = pd.to_numeric(source[numeric_column], errors="coerce")
        mask &= values.between(lower, upper).fillna(False).to_numpy(dtype=bool)
    return mask


def explore_dataset(
    source: pd.DataFrame,
    selected: Sequence[str],
    *,
    search_text: str = "",
    category_column: str | None = None,
    category_values: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    numeric_column: str | None = None,
    minimum: float | None = None,
    maximum: float | None = None,
    sort_column: str | None = None,
    ascending: bool = True,
    preview_limit: int = 100,
    export_limit: int = MAX_EXPORT_ROWS,
    chunk_size: int = DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, ExplorerMeta]:
    """Return small preview and capped export, with accurate full-data match count.

    Sorting uses a stable full-result order but only selected columns are copied.
    The export is unambiguously capped, never presented as full when truncated.
    """
    checked = _valid_columns(source, selected)
    if not 1 <= int(preview_limit) <= MAX_PREVIEW_ROWS:
        raise ValueError("Preview must contain 1â€“1,000 records.")
    if not 1 <= int(export_limit) <= MAX_EXPORT_ROWS:
        raise ValueError("Export must contain 1â€“50,000 records.")
    if sort_column is not None and sort_column not in checked:
        raise ValueError("Sort field must be a selected column.")
    start = perf_counter()
    mask = build_filter_mask(
        source, checked, search_text,
        category_column=category_column, category_values=category_values,
        numeric_column=numeric_column, minimum=minimum, maximum=maximum,
        chunk_size=chunk_size,
    )
    scan_seconds = perf_counter() - start
    positions = np.flatnonzero(mask)
    matched = len(positions)
    if sort_column is not None and matched:
        # Sorting the whole selected result is deliberate and may cost memory.
        selected_sort = source[sort_column].iloc[positions].reset_index(drop=True)
        order = selected_sort.sort_values(ascending=bool(ascending), kind="stable", na_position="last").index.to_numpy()
        positions = positions[order]
    preview_positions = positions[:int(preview_limit)]
    export_positions = positions[:int(export_limit)]
    preview = source.iloc[preview_positions][checked].reset_index(drop=True).copy()
    export = source.iloc[export_positions][checked].reset_index(drop=True).copy()
    elapsed = perf_counter() - start
    meta = ExplorerMeta(
        source_rows=len(source), matched_rows=matched, shown_rows=len(preview),
        exported_rows=len(export), export_truncated=matched > len(export),
        scan_seconds=round(scan_seconds, 4),
        preview_seconds=round(elapsed - scan_seconds, 4),
        total_seconds=round(elapsed, 4),
    )
    return preview, export, meta


def safe_preview(frame: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Display-safe conversion applies to bounded preview ONLY."""
    preview = frame.copy()
    for column in preview.select_dtypes(include=["object"]).columns:
        preview[column] = preview[column].astype("string").fillna("")
    return preview

========== FILE: investment_engine.py ==========

"""Python BI Studio | investment mathematics and strictly descriptive market analytics.

No Streamlit dependency, no live prices, and no guaranteed-return claims.
All project cash flows are incremental net cash flows entered by the user.
All market prices are historical series supplied by the user; no dividends,
fees, taxes, splits, inflation adjustments, or currency translation inferred.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Sequence
from math import isfinite, sqrt

import pandas as pd


FREQUENCY_PERIODS = {"Daily": 252, "Weekly": 52, "Monthly": 12}


def _finite_number(value, label: str) -> float:
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValueError(f"{label} must be a finite number, not a checkbox value.")
    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{label} must be numeric.") from exc
    if not isfinite(result):
        raise ValueError(f"{label} must be finite and cannot be missing.")
    return result


def _npv(initial: float, cashflows: list[float], rate: float) -> float:
    base = 1.0 + rate
    if base <= 0:
        raise ValueError("The discount rate must be above -100%.")
    return -initial + sum(amount / (base ** year) for year, amount in enumerate(cashflows, 1))


def _irr(initial: float, cashflows: list[float]) -> tuple[float | None, str]:
    # Multiple sign changes may allow multiple IRRs. Do not show an arbitrary root.
    series = [-initial] + cashflows
    nonzero = [v for v in series if v != 0]
    changes = sum((a > 0) != (b > 0) for a, b in zip(nonzero, nonzero[1:]))
    if changes == 0:
        return None, "No sign change; an IRR is not defined for these cash flows."
    if changes > 1:
        return None, "Non-conventional cash flows may have multiple IRRs; IRR is omitted."

    lo, hi = -0.999, 1.0
    f_lo = _npv(initial, cashflows, lo)
    f_hi = _npv(initial, cashflows, hi)
    while f_lo * f_hi > 0 and hi < 1_000_000:
        hi = hi * 2 + 1
        f_hi = _npv(initial, cashflows, hi)
    if f_lo * f_hi > 0:
        return None, "An IRR root could not be reliably bracketed."
    for _ in range(120):
        mid = (lo + hi) / 2
        f_mid = _npv(initial, cashflows, mid)
        if abs(f_mid) < 1e-9 or hi - lo < 1e-12:
            return mid * 100.0, "One conventional IRR root was found."
        if (f_mid > 0) == (f_lo > 0):
            lo, f_lo = mid, f_mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return (lo + hi) / 2 * 100.0, "One conventional IRR root was found."


def _payback(initial: float, amounts: Sequence[float]) -> float | None:
    cumulative = -initial
    tolerance = 1e-10 * max(1.0, abs(initial))
    for year, amount in enumerate(amounts, start=1):
        before = cumulative
        cumulative += amount
        if before < -tolerance and cumulative >= -tolerance and amount > 0:
            return year - 1 + min(1.0, max(0.0, -before / amount))
    return None


def evaluate_project(
    initial_investment: float,
    annual_net_cash_flows: Sequence[float],
    discount_rate_pct: float,
) -> tuple[dict, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Evaluate an explicit scenario, not predict future outcomes.

    year-zero cash flow is -initial_investment; subsequent values are *net*
    incremental annual cash flows, including any terminal value in the final year.
    """
    initial = _finite_number(initial_investment, "Initial investment")
    rate_pct = _finite_number(discount_rate_pct, "Discount rate")
    if initial <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Initial investment must be greater than zero.")
    if not -99 < rate_pct <= 1000:
        raise ValueError("The discount rate must be above -99% and at most 1,000%.")
    if isinstance(annual_net_cash_flows, (str, bytes)) or not isinstance(annual_net_cash_flows, Sequence):
        raise ValueError("Provide a sequence of annual net cash flows.")
    if not 1 <= len(annual_net_cash_flows) <= 40:
        raise ValueError("Provide between one and 40 annual net cash flows.")
    flows = [_finite_number(v, f"Year {i} cash flow") for i, v in enumerate(annual_net_cash_flows, 1)]
    rate = rate_pct / 100.0
    discounted = [v / ((1 + rate) ** i) for i, v in enumerate(flows, 1)]
    cumulative = -initial
    cumulative_discounted = -initial
    records = []
    for year, (cf, discounted_cf) in enumerate(zip(flows, discounted), 1):
        cumulative += cf
        cumulative_discounted += discounted_cf
        records.append({
            "Year": year,
            "Incremental Net Cash Flow": cf,
            "Discounted Cash Flow": discounted_cf,
            "Cumulative Cash Flow": cumulative,
            "Cumulative Discounted Cash Flow": cumulative_discounted,
        })
    irr_pct, irr_note = _irr(initial, flows)
    summary = {
        "initial_investment": initial,
        "discount_rate_pct": rate_pct,
        "years": len(flows),
        "sum_of_net_cash_flows": float(sum(flows)),
        "npv": float(_npv(initial, flows, rate)),
        "undiscounted_scenario_roi_pct": (sum(flows) - initial) / initial * 100,
        "simple_payback_years": _payback(initial, flows),
        "discounted_payback_years": _payback(initial, discounted),
        "irr_pct": irr_pct,
        "irr_note": irr_note,
        "scenario_only": True,
    }
    return summary, pd.DataFrame.from_records(records)


def summarize_business_evidence(data: pd.DataFrame, revenue_col: str, cost_col: str | None = None) -> dict:
    """Read company dataset columns without assuming these cover all expenses."""
    if not isinstance(data, pd.DataFrame) or data.empty:
        raise ValueError("Activate a non-empty business dataset first.")
    if revenue_col not in data.columns:
        raise ValueError("Select an available revenue column.")
    if cost_col and (cost_col not in data.columns or cost_col == revenue_col):
        raise ValueError("Select a separate, available mapped-cost column.")
    out = {"records": len(data), "revenue_field": revenue_col, "cost_field": cost_col}
    for label, column in [("observed_revenue", revenue_col), ("observed_mapped_cost", cost_col)]:
        if column is None:
            continue
        series = pd.to_numeric(data[column], errors="coerce")
        invalid = series.isna() | ~series.map(lambda x: bool(pd.notna(x)) and isfinite(float(x)))
        if invalid.any():
            raise ValueError(f"Column '{column}' contains {int(invalid.sum())} missing, invalid or non-finite values.")
        out[label] = float(series.sum())
    if cost_col:
        out["profit_after_mapped_cost"] = out["observed_revenue"] - out["observed_mapped_cost"]
    return out


def evaluate_market_history(
    data: pd.DataFrame,
    date_col: str,
    price_col: str,
    frequency: str = "Daily",
    annual_risk_free_pct: float = 0.0,
) -> tuple[dict, pd.DataFrame]:
    """Descriptive *price-only* risk/return analysis on an uploaded series.

    No adjustment is inferred for dividends, splits, fees, taxes or currency.
    Annualized vol/Sharpe require >=30 observed returns and plausible date cadence.
    """
    if frequency not in FREQUENCY_PERIODS:
        raise ValueError("Frequency must be Daily, Weekly, or Monthly.")
    rf_pct = _finite_number(annual_risk_free_pct, "Annual risk-free rate")
    if not -99 < rf_pct <= 100:
        raise ValueError("Annual risk-free rate must be above -99% and at most 100%.")
    if not isinstance(data, pd.DataFrame) or data.empty:
        raise ValueError("Upload a non-empty historical price dataset.")
    if date_col == price_col or date_col not in data.columns or price_col not in data.columns:
        raise ValueError("Choose distinct date and price columns.")
    if len(data) > 100_000:
        raise ValueError("The historical analysis is limited to 100,000 source rows.")

    dates = pd.to_datetime(data[date_col], errors="coerce", format="mixed", utc=True)
    prices = pd.to_numeric(data[price_col], errors="coerce")
    bad_date = dates.isna()
    bad_price = prices.isna() | ~prices.map(lambda x: bool(pd.notna(x)) and isfinite(float(x))) | (prices <= 0)
    if bad_date.any() or bad_price.any():
        raise ValueError(
            f"Invalid rows: {int(bad_date.sum())} with missing/unparseable dates and "
            f"{int(bad_price.sum())} with missing, non-positive or invalid prices. "
            "Correct the source data before proceeding."
        )
    if dates.duplicated().any():
        raise ValueError(f"{int(dates.duplicated().sum())} duplicate timestamps found. Resolve duplicates before analysing returns.")

    series = pd.DataFrame({"Date": dates, "Price": prices.astype(float)}, index=data.index)
    series = series.sort_values("Date").reset_index(drop=True)
    if len(series) < 2:
        raise ValueError("At least two distinct dated price records are required.")
    gaps = series["Date"].diff().dt.total_seconds().iloc[1:] / 86400.0
    median_gap = float(gaps.median())
    acceptable = {
        "Daily": (0.5, 4.5),
        "Weekly": (4.5, 11.5),
        "Monthly": (24, 36),
    }
    low, high = acceptable[frequency]
    cadence_ok = low <= median_gap <= high
    series["Period Return %"] = series["Price"].pct_change(fill_method=None) * 100
    series["Drawdown %"] = (series["Price"] / series["Price"].cummax() - 1) * 100

    first, last = float(series["Price"].iloc[0]), float(series["Price"].iloc[-1])
    start_date, end_date = series["Date"].iloc[0], series["Date"].iloc[-1]
    days = (end_date - start_date).total_seconds() / 86400.0
    returns = series["Period Return %"].dropna() / 100.0
    periods = FREQUENCY_PERIODS[frequency]
    vol = sharpe = None
    if len(returns) >= 30 and cadence_ok:
        volatility = float(returns.std(ddof=1))
        vol = volatility * sqrt(periods) * 100
        periodic_rf = (1 + rf_pct / 100.0) ** (1 / periods) - 1
        if volatility > 0:
            sharpe = float((returns.mean() - periodic_rf) / volatility * sqrt(periods))
    summary = {
        "records": len(series),
        "start_date": str(start_date.date()),
        "end_date": str(end_date.date()),
        "first_price": first,
        "last_price": last,
        "price_return_pct": (last / first - 1) * 100,
        "max_observed_drawdown_pct": float(series["Drawdown %"].min()),
        "annualized_price_cagr_pct": ((last / first) ** (365.25 / days) - 1) * 100 if days >= 90 else None,
        "annualized_volatility_pct": vol,
        "annualized_sharpe": sharpe,
        "observed_return_count": len(returns),
        "observation_frequency": frequency,
        "median_gap_days": median_gap,
        "frequency_consistent": cadence_ok,
        "annual_risk_free_pct": rf_pct,
        "price_only": True,
    }
    return summary, series


========== FILE: investment_workspace.py ==========

"""Python BI Studio â€” separately scoped investment analysis workspaces.

Business mode draws optional historical evidence from the current business dataset,
but all forward cash flows are provided or edited explicitly by the user.
Markets mode analyses a separate temporary historical file; it never changes
st.session_state['uploaded_df'] or the permanent Dataset Library.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import json

import pandas as pd
import streamlit as st

from forecast_panel import render_business_stress_test, render_market_research

from investment_engine import (
    evaluate_market_history,
    evaluate_project,
    summarize_business_evidence,
)


MISSING_FIELD = "â€” Not mapped â€”"


def _metric_value(value: float | None, suffix: str = "", decimals: int = 2) -> str:
    return f"{value:,.{decimals}f}{suffix}" if value is not None else "Not available"


def _business_workspace(active_data: pd.DataFrame | None, active_name: str) -> None:
    st.subheader("Business Investment Decisions")
    st.caption("Historical evidence and forward-looking project scenarios are kept separate. No returns are guaranteed.")

    with st.expander("01 Â· Business dataset evidence", expanded=True):
        if not isinstance(active_data, pd.DataFrame) or active_data.empty:
            st.info("To inspect historical business evidence, activate a dataset in Data Workspace first. You can still model a standalone project scenario below.")
        else:
            st.caption(f"Active source: {active_name} Â· {len(active_data):,} records. These are not assumed to be annual cash flows.")
            numeric_fields = active_data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
            if not numeric_fields:
                st.warning("No numeric fields detected. Prepare numeric columns in Data Workspace first.")
            else:
                default_revenue = next((i for i, name in enumerate(numeric_fields) if name.lower().strip() in ("sales", "revenue", "gross revenue")), 0)
                revenue_col = st.selectbox("Observed revenue field", numeric_fields, index=default_revenue, key="ii_evidence_revenue")
                cost_options = [MISSING_FIELD] + numeric_fields
                default_cost = next((i for i, name in enumerate(cost_options) if name.lower().strip() in ("total cost", "cost", "cogs")), 0)
                cost_col = st.selectbox("Mapped cost field (optional)", cost_options, index=default_cost, key="ii_evidence_cost")
                try:
                    evidence = summarize_business_evidence(active_data, revenue_col, None if cost_col == MISSING_FIELD else cost_col)
                    c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
                    c1.metric("Observed revenue", _metric_value(evidence["observed_revenue"]))
                    c2.metric("Mapped costs", _metric_value(evidence.get("observed_mapped_cost")))
                    c3.metric("Profit after mapped costs", _metric_value(evidence.get("profit_after_mapped_cost")))
                    st.caption("These are aggregates across the current datasetâ€”not necessarily net profit, free cash flow, annual results, or an investment recommendation.")
                except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
                    st.error(str(exc))

    with st.expander("02 Â· Evaluate an investment cash-flow scenario", expanded=True):
        st.caption("Enter an initial outlay and incremental NET annual cash flows. Values are illustrative until you enter your own assumptions. Use one consistent currency/unit; no conversion is performed.")
        left, mid, right = st.columns(3)
        initial = left.number_input("Initial investment (year 0)", min_value=0.01, value=100000.0, step=1000.0, key="ii_initial")
        discount = mid.number_input("Annual discount rate (%)", min_value=0.0, max_value=100.0, value=10.0, step=0.5, key="ii_discount")
        years = int(right.number_input("Forecast years", min_value=1, max_value=15, value=5, step=1, key="ii_years"))
        template = pd.DataFrame({
            "Year": range(1, years + 1),
            "Incremental Net Cash Flow": [0.0] * years,
        })
        edited = st.data_editor(
            template,
            disabled=["Year"],
            num_rows="fixed",
            hide_index=True,
            width="stretch",
            key=f"ii_cashflow_editor_{years}",
            column_config={
                "Incremental Net Cash Flow": st.column_config.NumberColumn(
                    "Incremental Net Cash Flow", help="Enter estimated cash flow received (+) or paid (-) in each year. Include terminal value in the last year if applicable.", format="%.2f"
                )
            },
        )
        st.caption("Cash flows start at the end of Year 1. Include salvage value and additional costs explicitly in the relevant year. Do not use sales revenue as net project cash flow without adjusting for all costs.")
        if st.button("Evaluate scenario", type="primary", key="ii_evaluate_business"):
            try:
                summary, period_rows = evaluate_project(
                    initial,
                    edited["Incremental Net Cash Flow"].tolist(),
                    discount,
                )
                k1, k2, k3 = st.columns(3)
                k1.metric("Scenario NPV", _metric_value(summary["npv"]))
                k2.metric("Scenario IRR", _metric_value(summary["irr_pct"], "%"))
                k3.metric("Simple payback (years)", _metric_value(summary["simple_payback_years"]))
                k4, k5 = st.columns(2)
                k4.metric("Discounted payback (years)", _metric_value(summary["discounted_payback_years"]))
                k5.metric("Undiscounted scenario ROI", _metric_value(summary["undiscounted_scenario_roi_pct"], "%"))
                st.caption(summary["irr_note"])
                st.dataframe(period_rows, width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                st.download_button(
                    "Download scenario audit (CSV)",
                    period_rows.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8-sig"),
                    file_name="python_bi_investment_scenario.csv",
                    mime="text/csv",
                    key="ii_business_export",
                )
                st.download_button(
                    "Download scenario assumptions and summary (JSON)",
                    json.dumps(summary, indent=2, allow_nan=False).encode("utf-8"),
                    file_name="python_bi_investment_summary.json",
                    mime="application/json",
                    key="ii_business_summary_export",
                )
            except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError, KeyError) as exc:
                st.error(f"Cannot evaluate this scenario: {exc}")

    render_business_stress_test(initial, edited["Incremental Net Cash Flow"].tolist(), discount)


def _market_workspace() -> None:
    st.subheader("Financial-Market Historical Analysis")
    st.caption("Import your own historical market CSV/XLSX. This file is analysed temporarily and is NOT stored in the business Dataset Library.")
    uploaded = st.file_uploader(
        "Upload historical market prices (CSV or XLSX)",
        type=["csv", "xlsx"],
        key="ii_market_upload",
    )
    if uploaded is None:
        st.info("Upload a file with one date/timestamp column and one numeric, strictly positive price column. A synthetic example is provided with the update package.")
        return
    if uploaded.size > 25 * 1024 * 1024:
        st.error("File exceeds the 25 MiB market-analysis limit. Reduce the input file size.")
        return
    try:
        data = pd.read_csv(io.BytesIO(uploaded.getvalue())) if uploaded.name.lower().endswith(".csv") else pd.read_excel(io.BytesIO(uploaded.getvalue()))
    except (ValueError, OSError, ImportError, UnicodeError) as exc:
        st.error(f"Could not read market file: {exc}")
        return
    if data.empty or len(data.columns) < 2:
        st.error("The market file needs at least two columns and at least two dated records.")
        return
    st.caption(f"Temporary file: {uploaded.name} Â· {len(data):,} rows Â· {len(data.columns)} columns")
    colnames = [str(c) for c in data.columns]
    if len(set(colnames)) != len(colnames):
        st.error("Market file has duplicate column names. Rename those columns first.")
        return
    c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
    default_date = next((i for i, c in enumerate(colnames) if "date" in c.lower() or "time" in c.lower()), 0)
    default_price = next((i for i, c in enumerate(colnames) if c.lower().strip() in ("adj close", "adjusted close", "close", "price")), min(1, len(colnames) - 1))
    date_col = c1.selectbox("Date field", colnames, index=default_date, key="ii_market_date")
    price_col = c2.selectbox("Price field", colnames, index=default_price, key="ii_market_price")
    freq = c3.selectbox("Observation frequency", ["Daily", "Weekly", "Monthly"], key="ii_market_frequency")
    rf = st.number_input("Assumed annual risk-free rate (%)", min_value=-50.0, max_value=100.0, value=0.0, step=0.25, key="ii_market_rf")
    try:
        summary, history = evaluate_market_history(data, date_col, price_col, freq, rf)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
        st.error(str(exc))
        return

    a, b, c = st.columns(3)
    a.metric("Historical price return", _metric_value(summary["price_return_pct"], "%"))
    b.metric("Maximum observed drawdown", _metric_value(summary["max_observed_drawdown_pct"], "%"))
    c.metric("Annualized price CAGR", _metric_value(summary["annualized_price_cagr_pct"], "%"))
    d, e = st.columns(2)
    d.metric("Annualized volatility", _metric_value(summary["annualized_volatility_pct"], "%"))
    e.metric("Historical Sharpe ratio", _metric_value(summary["annualized_sharpe"]))
    st.line_chart(history.set_index("Date")[["Price"]])
    if not summary["frequency_consistent"]:
        st.warning("The date spacing does not match the selected frequency. Annualized volatility and Sharpe are suppressed.")
    if summary["observed_return_count"] < 30:
        st.info("At least 30 observed returns are required before volatility and Sharpe are displayed.")
    if summary["annualized_price_cagr_pct"] is None:
        st.info("CAGR is omitted for histories shorter than 90 calendar days.")
    st.caption("Price-only observations. Corporate actions, dividends, splits, expenses, fees, taxes, market holidays and currency changes are NOT inferred. No live pricing or investment recommendations are provided.")
    with st.expander("Historical return and drawdown audit"):
        st.dataframe(history.head(500), width="stretch", hide_index=True)
        st.download_button(
            "Download analysed historical prices (CSV)",
            history.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8-sig"),
            file_name="python_bi_market_history_analysis.csv",
            mime="text/csv",
            key="ii_market_export",
        )

    render_market_research(history, freq, summary["frequency_consistent"])


def render_investment_intelligence(active_business_df: pd.DataFrame | None, active_name: str = "No active business dataset") -> None:
    st.header("Investment Intelligence")
    st.caption("Evidence-led decision support with two independent modes. All estimates depend on supplied data and assumptions; no returns are promised.")
    mode = st.radio(
        "Analysis mode",
        ["Business Investment Decisions", "Financial Markets"],
        horizontal=True,
        key="ii_mode",
    )
    if mode == "Business Investment Decisions":
        _business_workspace(active_business_df, active_name)
    else:
        _market_workspace()


========== FILE: local_file_guard.py ==========

"""Local-only file safety helpers. No authentication or cloud operations.

Guards reduce accidental / malformed inputs; they are NOT malware scanning,
secure multi-tenant storage, or certification for public deployment.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from io import BytesIO
import hashlib
import re
import zipfile

MAX_UPLOAD_MIB = 96
MAX_ARCHIVE_MIB = 512
MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES = 20_000
_STORED = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9_.-]{0,127}\.(csv|xlsx)$", re.I)


def safe_stored_path(folder: Path, filename: str, *, must_exist: bool = False) -> Path:
    """Prevent metadata traversal, unwanted extensions and symlink-file reads."""
    if not isinstance(filename, str) or not _STORED.fullmatch(filename) or '..' in filename:
        raise ValueError("Invalid stored dataset filename.")
    folder = Path(folder)
    if folder.is_symlink():
        raise ValueError("Dataset Library folder cannot be a symbolic link.")
    base = folder.resolve()
    target = base / filename
    if target.is_symlink() or target.resolve().parent != base:
        raise ValueError("Dataset path is outside its library.")
    if must_exist and not target.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError("Saved dataset is missing.")
    return target


def validate_upload(upload, max_mib: int = MAX_UPLOAD_MIB) -> dict:
    """Check extension, byte limit and XLSX structure before calling Pandas."""
    if not isinstance(max_mib, int) or max_mib <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Maximum upload size must be positive.")
    name = str(getattr(upload, 'name', ''))
    if not name or Path(name).name != name or '\\' in name or '/' in name:
        raise ValueError("Upload name is invalid.")
    suffix = Path(name).suffix.lower()
    if suffix not in ('.csv', '.xlsx'):
        raise ValueError("Only CSV and XLSX datasets are supported.")
    maximum = max_mib * 1024 * 1024
    indicated = getattr(upload, 'size', None)
    if indicated is not None and indicated > maximum:
        raise ValueError(f"File exceeds the {max_mib} MiB local processing limit.")
    raw = upload.getvalue()
    if not isinstance(raw, bytes) or not raw:
        raise ValueError("Dataset is empty or cannot be read.")
    if len(raw) > maximum:
        raise ValueError(f"File exceeds the {max_mib} MiB local processing limit.")
    if suffix == '.csv':
        if raw.startswith(b'PK\x03\x04') or b'\x00' in raw[:65536]:
            raise ValueError("CSV appears to be an archive or binary file.")
    else:
        if not zipfile.is_zipfile(BytesIO(raw)):
            raise ValueError("XLSX upload is not a valid Excel archive.")
        total = 0
        members = set()
        with zipfile.ZipFile(BytesIO(raw)) as zf:
            infos = zf.infolist()
            if len(infos) > MAX_ARCHIVE_ENTRIES:
                raise ValueError("Workbook contains too many archive entries.")
            for item in infos:
                member = item.filename.replace('\\', '/')
                if member.startswith('/') or '..' in member.split('/'):
                    raise ValueError("Unsafe workbook archive entry.")
                if item.flag_bits & 1:
                    raise ValueError("Encrypted workbook archives are unsupported.")
                total += item.file_size
                if total > MAX_ARCHIVE_MIB * 1024 * 1024:
                    raise ValueError("Workbook expands beyond the local processing limit.")
                members.add(member)
        if '[Content_Types].xml' not in members or 'xl/workbook.xml' not in members:
            raise ValueError("Workbook is missing required Excel components.")
    return {"name": name, "extension": suffix, "size_bytes": len(raw),
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()}


========== FILE: local_recovery_audit.py ==========

"""Read-only review of the locally stored active dataset; no data leaves the PC."""
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path
from local_file_guard import safe_stored_path


def audit_dataset_library(project_root: Path) -> dict:
    root = Path(project_root)
    folder = root / 'stored_datasets'
    active = folder / 'active_dataset.json'
    result = {"scope": "local single-user only", "dataset_library_present": folder.is_dir(),
              "active_metadata_present": active.is_file(), "saved_file_count": 0,
              "active_integrity": "NOT_CHECKED", "messages": []}
    if folder.is_symlink():
        result['active_integrity'] = 'UNSAFE_LIBRARY_PATH'
        return result
    if not folder.is_dir():
        result['messages'].append('Dataset Library not created yet.')
        return result
    for path in folder.iterdir():
        if path.is_file() and not path.is_symlink() and path.suffix.lower() in ('.csv', '.xlsx'):
            try:
                safe_stored_path(folder, path.name)
                result['saved_file_count'] += 1
            except ValueError:
                pass
    if not active.exists():
        result['messages'].append('No active dataset metadata is present.')
        return result
    if active.is_symlink():
        result['active_integrity'] = 'UNSAFE_ACTIVE_METADATA'
        return result
    try:
        metadata = json.loads(active.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        target = safe_stored_path(folder, metadata['stored_name'], must_exist=True)
        expected = metadata.get('hash')
        if expected:
            result['active_integrity'] = ('PASS' if hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == expected
                                          else 'HASH_MISMATCH')
        else:
            result['active_integrity'] = 'LEGACY_NO_CHECKSUM'
    except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, json.JSONDecodeError):
        result['active_integrity'] = 'REVIEW_REQUIRED'
    return result


========== FILE: local_review_cli.py ==========

"""Generate local-only, data-minimised academic verification evidence.

Does not publish the app, upload any records, or alter Dataset Library state.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
from professor_review import review_packet, review_summary
from local_recovery_audit import audit_dataset_library
from sector_acceptance import run_sector_acceptance
from local_workload_benchmark import run_workload


def build_local_evidence(root: Path, *, rows: int=10_000) -> dict:
    sectors=run_sector_acceptance()
    report={
        'project': 'Python BI Studio',
        'hosting': 'PRIVATE_LOCAL_ONLY',
        'professor_approval': 'PENDING',
        'project_phase_status': review_summary()['phase_status'],
        'sector_checks': sectors,
        'dataset_library_audit': audit_dataset_library(root),
        'performance': run_workload(rows),
        'browser_review': 'MANUAL â€” not automatically certified',
        'public_deployment': 'NOT_REQUESTED_NOT_AUTHORIZED',
        'financial_assumptions': 'Profit called net only when all expenses are explicitly mapped and user confirms completeness.',
        'scope': 'Does not expose original business data, workbook paths or transaction values',
    }
    return report


def main(argv=None):
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('--rows',type=int,default=10000)
    ap.add_argument('--output-dir',default='professor_results')
    args=ap.parse_args(argv)
    base=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    report=build_local_evidence(base,rows=args.rows)
    target=base/args.output_dir
    # Reports should remain in the local workspace; prevent accidental traversal.
    if target.resolve().parent!=base.resolve():
        raise ValueError('Output folder must be directly inside this local project.')
    target.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (target/'LOCAL_ACADEMIC_READINESS.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    (target/'PROFESSOR_REVIEW_PACKET.zip').write_bytes(review_packet())
    print('PASS: Three synthetic sectors reconciled; workload completed.')
    print('Review materials created in:',target.name)
    print('Professor approval: PENDING; public hosting: NOT AUTHORIZED.')
    return 0
if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())


========== FILE: local_workload_benchmark.py ==========

"""Measured local Python BI module workflow, including chart preparation and report construction.

This is NOT a full browser benchmark. Real Streamlit interactions still need a manual test.
No user files, Dataset Library, network, or public server are accessed.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
from time import perf_counter
import math
from benchmark_explorer import make_synthetic
from full_dataset_engine import explore_dataset
from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics
from business_metrics import apply_dashboard_filters
from quality_engine import quality_overview
from portfolio_analytics import build_category_concentration
from scalable_analytics import category_chart_data, time_chart_data
from report_engine import build_executive_report
from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv


def run_workload(rows: int = 10_000) -> dict:
    if type(rows) is not int or not 100 <= rows <= 500_000:
        raise ValueError('Use 100 to 500,000 synthetic rows.')
    steps={}
    start=perf_counter(); source=make_synthetic(rows)
    steps['source_generation_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    mem=round(float(source.memory_usage(index=True, deep=True).sum())/1048576,2)
    sentinel=f'SPECIAL-ROW-{rows-1:09d}'
    start=perf_counter(); preview,_,meta=explore_dataset(source,source.columns.tolist(),search_text=sentinel,preview_limit=10)
    steps['full_dataset_search_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    assert meta.matched_rows==1 and len(preview)==1
    start=perf_counter(); health=quality_overview(source)
    steps['quality_scan_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    assert health['rows']==rows
    start=perf_counter(); filtered=apply_dashboard_filters(source,numeric_column='Sales',numeric_min=0,numeric_max=1e15)
    steps['dashboard_filter_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    start=perf_counter(); audit, totals=calculate_commercial_metrics(filtered,'Sales','Total Cost')
    steps['financial_calculations_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    assert math.isclose(totals['profit_after_mapped_costs'],totals['net_revenue']-totals['mapped_costs'],abs_tol=.01)
    start=perf_counter(); concentration=build_category_concentration(filtered,audit,totals,'Product')
    steps['category_concentration_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    assert math.isclose(concentration['groups']['Net Revenue'].sum(),totals['net_revenue'],abs_tol=.01)
    start=perf_counter(); bar,_=category_chart_data(filtered,audit,'Product','Calculated: Net Revenue')
    line,_=time_chart_data(filtered,audit,'Date','Calculated: Net Revenue','Monthly')
    steps['chart_series_preparation_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    assert math.isclose(bar['Value'].sum(),totals['net_revenue'],abs_tol=.01)
    assert math.isclose(line['Value'].sum(),totals['net_revenue'],abs_tol=.01)
    start=perf_counter(); report=build_executive_report(
        filtered,audit,totals,dataset_name='Synthetic performance case',
        category='Product',date_column='Date',period='Monthly',
        field_mapping={'Revenue':'Sales','Cost':'Total Cost'},filter_notes='Synthetic, unfiltered')
    steps['executive_html_generation_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    assert len(report['html'])>500
    start=perf_counter(); output=excel_safe_csv(audit.head(200))
    steps['bounded_csv_export_sec']=round(perf_counter()-start,4)
    assert output.startswith(b'\xef\xbb\xbf')
    return {'rows':rows,'columns':source.shape[1],'source_memory_mib':mem,
            'steps_sec':steps,'all_module_reconciliations':True,
            'includes_html_report_generation':True,
            'includes_chart_series_but_not_browser_render':True,
            'streamlit_full_ui_certified':False,
            'scope':'Local synthetic full module-chain performance; NOT a Streamlit user-interface load test',
            'status':'PASS'}


def main(argv=None):
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    p.add_argument('--rows',type=int,default=10000)
    p.add_argument('--output',default='professor_results/workload.json')
    args=p.parse_args(argv)
    result=run_workload(args.rows)
    dest=Path(args.output); dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    dest.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print('PASS: Local module-chain workload', args.rows,'rows; chart data and HTML report included.')
    print('Browser rendering and public hosting: NOT PERFORMED.')
    return 0

if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
