========== FILE: performance_insights.py ==========

"""Pure business-performance and planning functions with auditable assumptions."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
from business_metrics import break_even_scenario


def category_profitability(
    source: pd.DataFrame,
    calculations: pd.DataFrame,
    category: str,
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Calculate reconciled revenue and profit by category on identical rows.

    Missing categories are grouped as '(Missing)'. A zero net revenue has no
    defined profit margin; negative revenue is reported as such without hiding it.
    """
    if len(source) != len(calculations):
        raise ValueError("Source/calculation record count mismatch.")
    if category not in source.columns:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown category column: {category}")
    needed = {"Net Revenue", "Mapped Costs", "Profit After Mapped Costs"}
    if not needed.issubset(calculations.columns):
        raise ValueError("Map cost-of-sales before calculating category profitability.")
    frame = pd.DataFrame({
        "Category": source[category].reset_index(drop=True).astype("string").fillna("(Missing)"),
        "Net Revenue": pd.to_numeric(calculations["Net Revenue"].reset_index(drop=True), errors="raise"),
        "Mapped Costs": pd.to_numeric(calculations["Mapped Costs"].reset_index(drop=True), errors="raise"),
        "Profit After Mapped Costs": pd.to_numeric(
            calculations["Profit After Mapped Costs"].reset_index(drop=True), errors="raise"
        ),
    })
    grouped = frame.groupby("Category", dropna=False).agg(
        Records=("Net Revenue", "size"),
        **{
            "Net Revenue": ("Net Revenue", "sum"),
            "Mapped Costs": ("Mapped Costs", "sum"),
            "Profit After Mapped Costs": ("Profit After Mapped Costs", "sum"),
        },
    ).reset_index()
    grouped["Profit Margin %"] = (
        grouped["Profit After Mapped Costs"]
        .div(grouped["Net Revenue"].where(grouped["Net Revenue"].ne(0))) * 100
    )
    return grouped.sort_values("Net Revenue", ascending=False, kind="stable").reset_index(drop=True)


def loss_making_rows(source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Return only transactions whose profit after mapped costs is negative."""
    if len(source) != len(calculations):
        raise ValueError("Source/calculation record count mismatch.")
    if "Profit After Mapped Costs" not in calculations.columns:
        raise ValueError("Cost-of-sales mapping is required to identify loss-making rows.")
    from audit_exports import build_financial_export
    full = build_financial_export(source, calculations)
    return full.loc[calculations["Profit After Mapped Costs"].reset_index(drop=True).lt(0).to_numpy()].reset_index(drop=True)


def price_sensitivity(
    fixed_costs: float,
    selling_price_per_unit: float,
    variable_cost_per_unit: float,
    percent_changes: tuple[int, ...] = (-10, -5, 0, 5, 10),
) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Break-even across clearly labelled HYPOTHETICAL selling-price changes.

    Price and variable costs are per-unit. Fixed costs share the same period.
    These scenarios are not forecasts or predictions.
    """
    baseline = break_even_scenario(fixed_costs, selling_price_per_unit, variable_cost_per_unit)
    rows = []
    for variation in percent_changes:
        if not isinstance(variation, int) or variation < -100:
            raise ValueError("Price variations must be integer percentages >= -100.")
        hypothetical_price = baseline["unit_price"] * (1 + variation / 100)
        result = break_even_scenario(baseline["fixed_costs"], hypothetical_price,
                                     baseline["variable_cost_per_unit"])
        rows.append({
            "Price Change %": variation,
            "Hypothetical Unit Price": result["unit_price"],
            "Contribution Per Unit": result["contribution_per_unit"],
            "Break-even Units": result["break_even_units"],
            "Break-even Revenue": result["break_even_revenue"],
        })
    return pd.DataFrame(rows)


========== FILE: performance_lab.py ==========

"""An explicitly bounded, optional local performance diagnostic.

This tool is NOT a benchmark of full-source upload, Streamlit rendering, or
500k-record readiness. Only the selected, sampled grouping operation is timed.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from time import perf_counter

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

MAX_BENCHMARK_ROWS = 50_000


def benchmark_grouping(
    source: pd.DataFrame, category_column: str, numeric_column: str,
    sample_limit: int = 10_000,
) -> tuple[dict, pd.DataFrame]:
    if not 1 <= int(sample_limit) <= MAX_BENCHMARK_ROWS:
        raise ValueError(f"Benchmark sample must be between 1 and {MAX_BENCHMARK_ROWS:,} rows.")
    if not source.columns.is_unique:
        raise ValueError("The dataset contains ambiguous duplicate field names.")
    if category_column not in source.columns or numeric_column not in source.columns:
        raise ValueError("Select valid category and numeric source columns.")
    if category_column == numeric_column:
        raise ValueError("Choose different category and numeric columns.")
    if not pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(source[numeric_column]):
        raise ValueError("The measure must be a numeric column.")

    start = perf_counter()
    # Select just two columns first. The entire source is not copied for the test.
    two_columns = source[[category_column, numeric_column]]
    sample_n = min(len(two_columns), int(sample_limit))
    if sample_n < len(two_columns):
        sample = two_columns.sample(n=sample_n, random_state=42).reset_index(drop=True)
    else:
        sample = two_columns.reset_index(drop=True).copy()
    numeric = pd.to_numeric(sample[numeric_column], errors="coerce")
    values = numeric.to_numpy(dtype=float, na_value=np.nan)
    good = np.isfinite(values)
    working = pd.DataFrame({
        "Category": sample[category_column].astype("string").fillna("(Missing)"),
        "Value": numeric,
    }).loc[good]
    grouped = working.groupby("Category", observed=True)["Value"].agg(
        Total="sum", Records="size"
    ).reset_index().sort_values("Total", ascending=False, kind="stable")
    elapsed = (perf_counter() - start) * 1_000
    return {
        "source_rows": len(source),
        "sample_rows": sample_n,
        "excluded_nonnumeric": int((~good).sum()),
        "group_count": len(grouped),
        "elapsed_ms": round(elapsed, 2),
        "sample_memory_mib": round(sample.memory_usage(index=True, deep=True).sum() / (1024 * 1024), 3),
        "full_source_benchmarked": sample_n == len(source),
    }, grouped.head(15).reset_index(drop=True)


========== FILE: period_comparison.py ==========

"""Auditable period comparisons for Python BI Studio.

Works on already-filtered source records and their positionally matched
commercial calculations; does not mutate either input. Calendar, not fiscal,
periods are used, without seasonality or business-day adjustments.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
import pandas as pd

FREQUENCIES = {"Monthly": "M", "Quarterly": "Q", "Yearly": "Y"}
FINANCIAL_FIELDS = ("Gross Revenue", "Net Revenue", "Mapped Costs", "Profit After Mapped Costs")


def _period_series(source: pd.DataFrame, date_column: str, cadence: str) -> tuple[pd.Series, int]:
    if date_column not in source.columns:
        raise ValueError(f"The selected date column is missing: {date_column}")
    if cadence not in FREQUENCIES:
        raise ValueError("Select Monthly, Quarterly or Yearly.")
    # Positional index avoids accidental label alignment for datasets with repeated indices.
    date_values = pd.to_datetime(source[date_column].reset_index(drop=True), errors="coerce", format="mixed")
    invalid_count = int(date_values.isna().sum())
    try:
        periods = date_values.dt.to_period(FREQUENCIES[cadence])
    except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as error:
        raise ValueError("Cannot group this date column into calendar periods. Check mixed time zones or values.") from error
    return periods, invalid_count


def available_periods(source: pd.DataFrame, date_column: str, cadence: str) -> tuple[list[str], int]:
    """Return ordered nonempty observed periods and excluded invalid-date count."""
    periods, invalid_count = _period_series(source, date_column, cadence)
    distinct = sorted(periods.dropna().unique())
    return [str(value) for value in distinct], invalid_count


def _percent_difference(base: float, newer: float) -> float | None:
    """Percentage change is not defined if the baseline is zero or negative."""
    return ((newer - base) / base * 100) if base > 0 else None


def compare_periods(
    source: pd.DataFrame,
    calculations: pd.DataFrame,
    date_column: str,
    cadence: str,
    earlier: str,
    later: str,
    category_column: str | None = None,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    """Compare two observed calendar periods on the same matched transaction rows.

    Outputs: totals table, untruncated category table, and audit metadata.
    Excludes invalid dates ONLY from this comparison. Other dashboard results
    continue to use the entire selected filtered source.
    """
    if len(source) != len(calculations):
        raise ValueError("Source and financial calculation row counts do not match.")
    if not source.columns.is_unique or not calculations.columns.is_unique:
        raise ValueError("Ambiguous duplicate column names are not supported.")
    periods, invalid_dates = _period_series(source, date_column, cadence)
    code = FREQUENCIES[cadence]
    try:
        before_period = pd.Period(earlier, freq=code)
        after_period = pd.Period(later, freq=code)
    except (ValueError, TypeError) as error:
        raise ValueError("Select two valid calendar periods.") from error
    if before_period >= after_period:
        raise ValueError("The earlier period must precede the later period.")
    before_mask = periods.eq(before_period).fillna(False).to_numpy(dtype=bool)
    after_mask = periods.eq(after_period).fillna(False).to_numpy(dtype=bool)
    if not before_mask.any() or not after_mask.any():
        raise ValueError("Both comparison periods must contain source records.")
    fields = [name for name in FINANCIAL_FIELDS if name in calculations.columns]
    if "Net Revenue" not in fields:
        raise ValueError("Financial calculations must include Net Revenue.")
    validated: dict[str, pd.Series] = {}
    for field in fields:
        values = pd.to_numeric(calculations[field].reset_index(drop=True), errors="raise")
        if values.isna().any() or not all(math.isfinite(float(v)) for v in values):
            raise ValueError(f"{field} has missing or non-finite values; comparison stopped.")
        validated[field] = values
    totals_rows = [{"Metric": "Transactions", "Earlier": int(before_mask.sum()), "Later": int(after_mask.sum())}]
    for metric in fields:
        amounts = validated[metric]
        totals_rows.append({"Metric": metric, "Earlier": float(amounts.loc[before_mask].sum()), "Later": float(amounts.loc[after_mask].sum())})
    totals = pd.DataFrame(totals_rows)
    totals["Change"] = totals["Later"] - totals["Earlier"]
    totals["Change %"] = [
        _percent_difference(float(row.Earlier), float(row.Later))
        for row in totals.itertuples(index=False)
    ]
    metadata = {
        "date_column": date_column,
        "cadence": cadence,
        "earlier": str(before_period),
        "later": str(after_period),
        "earlier_rows": int(before_mask.sum()),
        "later_rows": int(after_mask.sum()),
        "excluded_invalid_dates": invalid_dates,
        "periods_are_adjacent": bool(after_period.ordinal - before_period.ordinal == 1),
        "excluded_other_period_rows": int(len(source) - before_mask.sum() - after_mask.sum() - invalid_dates),
    }
    category_table = pd.DataFrame()
    if category_column is not None:
        if category_column not in source.columns:
            raise ValueError("Selected category field is absent.")
        category_values = source[category_column].reset_index(drop=True).astype("string").fillna("(Missing)")
        if len(category_values) != len(validated["Net Revenue"]):
            raise ValueError("Category row count does not match calculations.")
        selected_rows = before_mask | after_mask
        frame = pd.DataFrame({
            "Category": category_values.loc[selected_rows].to_numpy(),
            "Period": pd.Series(["Earlier" if hit else "Later" for hit in before_mask[selected_rows]]),
            "Net Revenue": validated["Net Revenue"].loc[selected_rows].to_numpy(dtype=float),
        })
        if "Profit After Mapped Costs" in fields:
            frame["Profit"] = validated["Profit After Mapped Costs"].loc[selected_rows].to_numpy(dtype=float)
        aggregate = frame.groupby(["Category", "Period"], dropna=False, sort=False).agg(
            Records=("Net Revenue", "size"),
            Revenue=("Net Revenue", "sum"),
            **({"Profit": ("Profit", "sum")} if "Profit" in frame else {}),
        ).unstack("Period", fill_value=0)
        category_table = pd.DataFrame({"Category": aggregate.index.astype(str)})
        for name, output_name in (("Records", "Records"), ("Revenue", "Revenue"), ("Profit", "Profit")):
            if name not in aggregate:
                continue
            for position in ("Earlier", "Later"):
                category_table[f"{position} {output_name}"] = (
                    aggregate[name][position].to_numpy()
                    if position in aggregate[name].columns else 0
                )
            category_table[f"{output_name} Change"] = category_table[f"Later {output_name}"] - category_table[f"Earlier {output_name}"]
            if output_name == "Revenue":
                category_table["Revenue Change %"] = [
                    _percent_difference(float(x), float(y)) for x, y in zip(
                        category_table["Earlier Revenue"], category_table["Later Revenue"]
                    )
                ]
        category_table = category_table.sort_values(
            "Later Revenue", ascending=False, kind="stable"
        ).reset_index(drop=True)
        # Financial audit: grouping ALL categories must reconcile to period totals.
        for position in ("Earlier", "Later"):
            expected = float(validated["Net Revenue"].loc[before_mask if position == "Earlier" else after_mask].sum())
            actual = float(category_table[f"{position} Revenue"].sum())
            if not math.isclose(actual, expected, rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=1e-7):
                raise ValueError("Category comparison revenue did not reconcile; report stopped.")
    return totals, category_table, metadata

"""Commercial planning tools with explicit data/assumption boundaries."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st

from business_metrics import break_even_scenario, revenue_by_period
from performance_insights import price_sensitivity
from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv


def render_planning_workspace(data: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    st.markdown("### Business Planning & Growth")
    st.caption(
        "Historical growth uses uploaded data. Break-even is a manual scenario; "
        "it is not a prediction of actual future performance."
    )
    growth_tab, model_tab = st.tabs(["Historical revenue growth", "Break-even scenario"])
    with growth_tab:
        numeric = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
        dates = [
            col for col in data.columns
            if "date" in str(col).casefold() or "time" in str(col).casefold()
            or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[col])
        ]
        if not numeric or not dates or data.empty:
            st.info("Historical growth requires a dataset with a date field and numeric revenue field.")
        else:
            first, second, third = st.columns(3)
            with first:
                date_col = st.selectbox(
                    "Date / timestamp field", dates,
                    index=dates.index("Date") if "Date" in dates else 0,
                    key="plan_date",
                )
            with second:
                revenue_col = st.selectbox(
                    "Revenue field", numeric,
                    index=numeric.index("Sales") if "Sales" in numeric else 0,
                    key="plan_revenue",
                )
            with third:
                period = st.selectbox(
                    "Compare periods", ["Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"],
                    key="plan_cadence",
                )
            st.caption(
                "Choose a revenue field that is already net of any deductions you want included. "
                "This section does not automatically deduct returns, taxes or discounts."
            )
            try:
                summary, excluded = revenue_by_period(data[date_col], data[revenue_col], period)
                if excluded:
                    st.warning(f"Excluded {excluded:,} rows without valid dates. The source dataset is unchanged.")
                if summary.empty:
                    st.info("There are no valid date/revenue records to summarise.")
                else:
                    cols = st.columns(3)
                    cols[0].metric("Periods observed", f"{len(summary):,}")
                    cols[1].metric("Observed-period revenue", f"{summary['Revenue'].sum():,.2f}")
                    valid_growth = summary["Growth %"].dropna()
                    cols[2].metric(
                        "Latest defined growth",
                        f"{valid_growth.iloc[-1]:+.2f}%" if not valid_growth.empty else "Not available",
                    )
                    st.plotly_chart(
                        px.bar(summary, x="Period", y="Revenue", title=f"Revenue by {period.lower()} period"),
                        width="stretch",
                    )
                    with st.expander("Period-level audit and growth rules"):
                        st.dataframe(summary, width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                        st.caption(
                            "Growth = (current âˆ’ previous) / previous Ã— 100. Undefined for "
                            "missing intermediate periods or prior revenue of zero/less."
                        )
                        st.download_button(
                            "Export growth analysis", summary.to_csv(index=False).encode("utf-8"),
                            file_name="python_bi_revenue_growth.csv", mime="text/csv", key="plan_growth_export",
                        )
            except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
                st.warning(f"Revenue growth is unavailable: {exc}")
    with model_tab:
        st.info(
            "Enter values for one consistent currency and unit of production. "
            "These are your scenario assumptions, not inferred amounts from the dataset."
        )
        a, b, c = st.columns(3)
        with a:
            fixed = st.number_input(
                "Fixed costs per planning period", min_value=0.0, value=0.0, step=100.0,
                key="plan_fixed",
            )
        with b:
            price = st.number_input(
                "Selling price per unit", min_value=0.0, value=0.0, step=1.0,
                key="plan_price",
            )
        with c:
            variable = st.number_input(
                "Variable cost per unit", min_value=0.0, value=0.0, step=1.0,
                key="plan_variable",
            )
        if price == 0 and variable == 0 and fixed == 0:
            st.caption("Enter your scenario assumptions above to calculate a break-even point.")
        else:
            try:
                result = break_even_scenario(fixed, price, variable)
                metrics = st.columns(3)
                metrics[0].metric("Unit contribution", f"{result['contribution_per_unit']:,.2f}")
                cm = result["contribution_margin_pct"]
                metrics[1].metric("Contribution margin", f"{cm:.2f}%" if cm is not None else "Not defined")
                units = result["break_even_units"]
                metrics[2].metric("Break-even units", f"{units:,}" if units is not None else "Not attainable")
                if units is None:
                    st.warning("There is no finite break-even volume: unit contribution is zero or negative.")
                else:
                    st.metric("Revenue at break-even units", f"{result['break_even_revenue']:,.2f}")
                st.caption(
                    "Break-even units = ceiling(fixed costs / (unit price âˆ’ variable cost)). "
                    "The model assumes constant costs and price, and does not include financing, tax or capacity effects."
                )
                st.download_button(
                    "Export scenario assumptions", excel_safe_csv(pd.DataFrame([result])),
                    file_name="python_bi_break_even_scenario.csv", mime="text/csv", key="plan_break_even_export",
                )
                with st.expander("Hypothetical selling-price sensitivity", expanded=False):
                    st.caption(
                        "Illustrative Â±5% and Â±10% changes to selling price only; "
                        "fixed and variable costs remain constant. These are not forecasts."
                    )
                    sensitivity = price_sensitivity(fixed, price, variable)
                    st.dataframe(sensitivity, width="stretch", hide_index=True)
                    finite = sensitivity.dropna(subset=["Break-even Units"])
                    if not finite.empty:
                        st.plotly_chart(
                            px.line(
                                finite, x="Hypothetical Unit Price", y="Break-even Units",
                                markers=True, title="Break-even volume by hypothetical price",
                            ), width="stretch",
                        )
                    else:
                        st.warning("No finite break-even volume exists in these scenarios.")
                    st.download_button(
                        "Export sensitivity scenarios", excel_safe_csv(sensitivity),
                        file_name="python_bi_price_sensitivity.csv",
                        mime="text/csv", key="plan_sensitivity_export",
                    )
            except ValueError as error:
                st.warning(f"Invalid scenario: {error}")


========== FILE: portfolio_analytics.py ==========

"""Reconciled within-dataset category concentration. No market-share inference.

Functions are deterministic and do not mutate source datasets.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import pandas as pd

TOLERANCE = 0.01
MAX_GROUPS = 10000


def _finite_series(frame: pd.DataFrame, name: str) -> pd.Series:
    if name not in frame:
        raise ValueError(f"Missing financial field: {name}.")
    series = pd.to_numeric(frame[name].reset_index(drop=True), errors="coerce")
    if series.isna().any() or not series.map(math.isfinite).all():
        raise ValueError(f"Invalid or missing financial values in {name}.")
    return series.astype(float)


def build_category_concentration(
    source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame,
    totals: dict, category: str,
) -> dict:
    """Calculate grouped revenue/profit and optional nonnegative revenue shares.

    Shares are within THIS filtered dataset only; not total addressable market share.
    Negative category revenue disables percentage concentration calculations.
    """
    if source.empty or len(source) != len(calculations):
        raise ValueError("The source and calculation rows must be nonempty and aligned.")
    if category not in source or not source.columns.is_unique or not calculations.columns.is_unique:
        raise ValueError("Choose a valid, uniquely named category column.")
    grouped_label = source[category].reset_index(drop=True).astype("string").fillna("(Missing)")
    if grouped_label.nunique() > MAX_GROUPS:
        raise ValueError("Over 10,000 categories; narrow the filter for a meaningful comparison.")
    revenue = _finite_series(calculations, "Net Revenue")
    frame = pd.DataFrame({"Category": grouped_label, "Net Revenue": revenue})
    cost_mapped = "Mapped Costs" in calculations and "Profit After Mapped Costs" in calculations
    if cost_mapped:
        frame["Mapped Costs"] = _finite_series(calculations, "Mapped Costs")
        frame["Profit After Mapped Costs"] = _finite_series(calculations, "Profit After Mapped Costs")
    amounts = [col for col in frame.columns if col != "Category"]
    groups = (frame.groupby("Category", as_index=False, dropna=False)[amounts]
              .sum().sort_values("Net Revenue", ascending=False, kind="stable")
              .reset_index(drop=True))
    for group_col, total_key in (
        ("Net Revenue", "net_revenue"),
        ("Mapped Costs", "mapped_costs"),
        ("Profit After Mapped Costs", "profit_after_mapped_costs"),
    ):
        if group_col not in groups:
            continue
        expected = totals.get(total_key)
        if expected is None:
            raise ValueError(f"Missing total for reconciliation: {total_key}.")
        expected = float(expected)
        if not math.isfinite(expected) or not math.isclose(
            float(groups[group_col].sum()), expected, rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=TOLERANCE
        ):
            raise ValueError(f"Category {group_col} does not reconcile to Dashboard totals.")
    total_revenue = float(groups["Net Revenue"].sum())
    share_valid = total_revenue > 0 and not (groups["Net Revenue"] < -1e-9).any()
    metrics = {
        "category_count": len(groups), "total_net_revenue": total_revenue,
        "concentration_available": share_valid,
        "cost_mapped": cost_mapped,
        "top_category": str(groups.iloc[0]["Category"]),
        "top_category_share_pct": None,
        "top_three_share_pct": None,
        "categories_to_80_pct": None,
        "concentration_index": None,
    }
    notes = ["Shares measure distribution across the filtered records, not the wider market."]
    if share_valid:
        fractions = groups["Net Revenue"] / total_revenue
        groups["Revenue Share %"] = (fractions * 100).round(3)
        groups["Cumulative Revenue Share %"] = (fractions.cumsum() * 100).round(3)
        metrics.update(
            top_category_share_pct=float(fractions.iloc[0] * 100),
            top_three_share_pct=float(fractions.head(3).sum() * 100),
            categories_to_80_pct=int((fractions.cumsum() < .8 - 1e-12).sum() + 1),
            concentration_index=float((fractions ** 2).sum() * 10000),
        )
        notes.append("The concentration index runs from near 0 to 10,000; it is not a market HHI.")
    else:
        notes.append(
            "Revenue percentages were omitted because at least one category has negative "
            "net revenue, or total net revenue is not positive."
        )
    return {"groups": groups, "metrics": metrics, "notes": notes}


========== FILE: portfolio_panel.py ==========

"""Optional Executive Dashboard Section 10 â€” portfolio and Pareto insights."""
from __future__ import annotations
import pandas as pd
import plotly.express as px
import streamlit as st
from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv
from portfolio_analytics import build_category_concentration


def render_portfolio_panel(source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame, totals: dict) -> None:
    with st.expander("10  Â·  Revenue concentration and category mix", expanded=False):
        st.caption(
            "Analyze category revenue distribution after all Executive Dashboard filters. "
            "This does not estimate industry-wide market share."
        )
        fields = [c for c in source.columns if (
            pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(source[c]) or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(source[c])
            or isinstance(source[c].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype)
        )]
        if not fields:
            st.info("This dataset has no category or text field for revenue concentration.")
            return
        choice = st.selectbox(
            "Group revenue by", fields,
            index=fields.index("Product") if "Product" in fields else 0,
            key="ex_mix_category",
        )
        if not st.checkbox("Calculate category mix", value=False, key="ex_mix_run"):
            st.caption("Opt in to run the category group-by; no source data will be modified.")
            return
        try:
            result = build_category_concentration(source, calculations, totals, choice)
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError) as exc:
            st.warning(f"Category mix not available: {exc}")
            return
        metrics, groups = result["metrics"], result["groups"]
        a, b, c = st.columns(3)
        a.metric("Categories", f"{metrics['category_count']:,}")
        b.metric("Top category", metrics["top_category"])
        if metrics["concentration_available"]:
            c.metric("Top 3 revenue share", f"{metrics['top_three_share_pct']:.2f}%")
            d, e = st.columns(2)
            d.metric("Categories reaching 80% revenue", metrics["categories_to_80_pct"])
            e.metric("Within-dataset concentration index", f"{metrics['concentration_index']:,.0f}")
        else:
            c.metric("Revenue-share statistics", "Unavailable")
        for note in result["notes"]:
            st.caption(note)
        if metrics["concentration_available"]:
            top = groups.head(15)
            fig = px.bar(top, x="Category", y="Net Revenue", title="Top categories by net revenue")
            fig.update_layout(margin=dict(l=15, r=15, t=45, b=35))
            st.plotly_chart(fig, width="stretch")
        st.dataframe(groups.head(200), width="stretch", hide_index=True)
        st.download_button(
            "Download complete category-mix audit (CSV)",
            data=excel_safe_csv(groups),
            file_name="python_bi_category_mix.csv", mime="text/csv",
            key="ex_mix_download", on_click="ignore",
        )
        st.caption("The CSV includes all categories; the table previews at most 200 groups.")


========== FILE: prepare_privacy.py ==========

"""Append safe ignore patterns to .gitignore without deleting any existing rules.

Warning: .gitignore changes do NOT remove files previously committed to Git.
This command changes only .gitignore, not uploaded datasets or histories.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from deployment_audit import SENSITIVE_EXCLUSIONS


def add_ignores(root: Path) -> tuple[bool, tuple[str, ...]]:
    target = Path(root) / '.gitignore'
    original = target.read_text(encoding='utf-8') if target.exists() else ''
    existing = {line.strip() for line in original.splitlines()}
    missing = tuple(x for x in SENSITIVE_EXCLUSIONS if x not in existing)
    if missing:
        suffix = '' if not original or original.endswith('\n') else '\n'
        target.write_text(original + suffix + '\n# Python BI Studio â€” local data and release evidence\n' + '\n'.join(missing) + '\n', encoding='utf-8')
    return bool(missing), missing


def main() -> None:
    changed, added = add_ignores(Path(__file__).resolve().parent)
    if changed:
        print('Appended', len(added), 'privacy patterns to .gitignore without deleting existing entries.')
    else:
        print('Privacy ignore patterns are already present. Nothing changed.')
    print('IMPORTANT: Review git ls-files separately; .gitignore does not untrack committed files.')

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()

"""Data-minimised academic review packet and honest eight-phase status."""
from __future__ import annotations
from html import escape
from io import BytesIO, StringIO
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import csv
import json
import zipfile

PHASES = (
    ('1', 'Corporate website foundation', 'CORE_VERIFIED', 'Final professor presentation review pending'),
    ('2', 'Dataset Library and persistence', 'LOCALLY_VERIFIED', 'Local single-user workflow'),
    ('3', 'Advanced Data Workspace', 'VERIFIED', '14 development batches confirmed'),
    ('4', 'Executive dashboards and charts', 'CORE_VERIFIED', 'Additional multi-schema UI acceptance pending'),
    ('5', 'Business and commerce intelligence', 'CORE_VERIFIED', 'Accounting assumptions and real-sector validation pending'),
    ('6', 'Investment Intelligence', 'RESEARCH_VERIFIED', 'Scenarios and historical bootstrap; no guaranteed forecasts'),
    ('7', 'Reports and exports', 'CORE_VERIFIED', 'HTML, CSV, ZIP; final professor demo review pending'),
    ('8', 'Performance and final release', 'LOCAL_TESTING', '500k upload/search confirmed; broader workloads & professor approval pending'),
)

CHECKS = (
    ('Home page and corporate light design', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Original Dataset Library restore / switching', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Full-dataset search with original/synthetic files', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Prepare & Validate, missing-value finder', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Commercial financial mapping and accounting assumptions', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Executive Dashboard, filters, Chart Studio', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Professional report HTML/CSV/ZIP and reconciliations', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Business investment NPV/IRR/scenarios', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Market historical research, simulation/backtest limitations', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Large-row workload and memory responsiveness', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Local-only access; no public URL', 'MANUAL'),
    ('Professor assessment, comments and approval', 'AWAITING_PROFESSOR'),
)


def review_summary() -> dict:
    return {"product": "Python BI Studio", "operating_mode": "PRIVATE_LOCAL_ONLY",
            "academic_approval": "NOT_YET_REQUESTED", "public_hosting": "NOT_AUTHORIZED",
            "phase_status": [dict(zip(('phase','area','status','note'), row)) for row in PHASES],
            "disclaimer": "These are project history milestones, not a certificate of full accounting or production readiness."}


def checklist_csv() -> str:
    out = StringIO()
    writer = csv.writer(out)
    writer.writerow(['Review item', 'Initial status', 'Observed result', 'Professor comments'])
    writer.writerows((name, status, '', '') for name, status in CHECKS)
    return out.getvalue()


def review_html() -> str:
    rows = ''.join('<tr>'+''.join('<td>'+escape(str(x))+'</td>' for x in r)+'</tr>' for r in PHASES)
    return ('<!doctype html><html lang="en"><meta charset="utf-8">'
            '<title>Python BI Studio â€” Professor Review</title>'
            '<style>body{font:16px system-ui;color:#142B4A;background:#f5f8fc;max-width:1050px;margin:40px auto;padding:24px}'
            'h1{font-size:32px}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;background:#fff}'
            'td,th{border-bottom:1px solid #ddd;padding:12px;text-align:left;vertical-align:top}'
            '@media print{body{background:#fff}}</style>'
            '<h1>Python BI Studio</h1><p>Academic demonstration â€” private local application; not publicly hosted.</p>'
            '<p>The professor has not yet reviewed or approved this project.</p>'
            '<table><thead><tr><th>Phase</th><th>Area</th><th>Current status</th><th>Outstanding</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
            +rows+'</tbody></table></html>')


def review_packet() -> bytes:
    """No business data, names, machine paths or screenshots are included."""
    target=BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(target, 'w', compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as archive:
        archive.writestr('PROJECT_STATUS.json',json.dumps(review_summary(),indent=2))
        archive.writestr('PROFESSOR_REVIEW.html',review_html())
        archive.writestr('VERIFICATION_CHECKLIST.csv',checklist_csv())
        archive.writestr('README.txt','Private local academic review packet. No raw transactions included.\n'
                         'The professor must independently assess the final application.\n')
    return target.getvalue()


========== FILE: quality_engine.py ==========

"""Python BI Studio â€” reusable dataset quality checks; no dataset mutation."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pandas as pd


def quality_table(data: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Column-by-column missing/unique/type report; safely handles empty frames."""
    rows = len(data)
    missing = data.isna().sum()
    return pd.DataFrame({
        "Column": data.columns.astype(str),
        "Data type": [str(t) for t in data.dtypes],
        "Missing values": missing.to_numpy(),
        "Missing %": (missing.to_numpy() / rows * 100 if rows else [0.0] * len(data.columns)),
        "Non-null unique": [int(data[col].nunique(dropna=True)) for col in data.columns],
    }).round({"Missing %": 2})


def missing_records(data: pd.DataFrame, column: str | None = None) -> pd.DataFrame:
    """Return actual affected records; never remove or fill missing values."""
    if column is None:
        return data.loc[data.isna().any(axis=1)].copy()
    if column not in data.columns:
        raise KeyError(f"Unknown column: {column}")
    return data.loc[data[column].isna()].copy()


def quality_overview(data: pd.DataFrame) -> dict:
    return {
        "rows": len(data),
        "columns": len(data.columns),
        "missing_cells": int(data.isna().sum().sum()),
        "rows_with_missing": int(data.isna().any(axis=1).sum()),
        "duplicate_rows": int(data.duplicated().sum()),
    }


========== FILE: release_cli.py ==========

"""Offline local preflight and release-evidence generator.

Runs no server, deploys nothing and intentionally refuses to claim public readiness.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import json
from pathlib import Path
from release_evidence import build_report, html_report, evidence_zip


def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Python BI Studio local release preflight")
    parser.add_argument("--output", default="release_results", help="Local evidence output directory")
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    out = Path(args.output)
    if not out.is_absolute(): out = root / out
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    report = build_report(root)
    (out / "release_readiness.json").write_text(json.dumps(report, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    (out / "release_readiness.html").write_text(html_report(report), encoding="utf-8")
    (out / "release_readiness_pack.zip").write_bytes(evidence_zip(report))
    print("Python BI Studio â€” local release preflight")
    print("Release gate:", report["gate"])
    for name, count in report["counts"].items(): print(f"{name}: {count}")
    print("Created release_results/ release-readiness JSON, HTML and ZIP evidence.")
    print("NOTICE: Do not publicly deploy shared Dataset Library storage without user isolation.")
    return 0   # Zero means the preflight ran correctly; it does NOT mean approval.

if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())


========== FILE: release_evidence.py ==========

"""Data-minimising release evidence (no raw records, secrets or source paths)."""
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import json
from html import escape
from io import BytesIO
from zipfile import ZipFile, ZIP_DEFLATED
from deployment_audit import check_deployment, release_gate
from pathlib import Path


def build_report(root: Path) -> dict:
    checks = check_deployment(root)
    counts = {label: sum(c["status"] == label for c in checks) for label in ("PASS", "REVIEW", "MANUAL", "BLOCKED")}
    return {
        "product": "Python BI Studio",
        "release": "Enterprise Design and Production Readiness Candidate 1",
        "created_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "scope": "Offline local preflight; NOT an external security certification or public deployment",
        "gate": release_gate(checks),
        "counts": counts,
        "checks": checks,
    }


def html_report(report: dict) -> str:
    trs = "".join(
        "<tr><td>" + escape(x["id"]) + "</td><td>" + escape(x["status"]) + "</td><td>" + escape(x["area"]) + "</td><td>" + escape(x["finding"]) + "</td></tr>"
        for x in report["checks"]
    )
    return (
        '<!doctype html><html lang="en"><meta charset="utf-8"><title>Python BI Studio - Release Readiness</title>'
        '<style>body{font:16px/1.6 Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif;margin:40px auto;max-width:1000px;color:#142B4A}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}td,th{border:1px solid #DCE5F0;padding:10px;text-align:left}th{background:#EDF2F8}h1{font-size:30px} .gate{padding:12px;background:#F5F7FA}</style>'
        f'<h1>{escape(report["product"])} â€” Release Readiness</h1><p>{escape(report["scope"])}</p>'
        f'<p class="gate">Release gate: <strong>{escape(report["gate"])}</strong></p>'
        '<table><thead><tr><th>ID</th><th>Status</th><th>Area</th><th>Finding</th></tr></thead><tbody>'
        + trs + '</tbody></table><p>This is a static audit snapshot. No user datasets are included.</p></html>'
    )


def evidence_zip(report: dict) -> bytes:
    out = BytesIO()
    with ZipFile(out, "w", ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
        z.writestr("release_readiness.json", json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        z.writestr("release_readiness.html", html_report(report))
        z.writestr("README.txt", "Offline preflight only. Public deployment is BLOCKED pending user isolation, authentication and durable storage. No source records or credentials included.\n")
    return out.getvalue()


========== FILE: report_bundle.py ==========

"""Auditable ZIP containing ONLY aggregate executive-report outputs.

No network calls or raw source transaction rows. Chosen grouping labels may be sensitive.
The source report must already have passed report_engine reconciliation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import io
import json
import zipfile
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Mapping

import pandas as pd
from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv

MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES = 25 * 1024 * 1024
ALLOWED_REPORT_FILES = {
    "README.txt", "report.html", "financial_summary.csv",
    "category_performance.csv", "calendar_revenue.csv",
}


def build_report_archive(
    report: Mapping[str, object], *, dataset_name: str, filter_context: str,
    period: str, category: str | None, currency: str,
) -> bytes:
    """Package reconciled report tables with file digests for independent checking."""
    required = ("html", "summary", "categories", "periods", "matching_rows")
    if any(name not in report for name in required):
        raise ValueError("The executive report is incomplete; ZIP creation blocked.")
    html = report["html"]
    if not isinstance(html, bytes) or not html.startswith(b"<!DOCTYPE html>"):
        raise ValueError("Report HTML must be a complete UTF-8 HTML document.")
    summary, groups, periods = (report[k] for k in ("summary", "categories", "periods"))
    if not all(isinstance(x, pd.DataFrame) for x in (summary, groups, periods)):
        raise ValueError("Report tables must be Pandas dataframes.")
    rows = report["matching_rows"]
    if not isinstance(rows, int) or isinstance(rows, bool) or rows < 1:
        raise ValueError("Report matching-row count is invalid.")
    # This is intentionally an aggregate-only archive. The full row audit remains
    # a separate explicit download in Dashboard Section 03.
    files: dict[str, bytes] = {
        "report.html": html,
        "financial_summary.csv": excel_safe_csv(summary),
        "README.txt": (
            "PYTHON BI STUDIO â€” EXECUTIVE REPORT PACK\n\n"
            "Contents: print-ready report.html, financial_summary.csv, optional\n"
            "category_performance.csv and calendar_revenue.csv, and manifest.json.\n"
            "Raw transaction rows are not included. Category labels may identify people\n"
            "if you selected a customer/name/ID field. Review before sharing.\n"
            "Financial values use the selected dashboard filters and mappings.\n"
            "Profit after mapped costs is NOT necessarily accounting net profit.\n"
            "Financial reports are not investment advice.\n"
            "File SHA-256 values are listed in manifest.json. To verify them run:\n"
            "  python verify_report_pack.py python_bi_executive_report_pack.zip\n"
            "This manifest verifies ZIP content integrity, not authorship/signature.\n"
        ).encode("utf-8"),
    }
    if not groups.empty:
        files["category_performance.csv"] = excel_safe_csv(groups)
    if not periods.empty:
        files["calendar_revenue.csv"] = excel_safe_csv(periods)
    total_size = sum(map(len, files.values()))
    if total_size > MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES:
        raise ValueError("Aggregate report exceeds the 25 MiB ZIP input limit; use individual CSV downloads.")
    digests = {
        filename: {"sha256": hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest(), "bytes": len(data)}
        for filename, data in sorted(files.items())
    }
    manifest = {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "product": "Python BI Studio",
        "generated_utc": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "dataset_label": str(dataset_name),
        "filters": str(filter_context),
        "calendar_period": str(period),
        "category_field": None if category is None else str(category),
        "currency_label": str(currency),
        "filtered_row_count": rows,
        "includes_transaction_level_data": False,
        "includes_category_labels": not groups.empty,
        "reconciled_report": True,
        "files": digests,
    }
    files["manifest.json"] = json.dumps(
        manifest, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False, allow_nan=False
    ).encode("utf-8")
    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buffer, "w", compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED, compresslevel=6) as zf:
        for filename in sorted(files):
            zf.writestr(filename, files[filename])
    return buffer.getvalue()


def verify_report_archive(data: bytes) -> dict:
    """Verify complete archive integrity without extracting files to disk."""
    if not isinstance(data, bytes) or len(data) > MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES * 2:
        raise ValueError("Invalid or oversized report archive.")
    try:
        zf = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data), "r")
        with zf:
            info = zf.infolist()
            names = [item.filename for item in info]
            if (len(names) != len(set(names)) or "manifest.json" not in names
                    or len(names) > len(ALLOWED_REPORT_FILES) + 1
                    or any(n not in ALLOWED_REPORT_FILES | {"manifest.json"} for n in names)):
                raise ValueError("The report pack has unexpected or duplicate files.")
            if sum(f.file_size for f in info) > MAX_PAYLOAD_BYTES:
                raise ValueError("Uncompressed report pack is too large.")
            meta = json.loads(zf.read("manifest.json"))
            if (meta.get("schema_version") != 1
                    or meta.get("includes_transaction_level_data") is not False
                    or meta.get("reconciled_report") is not True):
                raise ValueError("Report manifest schema is invalid.")
            if set(meta["files"]) != set(names) - {"manifest.json"}:
                raise ValueError("Manifest file list does not match the ZIP.")
            for filename, expected in meta["files"].items():
                contents = zf.read(filename)
                if (len(contents) != expected["bytes"]
                        or hashlib.sha256(contents).hexdigest() != expected["sha256"]):
                    raise ValueError(f"Report content failed integrity verification: {filename}.")
            return meta
    except (zipfile.BadZipFile, KeyError, json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"Report archive is invalid: {exc}") from exc


========== FILE: report_engine.py ==========

"""Deterministic, audited executive reporting from *already-filtered* commercial metrics.

The saved HTML report is offline/printable and does not contain raw transaction
rows. Raw user content is escaped. CSV summary uses the existing guarded helper.
This module does not mutate source or calculations and requires no new packages.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from html import escape
import math
from typing import Mapping

import pandas as pd

from business_metrics import revenue_by_period
from performance_insights import category_profitability


def _finite(value: object) -> float:
    try:
        amount = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ValueError("Report contains an invalid numeric value.") from exc
    if not math.isfinite(amount):
        raise ValueError("Report contains a non-finite financial value.")
    return amount


def _fmt(value: object, digits: int = 2) -> str:
    if value is None or pd.isna(value):
        return "Not available"
    return f"{_finite(value):,.{digits}f}"


def _pct(value: object) -> str:
    if value is None or pd.isna(value):
        return "Not defined"
    return f"{_finite(value):,.2f}%"


def _e(value: object) -> str:
    return escape(str(value), quote=True)


def _html_table(frame: pd.DataFrame, *, max_rows: int = 20) -> str:
    if frame.empty:
        return '<p class="muted">No valid observations are available for this section.</p>'
    header = "".join(f"<th>{_e(c)}</th>" for c in frame.columns)
    rows = []
    for row in frame.head(max_rows).itertuples(index=False, name=None):
        cells = []
        for value in row:
            if value is None or pd.isna(value):
                display = "â€”"
            elif isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool):
                display = f"{value:,}"
            elif isinstance(value, float):
                display = _fmt(value)
            else:
                display = _e(value)
            cells.append(f"<td>{display}</td>")
        rows.append("<tr>" + "".join(cells) + "</tr>")
    return ("<div class='table-wrap'><table><thead><tr>" + header +
            "</tr></thead><tbody>" + "".join(rows) + "</tbody></table></div>")


def _profitability_groups(source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame, dimension: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
    if dimension not in source.columns:
        raise ValueError("Selected report category is not in the filtered source.")
    if "Mapped Costs" in calculations.columns:
        groups = category_profitability(source, calculations, dimension)
        return groups
    # When no costs were mapped, do not invent profit or profit margin.
    groups = pd.DataFrame({
        "Category": source[dimension].reset_index(drop=True).astype("string").fillna("(Missing)"),
        "Net Revenue": calculations["Net Revenue"].reset_index(drop=True),
    })
    return (groups.groupby("Category", as_index=False, dropna=False)["Net Revenue"]
            .sum().sort_values("Net Revenue", ascending=False, kind="stable")
            .reset_index(drop=True))


def build_executive_report(
    source: pd.DataFrame,
    calculations: pd.DataFrame,
    totals: Mapping[str, object],
    *, dataset_name: str,
    category: str | None,
    date_column: str | None,
    period: str,
    field_mapping: Mapping[str, object],
    filter_notes: str,
    currency_label: str = "Currency not specified",
    generated_at: datetime | None = None,
) -> dict:
    """Return a standalone HTML report plus audit-ready summary/group/time tables.

    Input rows must match in order. Explicit, finite totals must reconcile
    against row-level calculations; otherwise reporting is blocked.
    """
    if source.empty:
        raise ValueError("No filtered rows remain for the report.")
    if len(source) != len(calculations):
        raise ValueError("Filtered source and calculated rows do not align.")
    if not source.columns.is_unique or not calculations.columns.is_unique:
        raise ValueError("Duplicate column names prevent a reliable report.")
    if "Net Revenue" not in calculations:
        raise ValueError("Net Revenue calculation is missing.")
    if period not in ("Monthly", "Quarterly", "Yearly"):
        raise ValueError("Choose Monthly, Quarterly or Yearly.")
    if category is not None and category not in source.columns:
        raise ValueError("The category field is missing from the filtered data.")
    if date_column is not None and date_column not in source.columns:
        raise ValueError("The date field is missing from the filtered data.")
    for key in ("gross_revenue", "net_revenue"):
        if key not in totals:
            raise ValueError(f"Required financial total is missing: {key}.")
    for column, key in (("Gross Revenue", "gross_revenue"), ("Net Revenue", "net_revenue"),
                        ("Mapped Costs", "mapped_costs"), ("Profit After Mapped Costs", "profit_after_mapped_costs")):
        if column in calculations.columns:
            values = pd.to_numeric(calculations[column], errors="coerce")
            if not values.notna().all() or not values.map(math.isfinite).all():
                raise ValueError(f"Calculated column contains missing or invalid numbers: {column}.")
            if key not in totals or not math.isclose(float(values.sum()), _finite(totals[key]), rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=0.01):
                raise ValueError(f"Report totals do not reconcile for {column}.")
    has_cost = "Profit After Mapped Costs" in calculations.columns
    summary_rows = [
        ("Matching transactions", len(source)),
        ("Gross Revenue", _finite(totals["gross_revenue"])),
        ("Net Revenue", _finite(totals["net_revenue"])),
        ("Mapped Costs", _finite(totals["mapped_costs"]) if has_cost else None),
        ("Profit After Mapped Costs", _finite(totals["profit_after_mapped_costs"]) if has_cost else None),
        ("Profit Margin %", _finite(totals["profit_margin_pct"]) if has_cost and totals.get("profit_margin_pct") is not None else None),
        ("Net Business Loss", _finite(totals["net_business_loss"]) if has_cost else None),
    ]
    summary = pd.DataFrame(summary_rows, columns=["Metric", "Value"])
    groups = (_profitability_groups(source, calculations, category)
              if category is not None else pd.DataFrame())
    if not groups.empty:
        if not math.isclose(float(groups["Net Revenue"].sum()), _finite(totals["net_revenue"]),
                            rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=.01):
            raise ValueError("Category revenue does not reconcile to source totals.")
        if has_cost and not math.isclose(float(groups["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum()),
                                         _finite(totals["profit_after_mapped_costs"]), rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=.01):
            raise ValueError("Category profit does not reconcile to source totals.")
    periods, missing_dates = (revenue_by_period(source[date_column], calculations["Net Revenue"], period)
                              if date_column is not None else (pd.DataFrame(), 0))
    if date_column is not None:
        valid_dates_revenue = (pd.to_numeric(calculations["Net Revenue"].reset_index(drop=True), errors="raise")
                               [pd.to_datetime(source[date_column].reset_index(drop=True), errors="coerce", format="mixed").notna()]
                               .sum())
        if not math.isclose(float(periods["Revenue"].sum()), float(valid_dates_revenue),
                            rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=.01):
            raise ValueError("Time-series revenue does not reconcile to dated source rows.")

    generated = generated_at or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    if generated.tzinfo is None:
        raise ValueError("Report timestamp must have timezone information.")
    timestamp = generated.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime("%d %b %Y, %H:%M UTC")
    notes = [
        "All numbers use the selected Executive Dashboard filters and financial field mappings.",
        "Profit is after mapped costs, NOT accounting net profit unless all expenses and taxes are included.",
        "Missing/invalid dates are excluded from time analysis only; they remain in headline totals.",
        "No currency conversion is performed; use one consistent monetary unit.",
        "CSV transaction-level audits remain available in Section 03 of Executive Overview.",
    ]
    if not has_cost:
        notes.append("No cost field was mapped. Profit, loss and margin are not reported.")
    if missing_dates:
        notes.append(f"{missing_dates:,} records were omitted from time-series analysis for invalid/missing dates.")
    if category is None:
        notes.append("No category was selected for this report.")
    if date_column is None:
        notes.append("No date column was selected for this report.")
    map_rows = "".join(
        f"<tr><th>{_e(label)}</th><td>{_e(value)}</td></tr>"
        for label, value in field_mapping.items()
    )
    note_html = "".join(f"<li>{_e(item)}</li>" for item in notes)
    kpis = [("Gross Revenue", _fmt(totals["gross_revenue"])),
            ("Net Revenue", _fmt(totals["net_revenue"]))]
    if has_cost:
        kpis += [("Mapped Costs", _fmt(totals["mapped_costs"])),
                 ("Profit After Mapped Costs", _fmt(totals["profit_after_mapped_costs"])),
                 ("Margin", _pct(totals.get("profit_margin_pct")))]
    tiles = "".join(f"<div class='tile'><span>{_e(label)}</span><strong>{_e(value)}</strong></div>" for label, value in kpis)
    report = f"""<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>Python BI Studio â€” Executive Report</title><style>
