========== FILE: run_design_release_tests.py ==========

"""Run the new release tests plus all verified financial/investment regressions.

Outputs only test file names, results and summary. Actual browser testing remains manual.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

BASE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
NEW = [
    'test_design_system.py', 'test_deployment_audit.py', 'test_release_evidence.py',
    'test_operations_ui_mock.py', 'test_design_integration.py', 'test_design_release_manifest.py',
]
REGRESSION = [
    'test_assurance_contracts.py','test_phase45_acceptance.py',
    'test_assurance_reports.py','test_assurance_performance.py','test_assurance_ui_mock.py',
    'test_scale_batch12.py','test_explorer_ui_mock.py',
    'test_investment_foundation.py','test_investment_forecasting.py',
    'test_ui_integration_local.py',
    'test_phase3_batch2.py','test_phase3_batch34.py','test_phase3_batch56.py',
    'test_phase3_batch78.py','test_phase3_batch910.py',
    'test_phase3_batch1112.py','test_phase3_batch1314.py',
]

def main(argv=None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Python BI Studio local release tests')
    parser.add_argument('--fast',action='store_true',help='Run new tests only; skip earlier regression suite')
    args=parser.parse_args(argv)
    suite = NEW if args.fast else NEW + REGRESSION
    results=[]
    for test in suite:
        if not (BASE / test).is_file():
            results.append({'name': test,'status':'FAIL','reason':'Test file absent'})
            print(f'FAIL | {test} | File absent')
            continue
        try:
            process=subprocess.run(
                [sys.executable, str(BASE/test)],cwd=BASE,
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                text=True,timeout=180, encoding='utf-8',errors='replace',
            )
            ok=process.returncode==0
            results.append({'name':test,'status':'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL','exit_code':process.returncode})
            print(f'{"PASS" if ok else "FAIL"} | {test}')
            if not ok:
                print('   Review this script directly for details:',test)
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            results.append({'name':test,'status':'FAIL','reason':'Timeout after 180 seconds'})
            print('FAIL |',test,'| Timeout')
    dest=BASE/'release_results'
    dest.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    report={'timestamp_utc':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec='seconds'),
            'scope':'Local automated syntax/calculation/integration checks; browser and security gates remain manual',
            'mode':'fast' if args.fast else 'full',
            'results':results,'passed':sum(x['status']=='PASS' for x in results),
            'failed':sum(x['status']=='FAIL' for x in results)}
    (dest/'regression_tests.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print(f'FINISHED: {report["passed"]} passed, {report["failed"]} failed. Browser/manual gates remain.')
    return 1 if report['failed'] else 0

if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())


========== FILE: run_enterprise_assurance.py ==========

"""Run Phase4/5 calculation acceptance, environment audit, optional module benchmark."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
import json
from assurance_phase45 import run_phase45_checks
from assurance_project import inspect_project
from assurance_reports import report_document,bundle_audit
from assurance_performance import benchmark_workflow


def main()->int:
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description='Python BI Studio offline acceptance & scale suite')
    parser.add_argument('--rows',type=int,default=10000,help='Synthetic module benchmark rows, 100 to 500000')
    parser.add_argument('--report-dir',default='assurance_results')
    parser.add_argument('--csv',default=None,help='Optional synthetic benchmark CSV, not user business data')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    root=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    dest=Path(args.report_dir)
    if not dest.is_absolute():dest=root/dest
    dest.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    checks=[c.as_dict() for c in run_phase45_checks()]+inspect_project(root)
    try:
        perf=benchmark_workflow(args.rows, existing_csv=args.csv)
        checks.append({'id':'PERFORMANCE-CHAIN','area':'Module integration','status':'PASS',
                       'detail':f"{args.rows:,} rows: search, quality sample, financial mapping, group and period reconciled"})
    except Exception as exc:
        perf={'error_type':type(exc).__name__,'scope':'Module integration only'}
        checks.append({'id':'PERFORMANCE-CHAIN','area':'Module integration','status':'FAIL',
                       'detail':f'{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}'})
    report=report_document(checks,scope=f'Automated acceptance + {args.rows:,}-row module test')
    (dest/'enterprise_acceptance.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    (dest/'performance_module_results.json').write_text(json.dumps(perf,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    (dest/'enterprise_acceptance_pack.zip').write_bytes(bundle_audit(report))
    for status in ('PASS','FAIL','MANUAL'):
        print(f'{status}: {report["counts"][status]}')
    print(f'RELEASE GATE: {report["release_gate"]}')
    print('Evidence: assurance_results/enterprise_acceptance.json and enterprise_acceptance_pack.zip')
    print('MANUAL items remain pending even if automated tests all pass.')
    return 1 if report['counts']['FAIL'] else 0

if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())


========== FILE: run_final_acceptance.py ==========

"""Generate local, record-free final roadmap acceptance evidence (no hosting).

Automated module/fixture tests run immediately. Browser, real accounting data,
professional evaluation and public hosting are NOT auto-approved.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from html import escape
import json
from pathlib import Path
import zipfile
from final_financial_checks import financial_edge_suite
from final_reliability_checks import run_recovery_checks
from local_workload_benchmark import run_workload
from ui_runtime_metrics import summarize

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
OUT=ROOT/'acceptance_results'
GATES=[
 ('G1','Multi-schema Dashboard compatibility','Different valid schemas, legacy safety, null/empty filters','Compare classic practice workbook against 3 alternate-sector CSVs in Streamlit.'),
 ('G2','Independent accounting and financial reconciliation','Revenue, costs, discounts, returns, taxes, ROI and report consistency','Check mapping semantics against representative actual source/accounting definitions.'),
 ('G3','Full-application performance','Synthetic search/quality/finance/chart/report module workload','Perform timed Streamlit interactions at 10k, 100k, and 500k where feasible.'),
 ('G4','Edge reliability, safe exports and recovery','Temporary-only library integrity, invalid uploads, CSV/HTML exports','Verify actual restart, saved-dataset restoration, empty results and browser exports.'),
 ('G5','Technical roadmap sign-off evidence','Generates tested release evidence and manual acceptance matrix','Complete manual gates, record limitations, obtain your own final confirmation.')
]


def html_table(rows, columns):
    heading=''.join('<th>'+escape(str(col))+'</th>' for col in columns)
    body=''.join('<tr>'+''.join('<td>'+escape(str(row.get(col,'')))+'</td>' for col in columns)+'</tr>' for row in rows)
    return '<table><thead><tr>'+heading+'</tr></thead><tbody>'+body+'</tbody></table>'


def make_html(report):
    gates=report['gates']
    rows=[{'Gate':g['id'],'Area':g['name'],'Automated':'PASS' if g['automated']=='PASS' else 'FAIL',
           'Browser / sign-off':'PENDING'} for g in gates]
    failed=[r for r in report['automated_checks'] if r['status']=='FAIL']
    fails=''.join('<li>'+escape(x['id']+': '+x['detail'])+'</li>' for x in failed)
    return f'''<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
<title>Python BI Studio / Final Acceptance Evidence</title><style>
body{{font:16px/1.5 Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif;color:#26364a;background:#f5f8fc;margin:0}}main{{max-width:1080px;margin:28px auto;background:white;padding:34px;border-radius:12px}}
h1{{color:#142b4a;font-size:30px}}h2{{color:#142b4a;margin-top:30px}}.eyebrow{{color:#2878e5;text-transform:uppercase;font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.12em;font-weight:bold}}
.note{{background:#f2f6fc;padding:15px;border-left:4px solid #2878e5}}table{{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;font-size:13px}}th,td{{padding:10px;border-bottom:1px solid #dce5f0;text-align:left}}th{{background:#edf3fb}}@media print{{body{{background:white}}main{{margin:0;padding:0}}}}
</style></head><body><main><div class="eyebrow">Private local application / Quality assurance</div>
<h1>Final Roadmap Acceptance Evidence</h1><p><b>Generated:</b> {escape(report['utc_generated'])} UTC</p>
<div class="note"><b>Current gate:</b> {escape(report['release_status'])}<p>Automated fixture/module checks are distinct from actual Windows Streamlit browser checks. No public hosting, raw business records, usernames, saved paths or real accounting records are included.</p></div>
<h2>Five acceptance gates</h2>{html_table(rows,['Gate','Area','Automated','Browser / sign-off'])}
<h2>Automated checks</h2><p>{report['pass_count']} passed / {report['fail_count']} failed. Synthetic workload: {report['workload']['rows']:,} rows.</p>
{html_table(report['automated_checks'],['id','title','status'])}
<h2>Issues</h2>{'<ul>'+fails+'</ul>' if fails else '<p>None in automated checks.</p>'}
<h2>Full application performance</h2><p>{escape(report['ui_metrics']['measurement'])}. Samples collected: {report['ui_metrics']['samples']}.</p>
<h2>Required follow-up</h2><p>Complete manual browser checks in docs/05_GATE_BROWSER_CHECKLIST.md and assess financial column meaning in a real business dataset. Keep local-only mode. Original roadmap Phase 6 calibrated AI predictions and live-market feeds are not implemented; do not describe them as completed.</p>
<footer>Python BI Studio â€” offline evidence, not academic sign-off or production security certification.</footer></main></body></html>'''


def run(rows=10_000):
    if rows not in (10_000,100_000,500_000):
        raise ValueError('Rows must be exactly 10000, 100000 or 500000.')
    OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    finance=financial_edge_suite()
    recovery=run_recovery_checks()
    all_checks=finance+recovery
    benchmark=None
    try:
        benchmark=run_workload(rows)
        all_checks.append({'id':'G3-MODULE','title':f'{rows:,}-row synthetic module-chain benchmark','status':'PASS',
                           'detail':'Search, quality, filters, mapped financials, chart prep, report and CSV checks'})
    except Exception as exc:
        all_checks.append({'id':'G3-MODULE','title':'Synthetic module-chain benchmark','status':'FAIL',
                           'detail':type(exc).__name__+': '+str(exc)})
        benchmark={'rows':rows,'status':'FAIL','streamlit_full_ui_certified':False,'steps_sec':{}}
    checks_by_gate={
        'G1':[x for x in all_checks if x['id'].startswith('F4-') or x['id'].startswith('F45-SECTOR')],
        'G2':[x for x in all_checks if x['id'].startswith('F5-') or x['id']=='F7-01'],
        'G3':[x for x in all_checks if x['id']=='G3-MODULE'],
        'G4':[x for x in all_checks if x['id'].startswith('G4-') or x['id'].startswith('F7-')],
        'G5':all_checks,
    }
    gates=[]
    for gate,name,automated,manual in GATES:
        subset=checks_by_gate[gate]
        gates.append({'id':gate,'name':name,'automated':'PASS' if all(x['status']=='PASS' for x in subset) else 'FAIL',
                      'automated_scope':automated,'browser_or_real_data':'PENDING',
                      'pending_requirement':manual})
    status='AUTOMATED_PASS__MANUAL_ACCEPTANCE_PENDING' if all(x['status']=='PASS' for x in all_checks) else 'AUTOMATED_FAILURES_TO_CORRECT'
    report={'application':'Python BI Studio','scope':'Local-only final original roadmap QA',
            'utc_generated':datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S'),
            'release_status':status,'gates':gates,'automated_checks':all_checks,
            'pass_count':sum(x['status']=='PASS' for x in all_checks),
            'fail_count':sum(x['status']=='FAIL' for x in all_checks),
            'workload':benchmark,'ui_metrics':summarize(),
            'actual_browser_passed':False,'external_approval':'NOT_REQUESTED',
            'public_hosting':'NOT_AUTHORIZED',
            'known_limitations':['No trained/calibrated AI forecasting model or live market provider integration.',
                                 'Server-side render timings are not browser-load certification.',
                                 'Mapped financial results require correct accounting assumptions and consistent currency.',
                                 'Legacy tutorial view retains practice-workbook assumptions when schema accepted.']}
    json_file=OUT/'FINAL_ACCEPTANCE_REPORT.json'
    html_file=OUT/'FINAL_ACCEPTANCE_REPORT.html'
    json_file.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    html_file.write_text(make_html(report),encoding='utf-8')
    evidence=OUT/'FINAL_ACCEPTANCE_EVIDENCE.zip'
    with zipfile.ZipFile(evidence,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as bundle:
        bundle.write(json_file,json_file.name);bundle.write(html_file,html_file.name)
        for name in ('05_GATE_BROWSER_CHECKLIST.md','ACCOUNTING_ASSUMPTIONS.md',
                     'ROADMAP_FINAL_STATUS.md','LOCAL_RESTART_AND_BACKUP.md'):
            file=ROOT/'docs'/name
            if file.exists():bundle.write(file,'docs/'+name)
        test_result=OUT/'FINAL_TEST_RESULTS.json'
        if test_result.exists():bundle.write(test_result,test_result.name)
        ui=OUT/'UI_SERVER_TIMING_SUMMARY.json'
        if ui.exists():bundle.write(ui,ui.name)
    print('Acceptance:',status)
    print('Automated:',report['pass_count'],'PASS,',report['fail_count'],'FAIL')
    print('Module-chain synthetic row count:',rows)
    print('Real Streamlit browser and final approval: PENDING')
    print('Output:',evidence)
    return report


if __name__=='__main__':
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--rows',type=int,choices=[10000,100000,500000],default=10000)
    args=parser.parse_args()
    result=run(args.rows)
    raise SystemExit(0 if result['fail_count']==0 else 1)


========== FILE: run_final_gate_tests.py ==========

"""One terminal command: 5 new final-gate tests + 31 preserved prior scripts.

Superseded historical release SHA256 tests are deliberately excluded because
app.py and commerce_engine.py have changed. The new manifest verifies them.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime,timezone
import json
import importlib.util
import subprocess
import sys

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
NEW=['test_final_financial.py','test_final_legacy.py','test_final_reliability.py',
     'test_final_ui_timing.py','test_final_evidence.py']

# Preserve the latest baseline's regression list, except its obsolete manifest.
from run_local_review_tests import NEW as PRIOR_NEW, OLD as PRIOR_OLD
PRIOR=[p for p in PRIOR_NEW+PRIOR_OLD if p not in ('test_local_release_manifest.py','test_design_release_manifest.py')]
TESTS=NEW+PRIOR


def run():
    results=[]
    for name in TESTS:
        target=ROOT/name
        if not target.is_file():
            results.append({'file':name,'status':'FAIL','reason':'Missing test file'})
            print('FAIL:',name,'missing')
            continue
        if importlib.util.find_spec('streamlit') is None:
            # Streamlit isn't available in the *preparation* runner. The shim
            # only lets pure calculation and existing fake-UI tests import.
            cmd=[sys.executable,'-c',
              'import sys,types;sys.modules["streamlit"]=types.SimpleNamespace();sys.argv=[sys.argv[1]];sys.modules["__main__"].__file__=sys.argv[0];exec(compile(open(sys.argv[0],encoding="utf-8").read(),sys.argv[0],"exec"),sys.modules["__main__"].__dict__)',str(target)]
        else:
            cmd=[sys.executable,str(target)]
        try:
            p=subprocess.run(cmd,cwd=ROOT,text=True,encoding='utf-8',errors='replace',
                             capture_output=True,timeout=180)
            passed=p.returncode==0
            results.append({'file':name,'status':'PASS' if passed else 'FAIL','returncode':p.returncode})
            print(('PASS' if passed else 'FAIL')+':',name)
            if not passed:
                print('    STDERR:',p.stderr[-1500:])
                print('    STDOUT:',p.stdout[-1500:])
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            results.append({'file':name,'status':'FAIL','reason':'180-second limit exceeded'})
            print('FAIL:',name,'timed out')
    summary={'generated_utc':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec='seconds'),
             'test_results':results,'passed':sum(x['status']=='PASS' for x in results),
             'failed':sum(x['status']=='FAIL' for x in results),
             'browser_signoff':'PENDING','hosting':'NOT_AUTHORIZED'}
    output=ROOT/'acceptance_results';output.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (output/'FINAL_TEST_RESULTS.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print('FINISHED:',summary['passed'],'passed,',summary['failed'],'failed.')
    print('Browser checks and final local academic roadmap sign-off: NOT AUTOMATED.')
    return 0 if summary['failed']==0 else 1

if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(run())


========== FILE: run_local_review_tests.py ==========

"""One combined terminal run: new local-only checks and preserved regressions."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse, importlib.util, json, subprocess, sys
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime,timezone

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
NEW=[
    'test_local_file_guard.py','test_local_recovery.py','test_sector_acceptance.py',
    'test_professor_review.py','test_local_launcher.py','test_local_app_wiring.py',
    'test_local_workload.py','test_synthetic_sector_data.py','test_no_review_panel.py',
    'test_local_release_manifest.py',
]
OLD=[
    'test_design_system.py','test_deployment_audit.py','test_release_evidence.py',
    'test_operations_ui_mock.py','test_design_integration.py',
    # Old design-release manifest validates the PREVIOUS app.py intentionally;
    # it is superseded by the new local release manifest.
    'test_assurance_contracts.py','test_phase45_acceptance.py',
    'test_assurance_reports.py','test_assurance_performance.py','test_assurance_ui_mock.py',
    'test_scale_batch12.py','test_explorer_ui_mock.py',
    'test_investment_foundation.py','test_investment_forecasting.py',
    'test_ui_integration_local.py',
    'test_phase3_batch2.py','test_phase3_batch34.py','test_phase3_batch56.py',
    'test_phase3_batch78.py','test_phase3_batch910.py',
    'test_phase3_batch1112.py','test_phase3_batch1314.py',
]

def run(fast=False):
    results=[]
    for file in (NEW if fast else NEW+OLD):
        path=ROOT/file
        if not path.is_file():
            results.append({'test':file,'status':'FAIL','reason':'Missing file'})
            print('FAIL:',file,'not found')
            continue
        try:
            # The preparation environment may not include Streamlit. In that
            # case pure calculations/UI mocks use a stub in the CHILD process
            # only. A real installed Streamlit is always used on the user's PC.
            if importlib.util.find_spec('streamlit') is None:
                cmd=[sys.executable,'-c',
                     'import sys,types;sys.modules["streamlit"]=types.SimpleNamespace();sys.argv=[sys.argv[1]];sys.modules["__main__"].__file__=sys.argv[0];exec(compile(open(sys.argv[0],encoding="utf-8").read(),sys.argv[0],"exec"),sys.modules["__main__"].__dict__)',
                     str(path)]
            else:
                cmd=[sys.executable,str(path)]
            proc=subprocess.run(cmd,cwd=ROOT,capture_output=True,
                text=True,encoding='utf-8',errors='replace',timeout=180)
            ok=proc.returncode==0
            results.append({'test':file,'status':'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL',
                            'exit_code':proc.returncode})
            print(('PASS' if ok else 'FAIL')+':',file)
            if not ok:
                print('   Failed test; run this file directly to see the details.')
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            results.append({'test':file,'status':'FAIL','reason':'Timeout 180 seconds'})
            print('FAIL:',file,'timed out')
    report={'date_utc':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec='seconds'),
            'scope':'Local-only automated checks; browser and academic review still manual',
            'results':results,'passed':sum(x['status']=='PASS' for x in results),
            'failed':sum(x['status']=='FAIL' for x in results)}
    folder=ROOT/'professor_results';folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    (folder/'LOCAL_TEST_RESULTS.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print('FINISHED:',report['passed'],'passed,',report['failed'],'failed.')
    print('Browser review, external approval and public hosting are NOT included.')
    return 0 if report['failed']==0 else 1

if __name__=='__main__':
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--fast',action='store_true')
    raise SystemExit(run(parser.parse_args().fast))


========== FILE: scalable_analytics.py ==========

"""Bounded, testable chart data preparation for Python BI Studio.

Every chart calculation uses the SAME ordered Dashboard-filtered records as its
financial mapping. Plot-ready output is bounded; source frames are never mutated.
Missing/non-finite chart measure values are counted and excluded, not made zero.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

PERIODS = {"Monthly": "M", "Quarterly": "Q", "Yearly": "Y"}
MAX_GROUPS = 50
MAX_PERIODS = 250
MAX_SCATTER_POINTS = 5_000


def numeric_measure_options(source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame) -> list[str]:
    """Clearly label original numeric fields vs. computed financial fields."""
    _check_frames(source, calculations)
    source_options = [f"Source: {c}" for c in source.select_dtypes(include="number").columns]
    finance_options = [f"Calculated: {c}" for c in calculations.select_dtypes(include="number").columns]
    return finance_options + source_options


def is_ratio_measure(name: str) -> bool:
    """A sum of source percentages/margins is generally not a meaningful metric."""
    lowered = str(name).casefold()
    return any(token in lowered for token in ("%", "margin", "ratio", "roi", "rate", "percentage"))


def _check_frames(source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame) -> None:
    if len(source) != len(calculations):
        raise ValueError("Source records and calculated financial rows do not align.")
    if not source.columns.is_unique or not calculations.columns.is_unique:
        raise ValueError("Duplicate column names must be resolved before charting.")


def _value_series(source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame, metric: str) -> tuple[pd.Series, np.ndarray]:
    _check_frames(source, calculations)
    if metric.startswith("Source: "):
        name = metric[len("Source: "):]
        table = source
    elif metric.startswith("Calculated: "):
        name = metric[len("Calculated: "):]
        table = calculations
    else:
        raise ValueError("Select a valid source or calculated measure.")
    if name not in table.columns:
        raise ValueError(f"Measure '{name}' is not in the available dataset.")
    if not pd.api.types.is_numeric_dtype(table[name]):
        raise ValueError("The selected measure is not numeric.")
    # Reset indices before joining series; duplicated business record IDs are valid.
    values = pd.to_numeric(table[name], errors="coerce").reset_index(drop=True)
    arr = values.to_numpy(dtype="float64", na_value=np.nan)
    valid = np.isfinite(arr)
    return values, valid


def _category_values(source: pd.DataFrame, category: str) -> pd.Series:
    if category not in source.columns:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown category field: {category}")
    return source[category].reset_index(drop=True).astype("string").fillna("(Missing)")


def category_chart_data(
    source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame, category: str,
    metric: str, aggregation: str = "Sum", top_n: int = 12,
    direction: str = "Highest", with_remainder: bool = False,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    """Return bounded categories and audit metadata; donut remainder reconciles sums."""
    if aggregation not in ("Sum", "Average"):
        raise ValueError("Choose Sum or Average.")
    if direction not in ("Highest", "Lowest"):
        raise ValueError("Choose Highest or Lowest.")
    if not 1 <= int(top_n) <= MAX_GROUPS:
        raise ValueError(f"Top N must be between 1 and {MAX_GROUPS}.")
    if aggregation == "Sum" and is_ratio_measure(metric):
        raise ValueError("Summing percentages or ratios is misleading. Choose Average, or chart the underlying amounts.")
    if with_remainder and aggregation != "Sum":
        raise ValueError("A remainder can only be combined with Sum, not Average.")
    if with_remainder and direction != "Highest":
        raise ValueError("Donut charts require highest-first selection.")
    values, finite = _value_series(source, calculations, metric)
    valid_count = int(finite.sum())
    meta = {"included_rows": valid_count, "excluded_measure_rows": int((~finite).sum()),
            "all_groups": 0, "shown_groups": 0, "truncated_groups": 0}
    if not valid_count:
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=["Category", "Value", "Records"]), meta
    frame = pd.DataFrame({"Category": _category_values(source, category).loc[finite].to_numpy(),
                          "Value": values.loc[finite].to_numpy(dtype=float)})
    grouped = frame.groupby("Category", dropna=False, observed=True)["Value"].agg(
        Value="sum" if aggregation == "Sum" else "mean", Records="size"
    ).reset_index()
    grouped = grouped.sort_values(["Value", "Category"],
                                  ascending=[direction == "Lowest", True], kind="stable")
    meta["all_groups"] = len(grouped)
    top = grouped.head(int(top_n)).copy()
    tail = grouped.iloc[int(top_n):]
    if with_remainder:
        if (grouped["Value"] < 0).any():
            raise ValueError("A donut is not suitable when the selected groups have negative values. Use a bar chart.")
        if len(tail):
            other_name = f"Other categories ({len(tail)})"
            if other_name in grouped["Category"].tolist():
                other_name = f"Remaining groups ({len(tail)})"
            top = pd.concat([top, pd.DataFrame({
                "Category": [other_name], "Value": [float(tail["Value"].sum())],
                "Records": [int(tail["Records"].sum())],
            })], ignore_index=True)
    meta["shown_groups"] = len(top)
    meta["truncated_groups"] = len(tail)
    meta["complete_metric_total"] = float(frame["Value"].sum()) if aggregation == "Sum" else None
    return top.reset_index(drop=True), meta


def time_chart_data(
    source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame, date_column: str,
    metric: str, cadence: str = "Monthly", aggregation: str = "Sum",
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    if date_column not in source.columns:
        raise ValueError("The selected date field is missing.")
    if cadence not in PERIODS:
        raise ValueError("Choose Monthly, Quarterly or Yearly.")
    if aggregation not in ("Sum", "Average"):
        raise ValueError("Choose Sum or Average.")
    if aggregation == "Sum" and is_ratio_measure(metric):
        raise ValueError("Do not sum a percentage or ratio. Use Average or the underlying amounts.")
    values, finite = _value_series(source, calculations, metric)
    parsed = pd.to_datetime(source[date_column].reset_index(drop=True), format="mixed", errors="coerce")
    accepted = parsed.notna().to_numpy() & finite
    meta = {"included_rows": int(accepted.sum()), "excluded_invalid_dates": int(parsed.isna().sum()),
            "excluded_measure_rows": int((~finite).sum()), "periods": 0}
    if not accepted.any():
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=["Period", "Value", "Records"]), meta
    try:
        indexed = parsed.loc[accepted].dt.to_period(PERIODS[cadence])
    except (AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as error:
        raise ValueError("Dates cannot be grouped; check inconsistent time zones and formats.") from error
    frame = pd.DataFrame({"Period": indexed.to_numpy(),
                          "Value": values.loc[accepted].to_numpy(dtype=float)})
    grouped = frame.groupby("Period", observed=True, sort=True)["Value"].agg(
        Value="sum" if aggregation == "Sum" else "mean", Records="size"
    ).reset_index()
    if len(grouped) > MAX_PERIODS:
        raise ValueError(f"{len(grouped):,} time points exceed the {MAX_PERIODS} point chart limit. Choose a coarser period.")
    grouped["Period"] = grouped["Period"].astype(str)
    meta["periods"] = len(grouped)
    return grouped, meta


def scatter_chart_data(
    source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame,
    x_metric: str, y_metric: str, max_points: int = 1_000,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    if not 1 <= int(max_points) <= MAX_SCATTER_POINTS:
        raise ValueError(f"Sample size must be 1â€“{MAX_SCATTER_POINTS:,} points.")
    x, good_x = _value_series(source, calculations, x_metric)
    y, good_y = _value_series(source, calculations, y_metric)
    accepted = good_x & good_y
    full_count = int(accepted.sum())
    frame = pd.DataFrame({"X": x.loc[accepted].to_numpy(dtype=float),
                          "Y": y.loc[accepted].to_numpy(dtype=float)})
    if len(frame) > max_points:
        frame = frame.sample(n=int(max_points), random_state=42).sort_index()
    return frame.reset_index(drop=True), {"valid_rows": full_count,
                                          "excluded_rows": int((~accepted).sum()),
                                          "charted_rows": len(frame),
                                          "is_sample": len(frame) < full_count}


def histogram_chart_data(
    source: pd.DataFrame, calculations: pd.DataFrame, metric: str,
    bins: int = 20,
) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict]:
    if not 5 <= int(bins) <= 80:
        raise ValueError("Histogram bins must be between 5 and 80.")
    values, finite = _value_series(source, calculations, metric)
    good = values.loc[finite].to_numpy(dtype=float)
    meta = {"included_rows": len(good), "excluded_measure_rows": int((~finite).sum())}
    if not len(good):
        return pd.DataFrame(columns=["Bin midpoint", "Bin lower", "Bin upper", "Records"]), meta
    counts, edges = np.histogram(good, bins=int(bins))
    frame = pd.DataFrame({"Bin midpoint": (edges[:-1] + edges[1:]) / 2,
                          "Bin lower": edges[:-1], "Bin upper": edges[1:],
                          "Records": counts.astype(int)})
    assert int(frame["Records"].sum()) == len(good)
    return frame, meta


========== FILE: schema_aware_dashboard.py ==========

"""Final Phase 4 gate: make the tutorial/legacy Dashboard schema- and result-aware.

Normal workbook retains the original Detailed Analytics implementation unchanged.
For incompatible or excessively large datasets a safe, explicitly mapped summary
is shown instead of trusting column names or displaying misleading calculations.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
import math
import pandas as pd

LEGACY_COLUMNS = frozenset({
    "Sales", "Total Cost", "Profit", "Profit Margin (%)", "Quantity",
    "Product", "Region", "City", "Date", "ProductID", "CustomerID"
})
LEGACY_MAX_ROWS = 50_000


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class LegacyAssessment:
    compatible: bool
    reasons: tuple[str, ...]
    rows: int


def assess_legacy(data: pd.DataFrame, max_rows: int = LEGACY_MAX_ROWS) -> LegacyAssessment:
    reasons: list[str] = []
    if not isinstance(data, pd.DataFrame):
        return LegacyAssessment(False, ("No valid DataFrame was supplied.",), 0)
    if not data.columns.is_unique:
        reasons.append("Duplicate column labels cannot be interpreted safely.")
        return LegacyAssessment(False, tuple(reasons), len(data))
    missing = sorted(LEGACY_COLUMNS.difference(data.columns))
    if missing:
        reasons.append("Practice-workbook columns missing: " + ", ".join(missing))
    if data.empty:
        reasons.append("The active dataset has no records.")
    if len(data) > max_rows:
        reasons.append(f"Legacy tutorial view is limited to {max_rows:,} rows; use Executive Overview for larger datasets.")
    if not missing and not data.empty:
        numeric = {}
        for col in ("Sales", "Total Cost", "Profit", "Profit Margin (%)", "Quantity"):
            values = pd.to_numeric(data[col], errors="coerce")
            if values.isna().any() or not values.map(math.isfinite).all():
                reasons.append(f"{col} has missing, nonnumeric or infinite values.")
            else:
                numeric[col] = values.astype(float)
        if len(numeric) == 5:
            difference = (numeric["Sales"] - numeric["Total Cost"] - numeric["Profit"]).abs()
            if (difference > (0.01 + numeric["Sales"].abs() * 1e-9)).any():
                reasons.append("Precomputed Profit does not reconcile to Sales minus Total Cost.")
            zero_sales = numeric["Sales"].abs().le(1e-12)
            if zero_sales.any():
                reasons.append("The legacy Profit Margin (%) is undefined on zero-sales rows.")
            else:
                calc_margin = numeric["Profit"].div(numeric["Sales"]).mul(100)
                if (calc_margin - numeric["Profit Margin (%)"]).abs().gt(0.15).any():
                    reasons.append("Precomputed Profit Margin (%) does not reconcile to Profit / Sales.")
            if numeric["Quantity"].le(0).any():
                reasons.append("Legacy Quantity-based measures require a positive per-row quantity.")
        parsed = pd.to_datetime(data["Date"], errors="coerce", format="mixed")
        if parsed.isna().any():
            reasons.append("Date contains invalid/missing values incompatible with the legacy date filter.")
    return LegacyAssessment(not reasons, tuple(reasons), len(data))


def render_safe_details(data: pd.DataFrame, assessment: LegacyAssessment) -> None:
    """Optional compatible view of mapped data; no silent financial field guesses."""
    import streamlit as st
    import plotly.express as px
    from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics
    from business_metrics import revenue_by_period
    from audit_exports import excel_safe_csv, build_financial_export

    st.warning("The original Detailed Analytics tutorial is not suitable for this dataset. "
               "A field-mapped, bounded analysis is available below instead.")
    with st.expander("Why the legacy view was not used", expanded=True):
        for reason in assessment.reasons:
            st.write("â€¢ " + reason)
    st.caption("Existing Executive Overview remains the primary cross-dataset Dashboard. "
               "This compatible view does not change your uploaded data.")
    if data.empty or not data.columns.is_unique:
        st.info("Choose a nonempty dataset with unique source field names in Data Workspace.")
        return
    st.subheader("Compatible dataset summary")
    r1, r2, r3 = st.columns(3)
    r1.metric("Records", f"{len(data):,}")
    r2.metric("Fields", str(len(data.columns)))
    r3.metric("Missing cells", f"{int(data.isna().sum().sum()):,}")
    st.dataframe(data.head(150), width="stretch")
    st.caption("The preview is limited to 150 rows; source values have not been modified.")
    numeric = data.select_dtypes(include="number").columns.tolist()
    if not numeric:
        st.info("No numerical fields are available. Convert numeric-looking text in Prepare & Validate.")
        return
    no_field = "â€” Not mapped â€”"
    with st.expander("Map financial fields (optional)", expanded=True):
        revenue = st.selectbox("Revenue field", [no_field]+numeric, key="legacy_safe_revenue")
        cost = st.selectbox("Cost field", [no_field]+[c for c in numeric if c != revenue], key="legacy_safe_cost")
    if revenue == no_field:
        st.info("Choose a revenue field to display monetary KPIs. No currency assumptions are made.")
        return
    if cost == no_field:
        cost = None
    try:
        calculations, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(data, revenue, cost)
    except (ValueError, TypeError, OverflowError) as exc:
        st.error(f"Mapped financial calculations stopped: {exc}")
        return
    c1, c2, c3 = st.columns(3)
    c1.metric("Mapped gross revenue", f"{totals['gross_revenue']:,.2f}")
    c2.metric("Net revenue before other deductions", f"{totals['net_revenue']:,.2f}")
    c3.metric("Profit after mapped costs", f"{totals['profit_after_mapped_costs']:,.2f}" if cost else "Not available")
    st.caption("This view maps only the selected columns. Net profit, taxes, returns and currency "
               "are not inferred. Use Commercial Intelligence for complete financial mapping.")
    category_fields = [c for c in data.columns if c != revenue and (pd.api.types.is_object_dtype(data[c]) or pd.api.types.is_string_dtype(data[c]) or isinstance(data[c].dtype, pd.CategoricalDtype))]
    with st.expander("Category and period overview", expanded=False):
        if category_fields:
            category = st.selectbox("Category field", [no_field]+category_fields, key="legacy_safe_category")
            if category != no_field:
                grouped = pd.DataFrame({"Category": data[category].astype('string').fillna('(Missing)').to_numpy(),
                                        "Revenue": calculations["Net Revenue"].to_numpy()})
                grouped = grouped.groupby('Category', as_index=False, dropna=False)['Revenue'].sum().sort_values('Revenue', ascending=False).head(12)
                st.plotly_chart(px.bar(grouped, x="Category", y="Revenue"), width="stretch")
        dates = [c for c in data.columns if 'date' in str(c).casefold() or pd.api.types.is_datetime64_any_dtype(data[c])]
        if dates:
            selected_date = st.selectbox("Date field", [no_field]+dates, key="legacy_safe_date")
            if selected_date != no_field:
                try:
                    series, omitted = revenue_by_period(data[selected_date], calculations['Net Revenue'])
                    if omitted:
                        st.warning(f"{omitted:,} invalid/missing dates excluded from period chart, not KPIs.")
                    if not series.empty:
                        st.plotly_chart(px.line(series, x="Period", y="Revenue"), width="stretch")
                except (ValueError, TypeError) as exc:
                    st.warning(f"Time chart unavailable: {exc}")
    export_cap = 50_000
    if len(data) > export_cap:
        st.warning(f"Bounded CSV export: the first {export_cap:,} of {len(data):,} records only. "
                   "For full-dataset exports, use Data Workspace with its stated export limits.")
    st.download_button("Download mapped audit CSV", data=excel_safe_csv(build_financial_export(
                       data.head(export_cap), calculations.head(export_cap))),
                       file_name="python_bi_compatible_details.csv", mime="text/csv", key="legacy_safe_export")


========== FILE: sector_acceptance.py ==========

"""Cross-sector acceptance with independent arithmetic reconciliation.

Results never contain source records or dataset-specific names outside synthetic labels.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import pandas as pd
from sector_fixtures import make_sector_fixture
from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics
from performance_insights import category_profitability
from business_metrics import apply_dashboard_filters, revenue_by_period
from scalable_analytics import category_chart_data, time_chart_data
from audit_exports import build_financial_export, excel_safe_csv
from report_engine import build_executive_report


def _close(a, b, name):
    if not math.isclose(float(a), float(b), rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=.01):
        raise AssertionError(f'{name} did not reconcile.')


def check_one_sector(name: str, rows: int = 120) -> dict:
    case = make_sector_fixture(name, rows)
    data = case.frame
    original = data.copy(deep=True)
    audit, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(
        data, case.revenue, case.cost, case.discount, case.returns, case.operating, case.taxes,
        complete_costs=True,
    )
    gross = data[case.revenue].sum()
    net = gross - data[case.discount].sum() - data[case.returns].sum()
    costs = data[[case.cost,case.operating,case.taxes]].sum().sum()
    _close(totals['gross_revenue'], gross, 'gross revenue')
    _close(totals['net_revenue'], net, 'net revenue')
    _close(totals['mapped_costs'], costs, 'mapped costs')
    _close(totals['net_profit'], net - costs, 'synthetic fully-mapped profit')
    grouped = category_profitability(data,audit,case.category)
    _close(grouped['Net Revenue'].sum(),net,'category revenue')
    _close(grouped['Profit After Mapped Costs'].sum(),net-costs,'category profit')
    periods, missing = revenue_by_period(data[case.date],audit['Net Revenue'],'Monthly')
    assert missing == 0 and len(periods) >= 3
    _close(periods['Revenue'].sum(),net,'period revenue')
    chosen = apply_dashboard_filters(data,category_column=case.category,categories=[str(data[case.category].iloc[0])])
    assert 0 < len(chosen) < len(data)
    view = build_financial_export(data,audit)
    assert len(view)==rows and 'BI_Net_Revenue' in view.columns
    assert excel_safe_csv(view).startswith(b'\xef\xbb\xbf')
    bar, bmeta = category_chart_data(data,audit,case.category,'Calculated: Net Revenue',top_n=12)
    _close(bar['Value'].sum(),net,'category chart')
    line, lmeta = time_chart_data(data,audit,case.date,'Calculated: Net Revenue','Monthly')
    _close(line['Value'].sum(),net,'line chart')
    document=build_executive_report(data,audit,totals,dataset_name='Fictional Sector Fixture',
                                   category=case.category,date_column=case.date,period='Monthly',
                                   field_mapping={'revenue':case.revenue,'cost':case.cost},
                                   filter_notes='No filters â€” fictional dataset')
    assert b'<html' in document['html'].lower()
    assert len(document['summary'])>0
    invalid=data.copy()
    invalid.loc[0,case.cost]=float('nan')
    try:
        calculate_commercial_metrics(invalid,case.revenue,case.cost)
    except ValueError:
        pass
    else:
        raise AssertionError('Invalid financial input should be rejected')
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(data, original)
    return {'sector': name,'rows':rows,'gross_revenue_reconciled':True,
            'net_revenue_reconciled':True,'mapped_costs_reconciled':True,
            'synthetic_net_profit_reconciled':True,'category_reconciled':True,
            'time_reconciled':True,'charts_reconciled':True,
            'report_generated':True,'invalid_input_rejected':True,
            'source_unchanged':True, 'status':'PASS'}


def run_sector_acceptance(rows: int = 120) -> list[dict]:
    return [check_one_sector(s,rows) for s in ('Retail','Services','Manufacturing')]


========== FILE: sector_fixtures.py ==========

"""Deterministic fictional sector datasets â€” never real company records."""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SectorFixture:
    name: str
    frame: pd.DataFrame
    revenue: str
    cost: str
    discount: str
    returns: str
    operating: str
    taxes: str
    category: str
    date: str = 'Date'


def make_sector_fixture(sector: str, rows: int = 120) -> SectorFixture:
    if sector not in ('Retail', 'Services', 'Manufacturing'):
        raise ValueError('Choose Retail, Services or Manufacturing.')
    if not isinstance(rows, int) or not 12 <= rows <= 100_000:
        raise ValueError('Rows must be between 12 and 100,000.')
    n = np.arange(rows)
    dates = pd.Timestamp('2026-01-01') + pd.to_timedelta(n % 180, unit='D')
    if sector == 'Retail':
        category, values = 'ProductCategory', np.take(['Computing','Accessories','Displays'], n % 3)
        revenue, cost, discount, returned, opex, tax = 'GrossSales','CostOfGoods','Discounts','Returns','StoreExpenses','TaxExpense'
        gross = (100 + (n % 7) * 23).astype(float)
        cogs = (55 + (n % 11) * 9).astype(float)
        disc, ret = (n % 4).astype(float), (n % 9 == 0).astype(float) * 12
        expenses = np.full(rows, 6.0)
        taxes = np.full(rows, 3.0)
    elif sector == 'Services':
        category, values = 'ServiceLine', np.take(['Support','Analytics','Consulting'], n % 3)
        revenue, cost, discount, returned, opex, tax = 'ServiceFees','DeliveryCosts','ClientCredits','Refunds','OfficeExpenses','TaxesPaid'
        gross = (200 + (n % 8) * 20).astype(float)
        cogs = (60 + (n % 9) * 8).astype(float)
        disc, ret = (n % 5).astype(float), np.zeros(rows)
        expenses = np.full(rows, 15.0)
        taxes = np.full(rows, 7.0)
    else:
        category, values = 'ProductionLine', np.take(['Components','Hardware','Assemblies'], n % 3)
        revenue, cost, discount, returned, opex, tax = 'InvoiceValue','ProductionCost','TradeDiscount','RejectedReturns','FactoryExpenses','BusinessTax'
        gross = (350 + (n % 9) * 30).astype(float)
        cogs = (225 + (n % 10) * 25).astype(float)
        disc, ret = (n % 3).astype(float) * 4, (n % 19 == 0).astype(float) * 20
        expenses = np.full(rows, 22.0)
        taxes = np.full(rows, 11.0)
    df = pd.DataFrame({'RecordID': [f'FAKE-{sector[:3].upper()}-{i:06d}' for i in n],
                       'Date': dates, category: values, revenue: gross, cost: cogs,
                       discount: disc, returned: ret, opex: expenses, tax: taxes})
    return SectorFixture(sector, df, revenue, cost, discount, returned, opex, tax, category)


def write_demo_csvs(folder, rows: int = 120) -> list[str]:
    from pathlib import Path
    target = Path(folder)
    target.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    filenames=[]
    for sector in ('Retail','Services','Manufacturing'):
        item = make_sector_fixture(sector, rows)
        path = target / f'FAKE_{sector.lower()}_{rows}.csv'
        item.frame.to_csv(path,index=False)
        filenames.append(str(path))
    return filenames


========== FILE: start_local.py ==========

"""Launch the existing Streamlit app on loopback only. Does NOT deploy to cloud."""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import sys


def command(root: Path):
    app = Path(root) / 'app.py'
    if not app.is_file():
        raise FileNotFoundError('app.py must exist in the project root.')
    return [sys.executable, '-m', 'streamlit', 'run', str(app),
            '--server.address', '127.0.0.1', '--server.port', '8501',
            '--browser.gatherUsageStats', 'false']


def main():
    root = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    print('LOCAL MODE ONLY: http://127.0.0.1:8501 (no public deployment)')
    return subprocess.call(command(root),cwd=root)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())


========== FILE: summarize_browser_timings.py ==========

"""Summarise optional local server-side Streamlit timings after manual browser checks."""
from __future__ import annotations
import json
from pathlib import Path
from ui_runtime_metrics import summarize

if __name__=='__main__':
    report=summarize()
    out=Path(__file__).resolve().parent/'acceptance_results'/'UI_SERVER_TIMING_SUMMARY.json'
    out.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2),encoding='utf-8')
    print('Server-side render function samples:',report['samples'],' errors:',report['errors'])
    for page in report['pages']:
        print(f"{page['page']}: {page['observations']} samples; max rows {page['max_rows_observed']:,}; "
              f"median {page['median_server_seconds']:.2f}s; max {page['max_server_seconds']:.2f}s")
    print('NOTE: no browser-paint, download/network, or complete cleaning-page latency is measured.')
    print('Report:',out)

"""Dataset quality contract tests on deliberately duplicated/missing rows."""
import pandas as pd
from assurance_contracts import inspect_dataset, schema_fingerprint, readiness_notes

def main():
    data=pd.DataFrame({'Sales':[10,10,None,5], 'Product':['A','A',None,'B']})
    before=data.copy(deep=True)
    sample, table=inspect_dataset(data,sample_size=100,exact=False)
    assert sample['rows']==4 and sample['columns']==2 and sample['sample_rows']==4
    assert sample['scope']=='sample_only' and sample['checked_missing_cells']==2
    exact, fields=inspect_dataset(data,sample_size=2,exact=True)
    assert exact['checked_duplicate_rows']==1 and exact['checked_missing_cells']==2
    assert fields['Checked scope'].eq('All source rows').all()
    assert len(schema_fingerprint(data))==16
    assert inspect_dataset(None)[0]['status']=='no_active_dataset'
    try: inspect_dataset(data,sample_size=0)
    except ValueError: pass
    else: raise AssertionError('Invalid sample size accepted')
    try: inspect_dataset(data.rename(columns={'Product':'Sales'}))
    except ValueError:pass
    else: raise AssertionError('Duplicate names accepted')
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(data,before)
    print('PASS: Deterministic quality scope, exact counts, duplicate labels, source immutability')

========== FILE: test_assurance_reports.py ==========

"""Ensure audit bundles are parseable, escaped and do not contain raw data."""
from io import BytesIO
from zipfile import ZipFile
import json
from assurance_reports import report_document, render_html, bundle_audit

def main():
    checks=[{'id':'x<','area':'dataset','status':'PASS','detail':'<script>alert(1)</script>'},
            {'id':'manual','area':'UI','status':'MANUAL','detail':'Browser review pending'}]
    report=report_document(checks,scope='Phases 4 and 5')
    assert report['counts']['PASS']==1 and report['counts']['MANUAL']==1
    assert report['release_gate']=='REVIEW_REQUIRED'
    html=render_html(report)
    assert '<script>' not in html and '&lt;script&gt;' in html
    blob=bundle_audit(report)
    with ZipFile(BytesIO(blob)) as archive:
        assert set(archive.namelist())=={'acceptance_summary.json','acceptance_summary.html','README.txt'}
        parsed=json.loads(archive.read('acceptance_summary.json'))
        assert parsed['counts']==report['counts']
    fail=report_document([{'id':'fail','area':'x','status':'FAIL','detail':'Error'}],scope='test')
    assert fail['release_gate']=='BLOCKED'
    try: report_document([{'id':'x','area':'x','status':'UNKNOWN','detail':''}],scope='test')
    except ValueError:pass
    else: raise AssertionError('Unknown audit status accepted')
    print('PASS: Audit JSON, escaped offline HTML, ZIP contents and manual release gates')
if __name__=='__main__':main()

"""Lightweight interface wiring test independent of local Streamlit installation."""
from contextlib import nullcontext
import importlib
import pandas as pd
import assurance_ui

class Fake:
    def __init__(self): self.calls=[]
    def expander(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('expander');return nullcontext()
    def caption(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('caption')
    def info(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('info')
    def warning(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('warning')
    def error(self,*a,**k):raise AssertionError(f'Streamlit error: {a}')
    def selectbox(self,label,options,**k):self.calls.append('selectbox');return options[k.get('index',0)]
    def checkbox(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('checkbox');return False
    def columns(self,n):return [self]*n
    def metric(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('metric')
    def dataframe(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('dataframe')
    def download_button(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('download')

def main():
    previous=assurance_ui.st
    fake=Fake()
    try:
        assurance_ui.st=fake
        assurance_ui.render_assurance_home(None)
        assert 'info' in fake.calls
        fake.calls=[]
        assurance_ui.render_assurance_home(pd.DataFrame({'Sales':[1.0,2.0], 'Total Cost':[0.4,0.5]}))
        assert fake.calls.count('metric')==4 and fake.calls.count('download')==1
        assert 'dataframe' in fake.calls and 'error' not in fake.calls
    finally:
        assurance_ui.st=previous
    print('PASS: Home assurance controls, scope labels, preview and safe download')
if __name__=='__main__':main()


========== FILE: test_deployment_audit.py ==========

from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
from deployment_audit import check_deployment, release_gate, SENSITIVE_EXCLUSIONS
from prepare_privacy import add_ignores


def main():
    with TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
        root = Path(tmp)
        (root / '.streamlit').mkdir()
        (root / '.streamlit' / 'config.toml').write_text('[theme]\nbase="light"\n')
        (root / '.gitignore').write_text('stored_datasets/\n')
        report = check_deployment(root)
        assert release_gate(report) == 'BLOCKED_FOR_PUBLIC_DEPLOYMENT'
        assert sum(x['status']=='BLOCKED' for x in report) >= 3
        assert any(x['id']=='PRIVATE-GIT' and x['status']=='REVIEW' for x in report)
        changed, missing = add_ignores(root)
        assert changed and 'stored_datasets/' not in missing
        assert all(x in (root/'.gitignore').read_text() for x in SENSITIVE_EXCLUSIONS)
        changed2, _ = add_ignores(root)
        assert not changed2
        assert any(x['id']=='PRIVATE-GIT' and x['status']=='PASS' for x in check_deployment(root))
        assert (root/'.gitignore').read_text().count('stored_datasets/')==1
    print('PASS: Public release gate, shared-storage blockers, append-only privacy rules, idempotence')

========== FILE: test_explorer_ui_mock.py ==========

"""Mocked Explorer control-flow test (not a substitute for real browser checks)."""
from __future__ import annotations
import sys
import types

import pandas as pd

# Only used when Streamlit is not installed in the test environment.
try:
    import streamlit
except ImportError:
    sys.modules["streamlit"] = types.SimpleNamespace()

import explorer_workspace
from benchmark_explorer import make_synthetic


class Context:
    def __enter__(self):
        return self
    def __exit__(self, *_args):
        return False
    def metric(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
        return None


class FakeUI:
    def __init__(self, term: str):
        self.term = term
        self.metrics = []
        self.downloads = []
        self.tables = []
        self.messages = []
    def caption(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.messages.extend(args)
    def info(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.messages.extend(args)
    def warning(self, *args, **kwargs):
        self.messages.extend(args)
    def error(self, *args, **kwargs):
        raise AssertionError("Unexpected UI error: " + str(args))
    def markdown(self, *_a, **_kw):
        return None
    def columns(self, n, **_kw):
        return [self] * n
    def expander(self, *_a, **_kw):
        return Context()
    def metric(self, title, value, **_kw):
        self.metrics.append((title, str(value)))
    def multiselect(self, _label, options, default=None, **kwargs):
        return list(default or [])
    def text_input(self, _label, **kwargs):
        return self.term
    def selectbox(self, _label, options, index=0, **kwargs):
        return list(options)[index]
    def checkbox(self, _label, value=False, **kwargs):
        return value
    def dataframe(self, frame, **kwargs):
        self.tables.append(frame.copy())
    def download_button(self, _label, data, **kwargs):
        self.downloads.append((kwargs.get("file_name"), data))


def run():
    source = make_synthetic(1200)
    before = source.tail(1).copy(deep=True)
    fake = FakeUI("SPECIAL-ROW-000001199")
    explorer_workspace.st = fake
    explorer_workspace.render_explorer_workspace(source)
    assert ("Full-dataset matches", "1") in fake.metrics
    assert ("Records shown", "1") in fake.metrics
    assert fake.tables[0]["Customer Name"].iloc[0] == "SPECIAL-ROW-000001199"
    assert fake.downloads and fake.downloads[0][0] == "python_bi_full_dataset_explorer.csv"
    assert b"SPECIAL-ROW-000001199" in fake.downloads[0][1]
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(source.tail(1), before)
    print("PASS: Mocked Explorer UI controls, full-search count, preview and CSV export")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

========== FILE: test_investment_forecasting.py ==========

"""Unit and integration checks for Phase 6 combined Batches 3 + 4."""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd
from pandas.testing import assert_frame_equal

from forecast_engine import (
    backtest_expanding_mean,
    evaluate_cashflow_stress,
    simulate_historical_bootstrap,
)

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def market_history(n: int = 121) -> pd.DataFrame:
    returns = np.array([0.006 + 0.004 * np.sin(k / 5) for k in range(1, n)], dtype=float)
    prices = np.concatenate(([100.0], 100.0 * np.cumprod(1 + returns)))
    return pd.DataFrame({"Date": pd.bdate_range("2025-01-01", periods=n, tz="UTC"), "Price": prices})


class CashflowStressTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_npv_scenario_reconciliation(self):
        summary, audit = evaluate_cashflow_stress(100, [110], 10, 20, 20)
        npvs = summary.set_index("Scenario")["NPV"]
        self.assertAlmostEqual(npvs["Base"], 0, places=8)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(npvs["Downside"], -20, places=8)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(npvs["Upside"], 20, places=8)
        self.assertEqual(len(audit), 3)

    def test_negative_cost_shocks_are_conservative(self):
        summary, audit = evaluate_cashflow_stress(100, [-20, 150], 10, 30, 20)
        vals = audit.pivot(index="Year", columns="Scenario", values="Incremental Net Cash Flow")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(vals.loc[1, "Downside"], -26)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(vals.loc[1, "Upside"], -16)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(vals.loc[2, "Downside"], 105)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(vals.loc[2, "Upside"], 180)
        self.assertLess(summary.iloc[0]["NPV"], summary.iloc[1]["NPV"])

    def test_invalid_stress_rejected(self):
        for case in (-1, 120, float("nan"), True):
            with self.subTest(case=case), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                evaluate_cashflow_stress(100, [110], 10, case, 20)

    def test_invalid_cashflow_rejected(self):
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "finite"):
            evaluate_cashflow_stress(100, [float("inf")], 10)


class MarketBootstrapTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_seed_reproducibility_and_no_mutation(self):
        source = market_history()
        baseline = source.copy(deep=True)
        a, path_a = simulate_historical_bootstrap(source, "Daily", 12, 1000, 100, 42)
        b, path_b = simulate_historical_bootstrap(source, "Daily", 12, 1000, 100, 42)
        self.assertEqual(a, b)
        assert_frame_equal(path_a, path_b)
        assert_frame_equal(source, baseline)
        self.assertEqual(len(path_a), 13)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(path_a.iloc[0]["Simulation Median"], 100)
        self.assertLessEqual(a["terminal_value_p05"], a["terminal_value_median"])
        self.assertLessEqual(a["terminal_value_median"], a["terminal_value_p95"])
        self.assertFalse(a["calibrated_prediction"])

    def test_flat_prices_return_constant_distribution(self):
        source = market_history()
        source["Price"] = 100.0
        report, paths = simulate_historical_bootstrap(source, "Daily", 20, 500, 250, 7)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(report["terminal_value_median"], 250, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(report["simulated_share_finishing_below_start_pct"], 0.0)
        self.assertTrue((paths["Simulation P05"] == 250).all())

    def test_simulated_loss_can_appear(self):
        source = market_history()
        source["Price"] = 100.0 * 0.99 ** np.arange(len(source))
        report, _ = simulate_historical_bootstrap(source, "Daily", 10, 500, 100, 7)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(report["simulated_share_finishing_below_start_pct"], 100.0)

    def test_invalid_inputs_and_bounded_resources(self):
        source = market_history()
        cases = [(0, 500, 100, 42), (61, 500, 100, 42), (12, 5001, 100, 42),
                 (12, 500, -100, 42), (12, 500, 100, -2)]
        for h, p, cap, seed in cases:
            with self.subTest(case=(h, p, cap, seed)), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                simulate_historical_bootstrap(source, "Daily", h, p, cap, seed)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Frequency"):
            simulate_historical_bootstrap(source, "Annual")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "60"):
            simulate_historical_bootstrap(source.head(55), "Daily")

    def test_invalid_historical_rows_rejected(self):
        source = market_history()
        source.loc[10, "Price"] = -1
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "positive"):
            simulate_historical_bootstrap(source, "Daily")
        source = market_history().iloc[::-1].reset_index(drop=True)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "chronological"):
            simulate_historical_bootstrap(source, "Daily")


class ExpandingBacktestTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_flat_prices_both_zero_error(self):
        source = market_history()
        source["Price"] = 100.0
        summary, rows = backtest_expanding_mean(source)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(summary["mean_forecast_mae_pct_points"], 0)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(summary["flat_baseline_mae_pct_points"], 0)
        self.assertFalse(summary["mean_beats_flat_baseline_on_mae"])
        self.assertTrue((rows["Prior-Mean Forecast %"] == 0).all())

    def test_lookahead_guard(self):
        source = market_history()
        a, rows_a = backtest_expanding_mean(source)
        source.loc[source.index[-1], "Price"] *= 10
        b, rows_b = backtest_expanding_mean(source)
        # Last realized return changed, but no forecast may use that return.
        self.assertAlmostEqual(rows_a.iloc[-1]["Prior-Mean Forecast %"],
                               rows_b.iloc[-1]["Prior-Mean Forecast %"])
        self.assertAlmostEqual(rows_a.iloc[0]["Prior-Mean Forecast %"],
                               rows_b.iloc[0]["Prior-Mean Forecast %"])
        self.assertNotEqual(rows_a.iloc[-1]["Actual Period Return %"],
                            rows_b.iloc[-1]["Actual Period Return %"])
        self.assertTrue((rows_a["Prior Observations Used"] >= 40).all())
        self.assertFalse(a["machine_learning_model"])
        self.assertEqual(a["test_observations"], 60)

    def test_short_history_and_limits(self):
        source = market_history().head(50)
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "20 evaluation"):
            backtest_expanding_mean(source)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            backtest_expanding_mean(market_history(), 5)
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            backtest_expanding_mean(market_history(), 40, 500)

    def test_monotonic_loss_pattern(self):
        source = market_history()
        source["Price"] = 100.0 * 0.99 ** np.arange(len(source))
        summary, _ = backtest_expanding_mean(source)
        self.assertLess(summary["mean_forecast_mae_pct_points"],
                        summary["flat_baseline_mae_pct_points"])


class WiringTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_foundation_code_preserved_and_new_panels_connected(self):
        code = (ROOT / "investment_workspace.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        ast.parse(code)
        self.assertIn("render_business_stress_test(initial", code)
        self.assertIn("render_market_research(history, freq,", code)
        self.assertIn("evaluate_market_history", code)
        self.assertIn("evaluate_project", code)
        self.assertIn("io.BytesIO(uploaded.getvalue())", code)
        self.assertNotIn("save_active_dataset", code)
        for name in ("forecast_panel.py", "forecast_engine.py"):
            ast.parse((ROOT / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main(verbosity=2)


========== FILE: test_investment_foundation.py ==========

"""Offline tests for Phase 6 investment foundation. No Streamlit or internet required."""
from __future__ import annotations

import ast
from pathlib import Path
import unittest

import pandas as pd
from pandas.testing import assert_frame_equal

from investment_engine import (
    evaluate_project, evaluate_market_history, summarize_business_evidence,
)


ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


class ProjectScenarioTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_known_npv_irr_and_payback(self):
        out, annual = evaluate_project(100, [110], 10)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["npv"], 0.0, places=8)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["irr_pct"], 10.0, places=7)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["simple_payback_years"], 100 / 110, places=9)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["discounted_payback_years"], 1.0, places=8)
        self.assertEqual(len(annual), 1)
        self.assertTrue(out["scenario_only"])

    def test_two_year_npv(self):
        out, _ = evaluate_project(100, [60, 60], 10)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["npv"], 4.13223140496, places=8)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["irr_pct"], 13.06623863, places=6)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["simple_payback_years"], 1 + (40 / 60), places=9)

    def test_negative_and_no_payback(self):
        out, _ = evaluate_project(100, [-10, 10, -10], 10)
        self.assertIsNone(out["simple_payback_years"])
        self.assertIsNone(out["discounted_payback_years"])
        self.assertIsNone(out["irr_pct"])

    def test_nonconventional_irr_omitted(self):
        out, _ = evaluate_project(100, [230, -132], 10)
        self.assertIsNone(out["irr_pct"])
        self.assertIn("multiple", out["irr_note"])

    def test_invalid_inputs_rejected(self):
        cases = [(0, [50], 10), (100, [], 10), (100, [float("nan")], 10), (100, [30], -100), (100, [30], float("inf"))]
        for initial, flows, rate in cases:
            with self.subTest(initial=initial, flows=flows, rate=rate), self.assertRaises(ValueError):
                evaluate_project(initial, flows, rate)


class EvidenceTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_valid_business_evidence(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"Sales": [100, 200], "Total Cost": [30, 80]})
        original = df.copy(deep=True)
        e = summarize_business_evidence(df, "Sales", "Total Cost")
        self.assertEqual(e["observed_revenue"], 300)
        self.assertEqual(e["observed_mapped_cost"], 110)
        self.assertEqual(e["profit_after_mapped_cost"], 190)
        assert_frame_equal(df, original)

    def test_missing_financial_data_fails(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"Sales": [100, None], "Cost": [40, 20]})
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "missing"):
            summarize_business_evidence(df, "Sales", "Cost")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "separate"):
            summarize_business_evidence(df, "Cost", "Cost")


class MarketHistoryTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_price_return_and_drawdown(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({"Date": ["2026-01-15", "2026-01-01", "2026-01-08"], "Close": [99, 100, 110]})
        original = df.copy(deep=True)
        out, series = evaluate_market_history(df, "Date", "Close", "Weekly")
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["price_return_pct"], -1.0, places=8)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(out["max_observed_drawdown_pct"], -10.0, places=8)
        self.assertEqual(out["records"], 3)
        self.assertTrue(out["frequency_consistent"])
        self.assertIsNone(out["annualized_volatility_pct"])
        self.assertEqual(series["Price"].tolist(), [100, 110, 99])
        assert_frame_equal(df, original)

    def test_date_duplicate_and_invalid_price_rejected(self):
        duplicate = pd.DataFrame({"Date": ["2026-01-01", "2026-01-01"], "Price": [100, 101]})
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "duplicate"):
            evaluate_market_history(duplicate, "Date", "Price")
        invalid = pd.DataFrame({"Date": ["2026-01-01", "2026-01-02"], "Price": [100, 0]})
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Invalid rows"):
            evaluate_market_history(invalid, "Date", "Price")

    def test_daily_volatility_and_cadence(self):
        df = pd.DataFrame({
            "Date": pd.bdate_range("2026-01-01", periods=55),
            "Close": [100 + 0.4 * i + (i % 7) * 0.5 for i in range(55)],
        })
        out, _ = evaluate_market_history(df, "Date", "Close", "Daily", 2.0)
        self.assertIsNotNone(out["annualized_volatility_pct"])
        self.assertIsNotNone(out["annualized_sharpe"])
        self.assertIsNone(out["annualized_price_cagr_pct"])
        wrong, _ = evaluate_market_history(df, "Date", "Close", "Monthly")
        self.assertFalse(wrong["frequency_consistent"])
        self.assertIsNone(wrong["annualized_sharpe"])

    def test_limit_and_missing_dates_rejected(self):
        bad = pd.DataFrame({"date": ["2026-01-01", "invalid"], "price": [100, 101]})
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Invalid rows"):
            evaluate_market_history(bad, "date", "price")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "Frequency"):
            evaluate_market_history(bad, "date", "price", "Yearly")


class WiringTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_app_routes_preserved_and_added(self):
        app = (ROOT / "app.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")

        self.assertIn('render_executive_dashboard(', app)
        self.assertIn('save_active_dataset(', app)
        self.assertIn('render_investment_intelligence(', app)
        self.assertNotIn('save_active_dataset(uploaded)', (ROOT / 'investment_workspace.py').read_text())

    def test_no_unexpected_imports(self):
        for filename in ("app.py", "investment_engine.py", "investment_workspace.py", "home_experience.py"):
            compile((ROOT / filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8"), filename, "exec")

========== FILE: test_local_file_guard.py ==========

from io import BytesIO
from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
import zipfile
import openpyxl
from local_file_guard import validate_upload, safe_stored_path

class Upload:
    def __init__(self,name,raw):self.name=name;self._raw=raw;self.size=len(raw)
    def getvalue(self):return self._raw

def rejects(call):
    try: call()
    except (ValueError,FileNotFoundError): return
    raise AssertionError('Unsafe input was accepted')

def main():
    x=validate_upload(Upload('good.csv',b'A,B\n1,2\n'));assert x['size_bytes']==8
    rejects(lambda:validate_upload(Upload('../evil.csv',b'a,b\n1,2')))
    rejects(lambda:validate_upload(Upload('bad.csv',b'PK\x03\x04PAYLOAD')))
    rejects(lambda:validate_upload(Upload('bad.csv',b'X\x00Y')))
    rejects(lambda:validate_upload(Upload('bad.exe',b'Hello')))
    rejects(lambda:validate_upload(Upload('bad.xlsx',b'not a workbook')))
    rejects(lambda:validate_upload(Upload('big.csv',b'1'*2048),max_mib=0))
    book=openpyxl.Workbook(); book.active['A1']='Test'; stream=BytesIO();book.save(stream)
    result=validate_upload(Upload('good.xlsx',stream.getvalue()))
    assert result['extension']=='.xlsx'
    with TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
        folder=Path(temp)
        f=safe_stored_path(folder,'0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef.csv')
        f.write_text('a\n1\n')
        assert safe_stored_path(folder,f.name,must_exist=True)==f
        rejects(lambda:safe_stored_path(folder,'../data.csv'))
        rejects(lambda:safe_stored_path(folder,'..bad.csv'))
        rejects(lambda:safe_stored_path(folder,'file.exe'))
        rejects(lambda:safe_stored_path(folder,'missing.csv',must_exist=True))
    print('PASS: Local CSV/XLSX validation, size rules, safe Dataset Library paths')
if __name__=='__main__':main()

========== FILE: test_local_recovery.py ==========

from pathlib import Path
from tempfile import TemporaryDirectory
import json,hashlib
from local_recovery_audit import audit_dataset_library

def main():
    with TemporaryDirectory() as temp:
        root=Path(temp); d=root/'stored_datasets'; d.mkdir()
        a=audit_dataset_library(root)
        assert a['saved_file_count']==0 and a['active_integrity']=='NOT_CHECKED'
        name='abcd1234abcd1234abcd1234abcd1234.csv'; data=b'A,B\n1,2\n'
        (d/name).write_bytes(data)
        (d/'active_dataset.json').write_text(json.dumps({'stored_name':name,'hash':hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()}))
        report=audit_dataset_library(root)
        assert report['active_integrity']=='PASS' and report['saved_file_count']==1
        (d/name).write_bytes(b'A,B\n3,4\n')
        assert audit_dataset_library(root)['active_integrity']=='HASH_MISMATCH'
        (d/'active_dataset.json').write_text(json.dumps({'stored_name':'../../private.csv'}))
        assert audit_dataset_library(root)['active_integrity']=='REVIEW_REQUIRED'
    print('PASS: Read-only Dataset Library recovery/integrity checks')
if __name__=='__main__':main()
