========== FILE: test_operations_ui_mock.py ==========

from contextlib import nullcontext
try:
    import streamlit  # noqa: F401
except ImportError:
    import sys, types
    sys.modules['streamlit'] = types.SimpleNamespace()
import operations_ui

class Fake:
    def __init__(self): self.calls=[]
    def expander(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('expander');return nullcontext()
    def caption(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('caption')
    def columns(self,n):return [self]*n
    def metric(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('metric')
    def warning(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('warning')
    def dataframe(self,*a,**k):self.calls.append('dataframe')
    def download_button(self,*a,**k):
        self.calls.append('download')
        assert k['mime']=='application/zip' and len(k['data'])>100


def main():
    prior=operations_ui.st
    fake=Fake()
    try:
        operations_ui.st=fake
        operations_ui.render_operations_home()
        assert fake.calls.count('metric')==3
        assert 'expander' in fake.calls and 'warning' in fake.calls
        assert fake.calls.count('download')==1
    finally: operations_ui.st=prior
    print('PASS: Home release-readiness UI mock, blockers, evidence export')

if __name__=='__main__':main()


========== FILE: test_phase3_batch1112.py ==========

"""Pure tests for Batch 11 (audited report) and Batch 12 (Home integration).
Run: python test_phase3_batch1112.py
Real Streamlit page interaction must also be tested once in the browser.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
import math
import sys
import types

import pandas as pd

# This calculation logic can run even where the optional Streamlit UI is not installed.
try:
    import streamlit  # noqa: F401
except ImportError:
    sys.modules["streamlit"] = types.SimpleNamespace()

from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics
from report_engine import build_executive_report


def failed(function):
    try:
        function()
    except ValueError:
        return
    raise AssertionError("Expected a ValueError")


def close(a, b):
    assert math.isclose(float(a), float(b), rel_tol=1e-10, abs_tol=.01), (a, b)


def test_from_sample_workbook():
    workbook = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "Data" / "Python_BI_Complete_Business_Dataset.xlsx"
    if not workbook.exists():
        print("Bundled workbook not present; pure tests continue.")
        return
    source = pd.read_excel(workbook, sheet_name="Business Data")
    original = source.copy(deep=True)
    calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source, "Sales", "Total Cost")
    bundle = build_executive_report(
        source, calc, totals,
        dataset_name="Python BI Business Transactions 2026", category="Product",
        date_column="Date", period="Monthly",
        field_mapping={"Gross revenue": "Sales", "Cost of sales": "Total Cost"},
        filter_notes="All source records", currency_label="Unspecified",
        generated_at=datetime(2026, 11, 7, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )
    close(totals["net_revenue"], 293300)
    close(totals["mapped_costs"], 231800)
    close(totals["profit_after_mapped_costs"], 61500)
    assert bundle["matching_rows"] == 20
    assert bundle["missing_date_rows"] == 0
    close(bundle["categories"]["Net Revenue"].sum(), 293300)
    close(bundle["categories"]["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum(), 61500)
    assert bundle["periods"]["Period"].tolist() == ["2026-01"]
    close(bundle["periods"]["Revenue"].sum(), 293300)
    html = bundle["html"].decode("utf-8")
    assert "Business Performance Report" in html
    assert "01. Financial measures" in html and "04. Field mapping and assumptions" in html
    assert "2026-01" in html and "Save as PDF" in html
    assert "2026-11-07" not in html  # printed date deliberately human-readable, not raw string
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(source, original)
    print("Practice workbook: 20 records; revenue/cost/profit/category/time totals reconcile")


def test_malicious_text_and_missing_dates():
    source = pd.DataFrame({
        "Date": ["2026-01-01", "2026-02-01", "BAD"],
        "Product": ["Widget", "<script>alert('x')</script>", None],
        "Sales": [100.0, 210.0, 90.0],
        "Cost": [30.0, 300.0, 20.0],
    }, index=[4, 4, 9])
    before = source.copy(deep=True)
    calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source, "Sales", "Cost")
    bundle = build_executive_report(
        source, calc, totals,
        dataset_name='<img src=x onerror="alert(1)">', category="Product",
        date_column="Date", period="Monthly",
        field_mapping={"Gross revenue": "Sales", "Injected key": "<b>unsafe</b>"},
        filter_notes='City = "<script>"',
        generated_at=datetime(2026, 11, 7, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )
    html = bundle["html"].decode("utf-8")
    assert "<script>alert" not in html and "&lt;script&gt;alert" in html
    assert '<img src=x onerror' not in html and '&lt;img src=x onerror' in html
    assert "<b>unsafe</b>" not in html and "&lt;b&gt;unsafe&lt;/b&gt;" in html
    assert "(Missing)" in html
    assert bundle["missing_date_rows"] == 1
    assert bundle["periods"]["Period"].tolist() == ["2026-01", "2026-02"]
    close(bundle["periods"]["Revenue"].sum(), 310)
    close(bundle["categories"]["Net Revenue"].sum(), 400)
    close(bundle["categories"]["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum(), 50)
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(source, before)

    # Bad mapping / report totals / row-count mismatch must stop a report.
    defaults = dict(dataset_name="X", category="Product", date_column="Date", period="Monthly",
                    field_mapping={"Gross revenue":"Sales"}, filter_notes="All")
    failed(lambda: build_executive_report(source, calc.iloc[:2], totals, **defaults))
    failed(lambda: build_executive_report(source, calc, dict(totals, net_revenue=9), **defaults))
    failed(lambda: build_executive_report(source, calc, totals, **dict(defaults, period="Weekly")))
    failed(lambda: build_executive_report(source, calc, totals, **dict(defaults, category="Absent")))
    # Missing expenses: do NOT create pretend profit or margin.
    no_cost_calc, no_cost_totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source, "Sales")
    no_cost = build_executive_report(source, no_cost_calc, no_cost_totals, **defaults)
    assert "Profit After Mapped Costs" not in no_cost["categories"].columns
    assert no_cost["summary"].loc[
        no_cost["summary"].Metric == "Profit After Mapped Costs", "Value"
    ].isna().all()
    assert "No cost field was mapped" in no_cost["html"].decode("utf-8")

def test_mocked_ui():
    # Dry-run new widgets without starting the full browser server.
    import executive_report
    import home_experience

    class Context:
        def __enter__(self):
            return self
        def __exit__(self, *_args):
            return False
        def metric(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass

    class FakeStreamlit:
        def __init__(self):
            self.session_state = {}
            self.downloads = []
            self.markups = []
            self.captions = []
        def expander(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            return Context()
        def columns(self, count):
            return [Context() for _ in range(count)]
        def selectbox(self, _label, choices, index=0, **_kwargs):
            return choices[index]
        def text_input(self, _label, value='', **_kwargs):
            return value
        def button(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            return True
        def caption(self, *args, **_kwargs):
            self.captions.extend(args)
        def info(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass
        def markdown(self, *args, **_kwargs):
            self.markups.extend(args)
        def metric(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass
        def dataframe(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass
        def success(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
            pass
        def warning(self, *args, **_kwargs):
            raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected warning: {args}")
        def error(self, *args, **_kwargs):
            raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected error: {args}")
        def download_button(self, *args, **kwargs):
            assert kwargs.get('on_click') == 'ignore', 'Report download should not rerun Streamlit'
            self.downloads.append((args, kwargs))

    fake = FakeStreamlit()
    former_report = executive_report.st
    former_home = home_experience.st
    executive_report.st = fake
    home_experience.st = fake
    try:
        source = pd.DataFrame({
            'Date': ['2026-01-01', '2026-02-01'],
            'Product': ['A','B'], 'Sales': [150.0, 75.0], 'Cost': [50.0,80.0],
        })
        calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source,'Sales','Cost')
        executive_report.render_executive_report(source,calc,totals,
                                                 dataset_name='Test', mapping={'Sales':'Sales'},
                                                 filter_summary='All')
        assert len(fake.downloads) == 5, 'Expected ZIP, HTML and three CSV downloads'
        assert any(item[1].get('file_name') == 'python_bi_executive_report_pack.zip'
                   for item in fake.downloads), 'Audit pack download missing'
        assert any(item[1].get('file_name') == 'python_bi_executive_report.html'
                   for item in fake.downloads)
        fake.session_state['uploaded_df'] = source
        fake.session_state['uploaded_name'] = '<Customer & Company>'
        home_experience.render_home()
        assert any('Clarity in every decision' in text for text in fake.markups)
        assert any('&lt;Customer &amp; Company&gt;' in text for text in fake.markups)
    finally:
        executive_report.st = former_report
        home_experience.st = former_home


if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_from_sample_workbook()
    test_malicious_text_and_missing_dates()
    test_home_code_wiring()
    test_mocked_ui()
    print("PASS: Report reconciliations, escaped HTML, missing dates, missing costs, invalid inputs, source immutability, application integration")

"""Regression checks for Batch13 report ZIP + Batch14 category analysis / scaling.
Use: python test_phase3_batch1314.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import io
import json
from pathlib import Path
import sys

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pandas as pd

# Pure calculation checks also work if Streamlit is not installed in test container.

    import streamlit

    sys.modules["streamlit"] = types.SimpleNamespace()

from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics
from report_engine import build_executive_report

from benchmark_phase3 import benchmark


def assert_raises(func):
    try:
        func()
    except ValueError:
        return
    raise AssertionError("Expected ValueError")


def sample():
    data = pd.DataFrame({
        "Date": ["2026-01-01", "2026-02-01", None, "2026-02-03"],
        "Product": ["Alpha", "Beta", "Alpha", None],
        "Sales": [200.0, 300.0, 400.0, 100.0],
        "Cost": [80.0, 350.0, 300.0, 20.0],
    }, index=[11, 11, 14, 29])
    calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(data, "Sales", "Cost")
    return data, calc, totals


def test_report_pack():
    data, calc, totals = sample()
    orig = data.copy(deep=True)
    report = build_executive_report(
        data, calc, totals,
        dataset_name="<script>Unsafe</script>", category="Product", date_column="Date", period="Monthly",
        field_mapping={"Gross revenue": "Sales", "Cost of sales": "Cost"},
        filter_notes="All records",
        generated_at=datetime(2026, 11, 7, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )
    output = build_report_archive(report, dataset_name="Demo", filter_context="All records",
                                  period="Monthly", category="Product", currency="Not specified")
    verified = verify_report_archive(output)
    assert verified["filtered_row_count"] == 4
    assert verified["includes_transaction_level_data"] is False
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(output)) as z:
        assert "report.html" in z.namelist()
        assert "financial_summary.csv" in z.namelist()
        assert "category_performance.csv" in z.namelist()
        assert "calendar_revenue.csv" in z.namelist()
        assert "manifest.json" in z.namelist()
        assert "<script>Unsafe</script>" not in z.read("report.html").decode("utf-8")
        assert b"Alpha" in z.read("category_performance.csv")
        assert b"2026-02" in z.read("calendar_revenue.csv")
        # Auditable aggregate CSVs must not contain transaction-level IDs.
        assert b"TransactionID" not in z.read("financial_summary.csv")
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(data, orig)
    # Change bytes inside ZIP without updating manifest; verifier must stop.
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(output)) as z:
        members = {name: z.read(name) for name in z.namelist()}
    members["financial_summary.csv"] = b"Tampered data\n"
    buf = io.BytesIO()
    with zipfile.ZipFile(buf, "w") as z:
        for name, value in members.items():
            z.writestr(name, value)
    assert_raises(lambda: verify_report_archive(buf.getvalue()))
    assert_raises(lambda: build_report_archive({"html": b"Not HTML", "summary": pd.DataFrame(),
                                              "categories": pd.DataFrame(), "periods": pd.DataFrame(),
                                              "matching_rows": 1}, dataset_name="X", filter_context="Y",
                                             period="Monthly", category=None, currency="INR"))


def test_portfolio():
    data, calc, totals = sample()
    original = data.copy(deep=True)
    result = build_category_concentration(data, calc, totals, "Product")
    groups, met = result["groups"], result["metrics"]
    assert groups["Category"].tolist() == ["Alpha", "Beta", "(Missing)"]
    assert math.isclose(float(groups["Net Revenue"].sum()), 1000)
    assert math.isclose(float(groups["Mapped Costs"].sum()), 750)
    assert math.isclose(float(groups["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum()), 250)
    assert met["category_count"] == 3
    assert math.isclose(met["top_category_share_pct"], 60)
    assert math.isclose(met["top_three_share_pct"], 100)
    assert met["categories_to_80_pct"] == 2
    assert math.isclose(met["concentration_index"], 4600)
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(data, original)
    assert_raises(lambda: build_category_concentration(data.iloc[:2], calc, totals, "Product"))
    assert_raises(lambda: build_category_concentration(data, calc, dict(totals, net_revenue=1), "Product"))
    assert_raises(lambda: build_category_concentration(data, calc, totals, "Missing Column"))
    neg = pd.DataFrame({"Product": ["A", "B"], "Sales": [400., -100.]})
    neg_calc, neg_total = calculate_commercial_metrics(neg, "Sales")
    result = build_category_concentration(neg, neg_calc, neg_total, "Product")
    assert result["metrics"]["concentration_available"] is False
    assert "Revenue Share %" not in result["groups"]

    res = benchmark(1000)
    assert res["rows"] == 1000
    assert res["reconciled"] is True
    assert 0 < res["selected_rows"] <= 1000
    assert res["dataframe_memory_mib"] > 0
    assert_raises(lambda: benchmark(999))
    assert_raises(lambda: benchmark(500001))



def test_mocked_category_panel():
    import portfolio_panel
    class Context:
        def __enter__(self): return self
        def __exit__(self, *_): return False
        def metric(self, *_, **__): pass
    class Fake:
        def __init__(self):
            self.downloaded = []
            self.charts = 0
        def expander(self, *_, **__): return Context()
        def caption(self, *_, **__): pass
        def selectbox(self, label, options, index=0, **_): return options[index]
        def checkbox(self, *_, **__): return True
        def columns(self, n): return [Context() for _ in range(n)]
        def metric(self, *_, **__): pass
        def plotly_chart(self, *_, **__): self.charts += 1
        def dataframe(self, *_, **__): pass
        def download_button(self, *args, **kwargs): self.downloaded.append((args,kwargs))
        def warning(self, message): raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected warning: {message}")
        def info(self, message): raise AssertionError(f"Unexpected info: {message}")
    src, calc, totals = sample()
    old = portfolio_panel.st
    fake = Fake()
    portfolio_panel.st = fake
    try:
        portfolio_panel.render_portfolio_panel(src, calc, totals)
    finally:
        portfolio_panel.st = old
    assert fake.charts == 1
    assert len(fake.downloaded) == 1
    assert fake.downloaded[0][1]["file_name"] == "python_bi_category_mix.csv"


def test_workbook():
    workbook = Path(__file__).parent / "Data" / "Python_BI_Complete_Business_Dataset.xlsx"
    if not workbook.exists():
        print("Practice workbook not found; synthetic checks still ran")
        return
    data = pd.read_excel(workbook, sheet_name="Business Data")
    calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(data, "Sales", "Total Cost")
    result = build_category_concentration(data, calc, totals, "Product")
    assert math.isclose(float(result["groups"]["Net Revenue"].sum()), 293300)
    assert math.isclose(float(result["groups"]["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum()), 61500)
    assert result["metrics"]["category_count"] == 5
    assert math.isclose(float(result["groups"]["Revenue Share %"].sum()), 100, abs_tol=.005)
    print("Practice workbook reconciled: revenue 293,300 / mapped-cost profit 61,500")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_report_pack()
    test_portfolio()
    test_benchmark()
    test_workbook()
    test_mocked_category_panel()
    print("PASS: Aggregate report ZIP integrity, tamper detection, category shares, reconciliation, bounded synthetic benchmark")

frame = pd.DataFrame({
    'Sales':[100.0,80.0,20.0],
    'Total Cost':[40.0,90.0,10.0],
    'Discounts':[10.0,0.0,0.0],
    'City':['Delhi',None,'Mumbai'],
})

    raise AssertionError('Should reject duplicated mappings')
except ValueError:
    pass

from pathlib import Path

import pandas as pd

base = pd.DataFrame({

    "Product": ["Laptop", "Phone", "Laptop", "Phone"],
    "Sales": [100.0, 200.0, 150.0, 300.0],
    "Total Cost": [60.0, 100.0, 75.0, 120.0],
})

assert filtered["Sales"].sum() == 250

    base, category_column="Product", categories=["Laptop"],
    date_column="Date", start_date=pd.Timestamp("2026-02-01").date(),
    end_date=pd.Timestamp("2026-02-28").date(), numeric_column="Sales",
    numeric_min=120, numeric_max=160,
)

assert monthly["Revenue"].tolist() == [300.0, 150.0, 300.0]
assert round(float(monthly.loc[1, "Growth %"]), 2) == -50.0
assert pd.isna(monthly.loc[2, "Growth %"]), "Growth across a missing month must be undefined."

monthly2, omitted = revenue_by_period(
    pd.Series(["2026-01-01", "not-a-date", "2026-02-01"]),
    pd.Series([0, 200, 20]), "Monthly",
)
assert omitted == 1
assert pd.isna(monthly2.loc[1, "Growth %"]), "Growth after a zero previous period must be undefined."

for unit_price, variable, fixed, expected in [

    (80, 100, 200, None),
    (90, 40, 0, 0),
    (80, 100, 0, None),
]:

    assert calculated["break_even_units"] == expected
assert break_even_scenario(800, 100, 60)["break_even_revenue"] == 2000
for bad_inputs in [(-10, 20, 5), (10, float("inf"), 5), (10, 20, float("nan"))]:
    try:
        break_even_scenario(*bad_inputs)
        raise AssertionError("Invalid input was not rejected")
    except ValueError:
        pass

========== FILE: test_phase3_batch56.py ==========

"""Run with: python test_phase3_batch56.py (no Streamlit required)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import io
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd

from audit_exports import build_financial_export, excel_safe_csv
from performance_insights import category_profitability, loss_making_rows, price_sensitivity
from business_metrics import break_even_scenario


def assert_close(actual, target, message):
    assert abs(float(actual) - float(target)) < 1e-7, message


def test_export_headers_and_source_preserved():
    data = pd.DataFrame({
        "Product": ["A", "B", "C"],
        "Customer": ["=2+2", "@SUM(A1:A2)", "normal"],
        "BI_Net_Revenue": [999, 999, 999],
        "Sales": [120, 30, 40],
    }, index=[10, 10, 99])
    calculations = pd.DataFrame({
        "Gross Revenue": [120, 30, 40],
        "Net Revenue": [120, 30, 40],
        "Mapped Costs": [70, 50, 25],
        "Profit After Mapped Costs": [50, -20, 15],
        "Loss After Mapped Costs": [0, 20, 0],
    })
    original = data.copy(deep=True)
    result = build_financial_export(data, calculations)
    assert len(result) == 3
    assert result.columns.is_unique
    assert "BI_Gross_Revenue" in result
    assert "BI_Net_Revenue_2" in result, "Must not overwrite a real source field"
    assert "BI_Profit_After_Mapped_Costs" in result
    assert result.iloc[1]["BI_Profit_After_Mapped_Costs"] == -20
    csv = excel_safe_csv(result)
    assert csv.startswith(b"\xef\xbb\xbf"), "Excel UTF-8 BOM missing"
    loaded = pd.read_csv(io.BytesIO(csv), encoding="utf-8-sig")
    assert loaded.loc[0, "Customer"] == "'=2+2"
    assert loaded.loc[1, "Customer"] == "'@SUM(A1:A2)"
    assert loaded.loc[2, "Customer"] == "normal"
    assert_close(loaded["BI_Profit_After_Mapped_Costs"].sum(), 45, "Profit changed in export")
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(data, original)
    try:
        build_financial_export(data, calculations.head(2))
        assert False, "Mismatched source/calculated rows were allowed"
    except ValueError:
        pass
    return data, calculations


def test_category_and_exceptions(data, calculations):
    grouping = category_profitability(data, calculations, "Product")
    assert grouping["Records"].sum() == len(data)
    assert_close(grouping["Net Revenue"].sum(), calculations["Net Revenue"].sum(), "Revenue reconciliation")
    assert_close(grouping["Mapped Costs"].sum(), calculations["Mapped Costs"].sum(), "Cost reconciliation")
    assert_close(grouping["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum(), calculations["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum(), "Profit reconciliation")
    exceptions = loss_making_rows(data, calculations)
    assert len(exceptions) == 1
    assert exceptions.iloc[0]["Product"] == "B"
    # Null categories must be visible and zero-revenue margin undefined.
    data_null = pd.DataFrame({"Category": [None, "D"]})
    calc = pd.DataFrame({"Net Revenue": [0, 10], "Mapped Costs": [5, 3], "Profit After Mapped Costs": [-5, 7]})
    null_summary = category_profitability(data_null, calc, "Category")
    missing = null_summary.loc[null_summary["Category"] == "(Missing)"].iloc[0]
    assert pd.isna(missing["Profit Margin %"])
    assert missing["Records"] == 1
    return True


def test_sensitivity():
    report = price_sensitivity(1000, 20, 8)
    assert report["Price Change %"].tolist() == [-10, -5, 0, 5, 10]
    for row in report.itertuples(index=False):
        expected = break_even_scenario(1000, row[1], 8)
        assert row[3] == expected["break_even_units"]
    assert report.loc[report["Price Change %"] == 0, "Break-even Units"].iloc[0] == 84
    assert pd.isna(price_sensitivity(1000, 10, 11).iloc[2]["Break-even Units"])
    assert price_sensitivity(0, 10, 3).loc[2, "Break-even Units"] == 0
    try:
        price_sensitivity(100, 1, 0, percent_changes=(-150,))
        assert False, "Negative price scenario was allowed"
    except ValueError:
        pass


def test_sample_workbook():
    path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "Data" / "Python_BI_Complete_Business_Dataset.xlsx"
    # In the test workspace, a user may have the dataset in a different path.
    if not path.exists():
        return "Sample-workbook integration skipped (project Data workbook not found)."
    source = pd.read_excel(path, sheet_name="Business Data")
    from commerce_engine import calculate_commercial_metrics
    calc, totals = calculate_commercial_metrics(source, "Sales", "Total Cost")
    output = build_financial_export(source, calc)
    summary = category_profitability(source, calc, "Product")
    assert_close(summary["Net Revenue"].sum(), totals["net_revenue"], "Workbook grouped revenue mismatch")
    assert_close(summary["Profit After Mapped Costs"].sum(), totals["profit_after_mapped_costs"], "Workbook grouped profit mismatch")
    assert len(output) == len(source)
    assert len(loss_making_rows(source, calc)) == int(calc["Profit After Mapped Costs"].lt(0).sum())
    return f"Sample-workbook integration validated: {len(source)} rows."


if __name__ == "__main__":
    data, calculated = test_export_headers_and_source_preserved()
    test_category_and_exceptions(data, calculated)
    test_sensitivity()
    print(test_sample_workbook())
    print("PASS: Safe BI_ exports, collision handling, profitability reconciliation, loss exceptions, break-even sensitivity")


========== FILE: test_phase3_batch78.py ==========

"""Regression tests for financial period comparison and sampled profiling.

Run: python test_phase3_batch78.py
Pure computations only; final Streamlit UI check must be done in the browser.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import pandas as pd
from period_comparison import available_periods, compare_periods
from data_readiness import dataset_profile


def close(actual, expected):
    assert math.isclose(float(actual), float(expected), abs_tol=1e-8), (actual, expected)


def test_period_comparison():
    source = pd.DataFrame({
        "Date": ["2026-01-05", "2026-01-14", "2026-02-03", "2026-02-28", "bad", "2026-04-01"],
        "Product": ["A", "B", "A", None, "A", "C"],
        "Sales": [100, 50, 125, 45, 100, 90],
    }, index=[0, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4])
    finance = pd.DataFrame({
        "Gross Revenue": [100, 50, 125, 45, 100, 90],
        "Net Revenue": [100, 50, 125, 45, 100, 90],
        "Mapped Costs": [40, 20, 65, 25, 40, 30],
        "Profit After Mapped Costs": [60, 30, 60, 20, 60, 60],
    }, index=[12, 12, 14, 15, 16, 17])
    original = source.copy(deep=True)
    periods, excluded = available_periods(source, "Date", "Monthly")
    assert periods == ["2026-01", "2026-02", "2026-04"], periods
    assert excluded == 1
    totals, groups, audit = compare_periods(source, finance, "Date", "Monthly", "2026-01", "2026-02", "Product")
    assert audit["periods_are_adjacent"] and audit["earlier_rows"] == 2 and audit["later_rows"] == 2
    assert audit["excluded_invalid_dates"] == 1
    assert audit["excluded_other_period_rows"] == 1
    net = totals[totals["Metric"] == "Net Revenue"].iloc[0]
    close(net["Earlier"], 150)
    close(net["Later"], 170)
    close(net["Change %"], 20 / 150 * 100)
    close(groups["Earlier Revenue"].sum(), 150)
    close(groups["Later Revenue"].sum(), 170)
    close(groups["Earlier Profit"].sum(), 90)
    close(groups["Later Profit"].sum(), 80)
    assert "(Missing)" in groups["Category"].tolist()
    _, _, nonadjacent = compare_periods(source, finance, "Date", "Monthly", "2026-01", "2026-04")
    assert not nonadjacent["periods_are_adjacent"]
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(source, original)
    try:
        compare_periods(source, finance, "Date", "Monthly", "2026-02", "2026-01")
        raise AssertionError("Reverse ordering passed")
    except ValueError:
        pass
    try:
        compare_periods(source, finance.head(2), "Date", "Monthly", "2026-01", "2026-02")
        raise AssertionError("Row-count mismatch passed")
    except ValueError:
        pass
    finance_negative = finance.copy()
    finance_negative.loc[finance_negative.index[0], "Net Revenue"] = -50
    # Entire January prior period becomes nonpositive, so growth is undefined.
    finance_negative.iloc[1, finance_negative.columns.get_loc("Net Revenue")] = -60
    results, _, _ = compare_periods(source, finance_negative, "Date", "Monthly", "2026-01", "2026-02")
    assert pd.isna(results.loc[results.Metric == "Net Revenue", "Change %"].iloc[0])
    return source


def test_profile():
    frame = pd.DataFrame({"A": [1, None, 1, 1], "B": ["x", "y", "x", "x"]}, index=[1, 3, 1, 5])
    original = frame.copy(deep=True)
    values, fields, preview = dataset_profile(frame, sample_limit=10, exact_missing_scan=True)
    assert values["source_rows"] == 4 and values["sample_rows"] == 4
    assert values["exact_missing_cells"] == 1
    assert int(fields.loc[fields.Column == "A", "Exact missing in source"].iloc[0]) == 1
    assert values["sample_duplicate_rows"] == 2
    assert len(preview) == 4
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(frame, original)
    small, fields, preview = dataset_profile(frame, sample_limit=2, exact_missing_scan=False)
    assert small["sample_rows"] == 2 and small["exact_missing_cells"] is None
    assert "Exact missing in source" not in fields.columns
    empty, columns, preview = dataset_profile(frame.head(0), sample_limit=50)
    assert empty["sample_rows"] == 0 and empty["source_rows"] == 0
    big = pd.DataFrame({"K": range(55_000), "Date": ["2026-02-01"] * 55_000})
    summary, cols, sample = dataset_profile(big, sample_limit=3_000)
    assert summary["source_rows"] == 55_000 and summary["sample_rows"] == 3_000
    assert len(sample) == 3_000
    return True


if __name__ == "__main__":
    test_period_comparison()
    test_profile()
    print("PASS: Period comparison reconciliation, invalid dates, negative-baseline guard, sampled profiling, source immutability")


========== FILE: test_phase3_batch910.py ==========

"""Automated Batch 9+10 regression tests (pure computations, not browser tests).

Run from the project root:
    python test_phase3_batch910.py
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import math
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from scalable_analytics import (
    numeric_measure_options, category_chart_data, histogram_chart_data,
    is_ratio_measure, scatter_chart_data, time_chart_data,
)
from performance_lab import benchmark_grouping, MAX_BENCHMARK_ROWS


def close(actual, expected):
    assert math.isclose(float(actual), float(expected), abs_tol=1e-9), (actual, expected)


def fails(function, message=""):
    try:
        function()
    except ValueError:
        return
    raise AssertionError("Expected a validation error: " + message)


def test_chart_math():
    # Deliberately inconsistent and duplicate row indices test positional alignment.
    source = pd.DataFrame({
        "Product": ["A", "B", "A", None, "C", "B"],
        "Date": ["2026-01-02", "2026-01-05", "2026-02-08", "BAD", "2026-02-09", "2026-04-01"],
        "Sales": [100, 200, 300, 400, 500, np.nan],
        "Profit Margin (%)": [10, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15],
    }, index=[7, 7, 1, 2, 2, 9])
    calc = pd.DataFrame({
        "Net Revenue": [90, 185, 290, 385, 480, 590],
        "Profit After Mapped Costs": [30, 50, -5, 25, 200, 110],
    }, index=[300, 300, 301, 8, 9, 10])
    before = source.copy(deep=True)
    before_calc = calc.copy(deep=True)
    options = numeric_measure_options(source, calc)
    assert "Source: Sales" in options and "Calculated: Net Revenue" in options
    assert is_ratio_measure("Source: Profit Margin (%)")

    result, meta = category_chart_data(source, calc, "Product", "Calculated: Net Revenue", top_n=2)
    close(result["Value"].iloc[0], 775)
    return source, calc, before, before_calc


def test_category_correctness(source, calc):
    result, meta = category_chart_data(source, calc, "Product", "Calculated: Net Revenue", top_n=2)
    assert list(result["Category"]) == ["B", "C"]
    close(result.iloc[0]["Value"], 775)
    close(result.iloc[1]["Value"], 480)
    assert meta["all_groups"] == 4 and meta["truncated_groups"] == 2
    close(meta["complete_metric_total"], 2020)

    full, _ = category_chart_data(source, calc, "Product", "Calculated: Net Revenue", top_n=50)
    close(full["Value"].sum(), 2020)
    donut, donut_meta = category_chart_data(
        source, calc, "Product", "Calculated: Net Revenue", top_n=2, with_remainder=True
    )
    assert len(donut) == 3 and donut_meta["truncated_groups"] == 2
    close(donut["Value"].sum(), 2020)
    assert "Other categories" in donut.iloc[-1]["Category"]
    missing = full.loc[full["Category"] == "(Missing)"]
    close(missing["Value"].iloc[0], 385)

    lowest, _ = category_chart_data(source, calc, "Product", "Calculated: Net Revenue",
                                    top_n=2, direction="Lowest")
    assert lowest.iloc[0]["Category"] == "A"  # A=380, missing=385, C=480, B=775
    fails(lambda: category_chart_data(source, calc, "Product", "Source: Profit Margin (%)", aggregation="Sum"),
          "ratio sum")
    negative_finance = calc.copy()
    negative_finance.iloc[4, negative_finance.columns.get_loc("Profit After Mapped Costs")] = -200
    fails(lambda: category_chart_data(source, negative_finance, "Product",
                                      "Calculated: Profit After Mapped Costs", with_remainder=True), "negative donut")
    fails(lambda: category_chart_data(source, calc, "Product", "Calculated: Net Revenue", top_n=51),
          "top n limit")
    fails(lambda: category_chart_data(source.iloc[:-1], calc, "Product", "Calculated: Net Revenue"),
          "frame alignment")
    # Missing numeric values are explicitly excluded rather than treated as zero.
    sales, info = category_chart_data(source, calc, "Product", "Source: Sales", top_n=50)
    assert info["excluded_measure_rows"] == 1
    close(sales["Value"].sum(), 1500)


def test_time_correctness(source, calc):
    times, info = time_chart_data(source, calc, "Date", "Calculated: Net Revenue", "Monthly")
    assert times["Period"].tolist() == ["2026-01", "2026-02", "2026-04"]
    close(times.loc[times["Period"] == "2026-01", "Value"].iloc[0], 275)
    close(times.loc[times["Period"] == "2026-02", "Value"].iloc[0], 770)
    assert info["excluded_invalid_dates"] == 1 and info["included_rows"] == 5
    avg, _ = time_chart_data(source, calc, "Date", "Calculated: Net Revenue", "Monthly", "Average")
    close(avg.loc[avg["Period"] == "2026-01", "Value"].iloc[0], 137.5)
    fails(lambda: time_chart_data(source, calc, "Date", "Source: Profit Margin (%)", aggregation="Sum"),
          "sum ratio")
    fails(lambda: time_chart_data(source, calc, "Date", "Calculated: Net Revenue", cadence="Daily"),
          "unsupported cadence")


def test_scatter_histogram(source, calc):
    points, meta = scatter_chart_data(source, calc, "Source: Sales", "Calculated: Net Revenue", 2)
    assert len(points) == 2 and meta["valid_rows"] == 5 and meta["excluded_rows"] == 1
    points_again, _ = scatter_chart_data(source, calc, "Source: Sales", "Calculated: Net Revenue", 2)
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(points, points_again)
    fails(lambda: scatter_chart_data(source, calc, "Source: Sales", "Calculated: Net Revenue", 10_000),
          "too many points")
    hist, meta = histogram_chart_data(source, calc, "Source: Sales", bins=20)
    assert int(hist["Records"].sum()) == 5 and meta["excluded_measure_rows"] == 1
    fails(lambda: histogram_chart_data(source, calc, "Source: Sales", bins=200), "bins limit")


def test_bounded_benchmark():
    n = 100_000
    source = pd.DataFrame({
        "Category": np.where(np.arange(n) % 2 == 0, "Alpha", "Beta"),
        "Value": np.arange(n, dtype=float),
        "Extra": ["Supplementary data"] * n,
    })
    orig = source.head(5).copy(deep=True)
    result, summary = benchmark_grouping(source, "Category", "Value", MAX_BENCHMARK_ROWS)
    assert result["source_rows"] == n and result["sample_rows"] == MAX_BENCHMARK_ROWS
    assert result["group_count"] == 2 and not result["full_source_benchmarked"]
    assert int(summary["Records"].sum()) == MAX_BENCHMARK_ROWS
    assert result["elapsed_ms"] >= 0
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(source.head(5), orig)
    fails(lambda: benchmark_grouping(source, "Category", "Value", 100_000), "benchmark bound")
    fails(lambda: benchmark_grouping(source, "Category", "Category", 200), "numeric validation")

        Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "Data" / "Python_BI_Complete_Business_Dataset.xlsx",
        Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "Python_BI_Complete_Business_Dataset.xlsx",

========== FILE: test_scale_batch12.py ==========

"""Full-dataset Explorer regression suite; pure Pandas tests and wiring."""
import ast
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np
import pandas as pd

from full_dataset_engine import (
    MAX_EXPORT_ROWS, build_filter_mask, explore_dataset,
    literal_search_mask, safe_preview,
)
from benchmark_explorer import make_synthetic

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def raises(fn):
    try:
        fn()
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return
    raise AssertionError("Expected an invalid-input error")


def run():
    df = pd.DataFrame({
        "Product": ["Mouse", "Monitor", "Laptop", "Mouse", None, "LAPTOP"],
        "Sales": [10, 60, 150, 25, np.nan, 240],
        "Notes": ["s+?", "ordinary", "a.b", None, "", "Matched"],
    }, index=[6, 6, 2, 2, 2, 6])
    backup = df.copy(deep=True)
    selected = df.columns.tolist()
    mask = literal_search_mask(df, selected, "s+?", chunk_size=2)
    assert mask.tolist() == [True, False, False, False, False, False]
    assert literal_search_mask(df, selected, "a.b", chunk_size=1).sum() == 1
    assert literal_search_mask(df, selected, "LAPTOP", chunk_size=2).sum() == 2
    assert literal_search_mask(df, selected, "absent", chunk_size=2).sum() == 0
    assert literal_search_mask(df, selected, "", chunk_size=2).sum() == len(df)

    mask = build_filter_mask(df, selected, "Laptop", category_column="Product",
                             category_values=["Laptop"], numeric_column="Sales", minimum=100, maximum=200)
    assert np.flatnonzero(mask).tolist() == [2]
    none = build_filter_mask(df, selected, category_column="Product", category_values=[])
    assert not none.any()
    preview, export, meta = explore_dataset(df, selected, search_text="LAPTOP", sort_column="Sales", ascending=False,
                                            preview_limit=1, export_limit=2, chunk_size=2)
    assert meta.matched_rows == 2 and meta.shown_rows == 1 and meta.exported_rows == 2
    assert export.Sales.tolist() == [240.0, 150.0]
    assert meta.export_truncated is False and meta.search_scope == "Entire active dataset"
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(df, backup)
    display = safe_preview(df.iloc[:3])
    assert isinstance(display.iloc[0]["Product"], str)
    assert len(display) == 3

    raises(lambda: explore_dataset(df, ["Unknown"]))
    raises(lambda: explore_dataset(df, selected, preview_limit=1001))
    raises(lambda: explore_dataset(df, selected, export_limit=50001))
    raises(lambda: explore_dataset(df, selected, numeric_column="Sales", minimum=3, maximum=1))
    raises(lambda: literal_search_mask(df, selected, "L", chunk_size=0))
    raises(lambda: explore_dataset(df.rename(columns={"Product": "Sales"}), ["Sales"]))
    raises(lambda: explore_dataset(df, selected, sort_column="missing"))

    # Small sentinel-appears-at-end case proves preview is applied AFTER filter.
    synthetic = make_synthetic(1_001)
    before = synthetic.tail(1).copy(deep=True)
    sentinel = "SPECIAL-ROW-000001000"
    view, result, meta = explore_dataset(synthetic, ["Customer Name", "Sales"], search_text=sentinel,
                                        preview_limit=10)
    assert meta.matched_rows == 1 and view["Customer Name"].iloc[0] == sentinel
    pd.testing.assert_frame_equal(synthetic.tail(1), before)
    # Export cap must not hide that the result set is larger.
    big = pd.DataFrame({"A": np.arange(50_011)})
    view, exported, meta = explore_dataset(big, ["A"], preview_limit=10, export_limit=MAX_EXPORT_ROWS)
    assert meta.matched_rows == 50_011 and len(exported) == MAX_EXPORT_ROWS and meta.export_truncated
    assert len(view) == 10

    app = (ROOT / "app.py").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    ast.parse(app)
    assert "from explorer_workspace import render_explorer_workspace" in app
    assert "render_explorer_workspace(df)" in app
    assert 'elif workspace_mode == "Prepare & Validate":' in app
    assert 'elif page == "Investment Intelligence":' in app
    assert "render_executive_dashboard(" in app
    assert "save_active_dataset(uploaded_file)" in app
    assert (ROOT / "explorer_workspace.py").is_file()
    print("PASS: Entire-source search, bounded preview/export, filters, duplicates, source immutability, UI wiring")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

========== FILE: test_ui_integration_local.py ==========

"""Optional offline smoke-check for the new Streamlit controls (no browser)."""
import sys
from contextlib import nullcontext
from types import SimpleNamespace
import pandas as pd
import numpy as np

class FakeSt:
    session_state = {}
    clicked = True
    downloads = []
    outputs = []
    def expander(self,*a,**kw): return nullcontext()
    def columns(self,n,*a,**kw): return [self for _ in range(n)]
    def number_input(self,label,*a,**kwargs): return kwargs.get('value',0)
    def selectbox(self,label,values,*a,**kwargs): return values[kwargs.get('index',0)]
    def caption(self,*a,**kw): pass
    def warning(self,*a,**kw): pass
    def info(self,*a,**kw): pass
    def markdown(self,*a,**kw): pass
    def error(self,*a,**kw): raise AssertionError(str(a))
    def dataframe(self,*a,**kw): self.outputs.append('dataframe')
    def metric(self,*a,**kw): self.outputs.append('metric')
    def line_chart(self,*a,**kw): self.outputs.append('chart')
    def download_button(self,label,*a,**kw): self.downloads.append(label)
    def button(self,*a,**kw): return self.clicked

fake.clicked=False

========== FILE: ui_runtime_metrics.py ==========

"""Opt-in, local-only Streamlit server-side render timing. No dataset values/names.

Set PYTHON_BI_UI_METRICS=1 *before* starting Streamlit; generated JSONL is
local, outside the website. Does not measure browser painting or network time.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from time import perf_counter
import json
import os
from collections import defaultdict
from functools import wraps

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
TIMINGS = ROOT / 'acceptance_results' / 'ui_server_timings.jsonl'
VALID_PAGES = {'Home','Home assurance','Home readiness','Explorer','Commercial Intelligence',
               'Business Planning','Investment Intelligence','Executive Dashboard','Legacy compatible view'}


def enabled() -> bool:
    return os.environ.get('PYTHON_BI_UI_METRICS', '').strip() == '1'


def _rss_mib():
    try:
        import psutil
        return round(psutil.Process().memory_info().rss / (1024*1024), 2)
    except (ImportError, OSError):
        return None


def tracked_render(page: str, fn, *args, **kwargs):
    if not enabled():
        return fn(*args, **kwargs)
    if page not in VALID_PAGES:
        raise ValueError('Unknown predefined page timing label.')
    rows = cols = None
    for arg in args:
        if hasattr(arg, 'shape') and len(getattr(arg, 'shape', ())) == 2:
            rows, cols = int(arg.shape[0]), int(arg.shape[1])
            break
    start_mem = _rss_mib()
    start = perf_counter()
    status = 'PASS'
    error_kind = None
    try:
        return fn(*args, **kwargs)
    except Exception as exc:
        status = 'ERROR'
        error_kind = type(exc).__name__  # Never store exception messages or user data
        raise
    finally:
        info = {
            'timestamp_utc':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec='seconds'),
            'page':page,'records':rows,'columns':cols,
            'seconds':round(perf_counter()-start,4), 'rss_before_mib':start_mem,
            'rss_after_mib':_rss_mib(), 'status':status,'error_class':error_kind,
            'scope':'server-side selected render function; excludes browser/network'
        }
        try:
            TIMINGS.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
            if TIMINGS.exists() and TIMINGS.stat().st_size > 2_000_000:
                # Keep logging bounded, not an unbounded growing run history.
                TIMINGS.rename(TIMINGS.with_suffix('.previous.jsonl'))
            with TIMINGS.open('a', encoding='utf-8') as out:
                out.write(json.dumps(info, separators=(',', ':'))+'\n')
        except (OSError, TypeError):
            pass  # Never fail the actual Streamlit page because telemetry failed.


def summarize(path: Path | None = None) -> dict:
    location = Path(path) if path else TIMINGS
    groups = defaultdict(list)
    errors = 0
    if location.exists():
        for line in location.read_text(encoding='utf-8').splitlines():
            try:
                record = json.loads(line)
                if record.get('page') not in VALID_PAGES:
                    continue
                groups[record['page']].append(record)
                errors += record.get('status') != 'PASS'
            except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError):
                continue
    summary = []
    for page, records in sorted(groups.items()):
        elapsed = sorted(float(r['seconds']) for r in records)
        summary.append({'page':page,'observations':len(records),
                        'max_rows_observed':max((r.get('records') or 0) for r in records),
                        'median_server_seconds':elapsed[(len(elapsed)-1)//2],
                        'max_server_seconds':max(elapsed),
                        'last_rss_mib':records[-1].get('rss_after_mib'),
                        'errors':sum(r['status']!='PASS' for r in records)})
    return {'measurement':'Server-side selected render functions only, not complete browser latency',
            'samples':sum(len(s) for s in groups.values()),'errors':int(errors), 'pages':summary,
            'browser_acceptance':'MANUAL'}


def instrument(page: str, fn):
    """Wrap an existing view without changing its original UI call site."""
    @wraps(fn)
    def measured(*args, **kwargs):
        return tracked_render(page, fn, *args, **kwargs)
    return measured


========== FILE: verify_assurance_release.py ==========

"""Check all installed release files against the supplied SHA-256 manifest.

Run after replacing/copying files into the MyStreamlit App project root.
This is local integrity verification, not a software security certification.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib
import json
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent

def main()->int:
    manifest_path=ROOT/'FILE_MANIFEST.json'
    if not manifest_path.exists():
        print('FAIL: FILE_MANIFEST.json is missing from the project root.')
        return 1
    mapping=json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    if mapping.get('product')!='Python BI Studio Enterprise Assurance Release':
        print('FAIL: Unexpected release manifest.')
        return 1
    errors=[]
    files=mapping.get('files',{})
    if not files:
        print('FAIL: No release entries found in manifest.')
        return 1
    for filename, expected in files.items():
        if Path(filename).name!=filename:
            errors.append('Unsafe manifest filename')
            continue
        path=ROOT/filename
        if not path.is_file():
            errors.append(f'Missing: {filename}')
            continue
        digest=hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        if digest!=expected:
            errors.append(f'Mismatch: {filename}')
    if errors:
        for error in errors:print('FAIL:',error)
        return 1
    print(f'PASS: {len(files)} installed code/theme files match the release SHA-256 manifest.')
    return 0

if __name__=='__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())


========== FILE: verify_design_release.py ==========

"""Verify installed project files against the SHA-256 design-release manifest.

This checks copied-file integrity only; it does not audit code correctness or
certify that the app is safe for public deployment.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib
import json

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
MANIFEST=ROOT/'DESIGN_RELEASE_MANIFEST.json'


def check(root:Path=ROOT)->tuple[bool,list[str]]:
    manifest_path=root/'DESIGN_RELEASE_MANIFEST.json'
    if not manifest_path.is_file():return False,['DESIGN_RELEASE_MANIFEST.json missing']
    try:
        manifest=json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    except (ValueError,OSError):return False,['Manifest cannot be parsed']
    if manifest.get('product')!='Python BI Studio' or manifest.get('release')!='Enterprise Design & Release Readiness 1':
        return False,['Unexpected manifest identity']
    files=manifest.get('files',{})
    if not isinstance(files,dict) or not files:return False,['Manifest has no files']
    errors=[]
    for relative,hash_expected in files.items():
        path=Path(relative)
        if path.is_absolute() or '..' in path.parts or str(path).startswith('\\'):
            errors.append('Unsafe file entry')
            continue
        target=root/path
        if not target.is_file():errors.append('Missing: '+relative);continue
        actual=hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        if actual!=hash_expected:errors.append('Hash mismatch: '+relative)
    return not errors,errors


def main()->int:
    ok,errors=check()
    if ok:
        mapping=json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text())['files']
        print(f'PASS: {len(mapping)} design-release files match the supplied SHA-256 manifest.')
        print('This verifies file copies only; public deployment remains blocked.')
        return 0
    for error in errors:print('FAIL:',error)
    return 1

if __name__=='__main__':raise SystemExit(main())


========== FILE: verify_final_release.py ==========

"""Verify installed final-roadmap release files against its SHA-256 manifest.

Runs offline. Does not read datasets, credentials, or any other private files.
"""
from pathlib import Path
from hashlib import sha256
import json

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
MANIFEST=ROOT/'FINAL_ACCEPTANCE_MANIFEST.json'


def verify():
    data=json.loads(MANIFEST.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    if data.get('release')!='Python BI Studio Final Roadmap Acceptance':
        raise AssertionError('Wrong release manifest.')
    files=data['files']
    if len(files)<10:
        raise AssertionError('Incomplete manifest.')
    for rel,expected in files.items():
        if Path(rel).is_absolute() or '..' in Path(rel).parts:
            raise AssertionError('Unsafe manifest path: '+rel)
        target=ROOT/rel
        if not target.is_file():raise AssertionError('Missing file: '+rel)
        digest=sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        if digest!=expected:raise AssertionError('Modified or incomplete file: '+rel)
    return len(files)

if __name__=='__main__':
    count=verify()
    print(f'PASS: {count} final-acceptance installation files match SHA-256 manifest.')
    print('Saved datasets and private project data were NOT scanned.')


========== FILE: verify_local_release.py ==========

"""Verify this package's own file digests after installation, without old-manifest confusion."""
from __future__ import annotations
import hashlib,json
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent


def verify(root=ROOT):
    root=Path(root)
    manifest=root/'LOCAL_REVIEW_MANIFEST.json'
    if not manifest.is_file():return False,['LOCAL_REVIEW_MANIFEST.json is missing']
    try:info=json.loads(manifest.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    except (ValueError,OSError):return False,['Invalid local release manifest']
    if info.get('release')!='Local Roadmap Validation & Offline Demonstration â€” No Review Website Panel':
        return False,['Unexpected release identity']
    files=info.get('files',{})
    if not files or not isinstance(files,dict):return False,['Manifest contains no files']
    errors=[]
    for name,digest in files.items():
        p=Path(name)
        if p.is_absolute() or '..' in p.parts or '\\' in name:
            errors.append('Unsafe manifest entry');continue
        target=root/p
        if not target.is_file(): errors.append('Missing: '+name)
        elif hashlib.sha256(target.read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=digest:
            errors.append('Changed: '+name)
    return not errors,errors

if __name__=='__main__':
    good,errors=verify()
    if good:
        print('PASS: Corrected local-only roadmap release files match SHA-256 manifest.')
    else:
        for error in errors:print('FAIL:',error)
    raise SystemExit(0 if good else 1)


========== FILE: verify_report_pack.py ==========

"""Optional verifier for local executive-report ZIP downloads."""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from report_bundle import verify_report_archive


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Check aggregate report ZIP SHA-256 integrity.")
    parser.add_argument("report_zip", type=Path, help="Path to downloaded report pack ZIP")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        meta = verify_report_archive(args.report_zip.read_bytes())
    except (ValueError, OSError) as exc:
        parser.exit(1, f"FAIL: {exc}\n")
    print(f"PASS: {len(meta['files'])} report files match the manifest")
    print(f"Dataset: {meta['dataset_label']}; filtered rows: {meta['filtered_row_count']}")
    print("Note: Checksums verify integrity, not report authorship or accounting correctness.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
